# Life and Miracles of Madḫanina Egzi’ (Vie et miracles de)

**Author**: Anonymous  
**Series**: PO Tome 51  
**Columns**: pp. 398–574  

[p. 398] {#po.51.p398}
ገድል ፡
The early years and monastic vocation of Madhanina Egzi'

1 In the name of God the Father Adonay, whose design is great and whose greatness is powerful, equal with his Son and with his Spirit; and in the name of God the Son, substance which (comes) from a substance, whose name is one with the Father and with the Spirit before the sun (appeared), whose divinity clothed itself in flesh while being invisible; and in the name of God the Holy Spirit, drink of rest with the Father and with the Son, complete, who cannot be diminished. To the Trinity belong glory and thanksgiving on earth and in heaven for ever and ever, amen.
ወበሰምይ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አብርህ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ አልባቢነ ፡ ወጽልመተ ፡ ሕሊናነ ፡ ወፍታሕ ፡ ማእሰረ ፡ ልሳንነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንክሥቶ ፡ ለመዝገብ ፡ ኅቡእ ፡ ወለቲሩት ፡ ምሉእ ፡ ዘኀባእኮ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ በቀሩዔተ ፡ ለብዑላነ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ወብዕለ ፡ ለጽኑሳነ ፡ ጽድቅ ። አኮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘያዝ ህሮ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ወያቴክዞ ፡ ለነዳይ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኢረከቦ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃሥሥ ። ብዕለ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ይከውኖ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ይሰምዕ ፡ ወይገ ብር ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ብፁዓንሰ ፡ እለ ፡ ይሰምዑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ወየአምኑ ፡ ወይገብሩ ። መዝገብሰ ፡ ወቲሩት ፡ ዘንቤ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ወትዕግሥት ፡ ዘአቡነ ፡ ቴር ፡ ታናኅ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ክቡራን ፡ እለ ፡ አሰመዩ ፡ ስመ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወኀደጉ ፡ ዝክረ ፡ ለውሉዶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ አበ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ኃረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በዓጽፈ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አእሚሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሀለዎ ፡ ያአኀዞሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ወያዕቅቦሙ ፡ ፍናዊሁ ። ለአብርሃምኒ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ኢተጽሕፈ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ አላ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፪፻ ዓመት ፡ በኃምስ ፡ ትውልድ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ተጽሕፈ ፡ ዜና ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ለባሴ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ድርዐ ፡ ኀጺን ፡ ዘኢይበርሮ ፡ ሓፀ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ። ወተወል ተወ ፡ በሓገፋ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ።
2 Give light, O Lord, to the eyes of our hearts and to the darkness of our thought, and untie the bonds of our tongue so that we may unveil a hidden treasure and a complete virtue that I have hidden until this very moment, so that they may be a profit for those who are rich in spirit and a wealth for those who are devoid of justice—not like a wealth of this world which makes a man boast when he has found it and makes the poor man grieve when he has not found it while he was seeking it. Wealth in spiritual justice comes to him who listens and acts, according to what Our Lord said: Blessed rather are those who hear the word of God, believe (in it), and put it into practice (Lk 11:28; 8:21). 3 As for the treasure and the virtue of which we have spoken, (it is) the struggle of mortification and abstinence of our virtuous father... Madhanina Egzi', the father of the glorious fathers who have acquired a good name and have left a memory to their (spiritual) children like Abraham the father of many: God chose him with the mantle of the saints, knowing that it would be his duty to command his children and make them observe his ways (Gn 18:18-19). As for Abraham, his story was not written immediately either, but four hundred years later, in the fifth generation. Likewise was written the story of our father Madhanina Egzi', clothed with the Holy Spirit as with an iron breastplate that the devil's arrow does not pierce, and he is armed with the shield of the holy Gospel.
እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሀቤ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ጢአተ ፡ እዘኒክሙ ፡ ወይሚጥ ፡ ገጸክሙ ፡ ወያብርህ ፡ አልባቢክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስምዑ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መክብብ ፡ ዘይሰምዕ ፡ ዝክሮ ፡ ለጎር ፡
4 May God, lamp of wisdom, God, pillar of light, God, dispenser of grace, open the closure of your ears, turn your faces, and illuminate your hearts so that you may listen to the story of this holy father, according to what Ecclesiastes says: Whoever listens to the memory of a virtuous man becomes wise. It is a good

precious thing, the wise man, and the story of his memory fattens the bones (Si 26:13). The ear that listens accumulates knowledge, and the eye that looks at the good rejoices the heart (Pr 15:30),
ወከማሁ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ዮም ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋባእክሙ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሥኡ ፡ በረከቶ ፡ እለ ፡ ተጸዋዕክሙ፡ በፍቅሩ ። ወናሁ ፡ ንከሥት ፡ ለክሙ ፡ አስከ ሬነ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ኅቱመ ፡ እምብዕለ ፡ ጽድቁ ፡ ወዕንቄ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ እሙዳየ ፡ ዓጽሙ ፡ ወመድፍነ ፡ ክቡተ ፡ እምገራህተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወመዝገበ ፡ ኅቡአ ፡ እም ፍሬ ፡ ጻማሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታጥርይዋ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ታስተነፍስ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወት ትሓወስ ፡ ጽላሎት ። ይእቲ ፡ ታድኅነክሙ ፡ እሞተ ፡ ኃጢአት ። አኮ ፡ በወ ርቅ ፡ ዘተሣየጥክምዋ ፡ ወኢበኩሉ ፡ ንዋይ ፡ አላ ፡ በሰሚዐ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ወበተ ማኅፅኖቱ ፡ ይሁበክሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘዓሠረክሙ ፡ ለመርዓ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ውስተ ዛቲ ፡ ተድባብ ። የዓሥርክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መርዓ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፡ በኃዳስ ፡ ከብካብ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበበረከቱ ፡ ወበሀብተ ፡ ረድኤቱ ። ይትሓበይክሙ ፡ ለኩል ክሙ ፡ ሰማዕያን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዘዐ፤ረ፲/፣ሠርሪ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
5 (Do) the same today, you who have gathered here to receive his (= Madhanina Egzi's) blessing, who have been called by his love. Behold, we are going to unveil for you the sealed golden chest (made) of the wealth of his justice, the precious stone (made) of the vessel of his bones, the hidden treasure (Si 20:30) (made) of the fields of his heart, and the hidden deposit (made) of the flower of his maceration, so that you may acquire them until the day breathes and the shadows flee (Ct 4:6); they will save you from the death of sin. It is not with gold that you will buy them, nor with any goods; but as you listen to (the story of) his life and entrust yourselves to him, this father will give (them) to you, he who has invited you to the wedding of his feast under this roof. || will invite you to the wedding that (is) in the heavens, in a new marriage, by his prayer, by his blessing, and by the gift of his help. He will be a guarantor for all of you, listeners, with... for ever and ever, amen.
እባርከከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለነፍሰ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ በኩሉ ፡ በሓውርት ። አልፕስስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዘንየ ፡ ወተና ገር ፡ በላዕሌየ ፡ ወአንብር ፡ ቃለከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንግር ፡ ዜና ፡ ገድል ፡ ወኃይለ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ዘከሠትከ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በብዝኃ ፡ ፍቅርከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ፈቃደከ ፡ ወፈቃዶ ፡ ለአቡከ ፡ በሥምረተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘአኅደ ርከ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ይኩን ፡ ከመ ፡ ጠለ ፡ አርሞንኤም ፡ ዘያስተፌሥሕ ፡ አድ ባረ ፡ ወፈውሰ ፡ ለሕሙማን ፡ ወብዕለ ፡ ለነዳያን ።
6 I bless you, O Lord my God, God of heaven and earth, rest of the soul of the righteous in all lands. Whisper in my ears, speak in me, and place your word in my mouth so that I may narrate the story of the life (of Madhanina Egzi') and the great power that you revealed for him in the greatness of your love, when he did your will and the will of your Father by the good pleasure of the Holy Spirit that you made dwell upon him so that he would be like the dew of Hermon that rejoices the mountains (Ps 133[132]:3), healing for the suffering and wealth for the poor.
ንስቲተ ፡ ዘተናገርኩ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ገበርከ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ ወመድም ማተ ፡ በተጋድሎቱ ፡ ወበትጋሁ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ በስእለታ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ እምከ ፡ ድንግል ፡ በክልኤ ። ወበአስተብቀ-'ፆቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ይኩን ፡ ኀይልከ ፡ ዘይመርሓኒ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ እምተርፈ ፡ ገይሉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድ ኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ መካሬ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዝመጋቤ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡
7 (This is) little, what I will have told of the miracles and wonders that you did for him in his (spiritual) struggle and in his vigil day and night through the intercession of Mary your mother, twice virgin. May your power, through the supplication of all the saints, show me a little of the wealth of the life of our father Madhanina Egzi', counselor of light, that is (to say) minister of the faith, for ever and ever, amen.
አሜን ። ንወጥን ፡ በረድኤተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዜና ፡ ገድል ፡ ዘአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተወልድ ፡ በሀገር ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ቢሓት ፡ ወስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ስንበት ፡ ተስፋነ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ካህን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ ኅሪት ፡ ማርያም ፡ ክቡራነ ፡ ዘመድ ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወክልኤሆሙ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወንጹሓን ፡ ወየሓውሩ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ትእዛዙ ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ ኩነኔሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአልቦሙ ፡ ውሉድ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ እንዘ፡ ይስእሉ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚ አቡሔር ፡ ወኅበ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እሙ ፡ መዝገበ ፡ ረድኤት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እግዚአቡሔር ፡ ገዓሮሙ ፡ ወብካዮሙ ፡ ወምጽዋተ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ዘወሀቡ ፡ ለነዳ ያን ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስእለቶሙ ፡ ወተወከፈ ፡ መሥዋዕቶው ፡ ወወ ሀቦሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዘይምዕዝ ፡ እምአፈወ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወእምጽጌያተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘወርኀ ፡ ኔዎን ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ መርሐ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ።
8 We begin with the help of Our Lord Jesus Christ the story of the life of our father Madhanina Egzi': he was born in the city whose name (is) Bihat. The name of his father (is) Sanbat tasfana, he was a great priest; the name of his mother (is) Herit Maryam; they were notables regarding their family. Both were righteous, pure, and followed all the commandment and judgment of God; they had no children and they begged God and Mary his mother, the treasure of help. When God saw their groans, their tears, and the abundant alms they gave to the poor, God (therefore) heard their supplication, accepted their sacrifice, and gave them this beautiful flower, which was sweeter than the perfume of the garden and than the flowers of the field of the month of renewal (Si 50:8), which would be a guide for many in the kingdom of heaven.


[p. 402] {#po.51.p402}
ወሶበ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አዝማዲሁ ፡ ወሓፀንዎ ፡ በፈሪሀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአልሀቅዎ ፡ በጥበብ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይተሉ ፡ አባግዐ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይል በስ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይትፈሣሕ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በሥነ ፡ ሞገሰ ፡ ወልዱ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ለነዳይ ፡ ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ሆኮ ፡ መለኮተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ፡ በከወ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዚአነ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ ዘለሕይወት ፡ በአምልኮቱ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተውህበት ፡ ለነ ። ወነገርዎ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ለነዳይ ። ወይቤ ፡ ፅፁብ ፡ ግብረዝ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ዘይሁብ ፡ ለምስኪን ፡ ኢይፄነስ ። ወተፈሥሐ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይዌስኮ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ይሁብ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ አኮ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ባሕቲቶ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ አላ ፡ መባል ፅተ ፡ ጥዑማተ ፡ ዘትገብር ሎቱ ፡ እሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ታፈቅሮ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ፳ውእቱ ፡ ላቲ ። ቦዕለት ፡ አመ ፡ ክፍሎ ፡ ዘይሁብ ፡ ወቦ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢያተርፍ ፡ ንስ ቲተ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ይሁብ ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ ወየሓውር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወይትሜጦ ፡ እምሥጢረ ፡ ቅድሳት ።
9 When he was born, all his relatives rejoiced; they nourished him in the fear of God and raised him in wisdom. When he had grown a little, he was to follow his father's sheep; his father gave him a beautiful garment so that he might clothe himself (in it) and so that (he himself) might rejoice, thanks to him, in the beauty of his son's grace. The latter gave it to a poor man, and from that moment, the divinity of God that (was) upon him stirred him, according to what Peter says: As is all to us his divinity which (leads) to life through piety (toward) him who has been given to us (2 P 1:3). It was said to his father that he had given (the garment) to a poor man. (His father) said: "Astonishing is the action of this child who gives to a needy person: he is not deprived!" His father rejoiced and, because of that, he (gave) him much more; he himself (= Madhanina Egzi') gave according to his habit not only what he made, but (also) the tasty dishes that his mother made for him as she loved him: he was her only (child). There was a day when (it was) (a part of) his share that he gave, sometimes he did not even reserve a little and gave everything; as for him, he fasted, went to church, and received the holy mystery.
ወጸሎተኒ ፡ ይገብር ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ በነቢይ ፡ ክብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የኃብእ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወይከሥት ፡ ዕሙቃተ ። ወኮነ ፡ ግብረዝ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ጠቢባን ፡ ወልቡባን ፡ እምንአሱ ፡ ኃረያ ፡ ለሠናይት ። ኅፃናትሰ ፡ ይበክዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይወስክዎሙ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወልብስሂ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ጻህቅ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ መልብስ ። ወለ ዝንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ አመሮ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘተብህለ ፡ እስመ ፡ ምጽዋት ፡ ታድኅን ፡ እሞት ፡ ወታነጽሕ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ኃጢአት ። ወአለሰ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ወምጽዋተ ፡ ይመልኡ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሕይወተ ። ወኢታበውኅ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ትባእ፤! ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ እስመ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይገብራ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ልዑል ። ወአኀዘ ፡ ይግበራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጦቢት ፡ አበ ፡ ጦብያ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘይ ብል ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ኣበድር ፡
10 Prayer, he (did) it in secret, according to what is said by the prophet: The glory of God hides the word (Pr 25:2); he unveiled the depths (Jb 12:22). The way of acting of this child was like the way of acting of the wise and the prudent; from his childhood, he had chosen the good. Children cry so that one (gives) them more bread, and likewise regarding clothing; indeed they have no other desire than food and clothing. But as for this father of ours Madhanina Egzi', from his childhood the Holy Spirit indicated to him what is said: Almsgiving saves from death and purifies from all sin; those who do justice and almsgiving will be filled with life (Tb 12:9). (Almsgiving) does not let you, on the day of misfortune, enter into darkness, for it is a beautiful grace for whoever practices it before the Most High (Tb 4:10-11). (Madhanina Egzi') began to practice it like Tobit, the father of Tobias, for he had heard what says: I desire compassion and not sacrifice, I prefer almsgiving to sacrifice (Os 6:6; Mt 9:13, 12:7).
እምነ ፡ መሥዋዕት ። ወበ ፳ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ርእየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ድዉየ ፡ ዘንህለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በቀስል ፡ ወተራኅርሐ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አድገ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ይጸዓን ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ቲኋሩተ ፡ ወልዱ ።
11 One (day) among the days, our father Madhanina Egzi' saw a sick man whose body was devastated by sores. His heart was touched with pity, he took his father's donkey and gave (it) to him so that he might ride upon it. His father rejoiced when he saw the goodness of his son.
ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ለመዊት ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድ ኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ባረኮ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይባርኩ ፡ አበው ፡ ለውሉዶሙ ። ወጥበበስ ፡ ኢመዓዶ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አሶተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወፍድፋዴ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ። ወእምሔዉሰ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ርእየ ፡ በእንግድዓሁ ፡ ወበድኅሬሁ ፡ አመ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡
12 When his father was near death, he called his son, our father Madhanina Egzi'; he blessed him much, as fathers bless their sons. He did not exhort him to wisdom when he saw the remedy of wisdom and the abundance of justice that (were) upon him. As for the grandfather of this saint, he had seen the sign of salvation on his chest and on his back. When he was born, he had prophesied saying: "He will be an elect on earth," and he had said to his son:


[p. 404] {#po.51.p404}
ይከውን ፡ ኅሩየ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ መሀሮ ፡ መሕፅኖ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ሕፅነተ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ እክበሮ ፡ ወአብሖ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጥሪቱ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ዘንተኒ ፡ ስብዓ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ወሀብ ፡ ለዘፈቀድከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ነሀብ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቂርቆስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ግበር ፡ በዘ ፡ ይኤድመከ ፡ መይቤሎ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ በትርከ ፡ ወመስቀል ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ይበቀሩዓኒ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ።
"Instruct him and give him a good education." Because of that, he had honored him and had allowed him to do what he wanted with all his goods; he said to him: "Take these seventy pounds and give (them) to whom you want." His son said to him: "Let us give (them) to the church of Cyriacus." His father said to him: "Do according to what pleases you." His son said to him: "Your staff and the cross that is in your hand will be useful to me." (His father) gave (them) to him then died.
ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘረወ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ወወሀበ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ። አኀዘ ፡ ያጻሙ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በአቅማሐ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ፍርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተገሓስ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ይከውነከ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ለሥጋከ ፡ ወሥራየ ፡ ለአዕፅምቲከ ፡ አክብሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እምዘ ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ ጥሪ ትከ ፡ ወአብእ ፡ ሌቱ ፡ እምፍሬ ፡ ጽድቅከ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚአነ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይእኅዝ ፡ ዕርፈ ፡ ወየሓርስ ፡ ደኃሪተ ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መንግ ሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘኢወፅአ ፡ ወዘኢተዓደወ ፡ እምኩሉ ። ንዋዩ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ተጸምዶትየ ።
13 When our father Madhanina Egzi' saw that his father was dead, he distributed all his goods and gave (them) to the poor and the needy. He began to mortify his body with the fruits of the Scriptures, having heard what is said: Fear God and turn away from all evil; then there will be for you health for your body and remedy for your bones. Honor God with what (is) justly your goods and bring him fruits of your justice (Pr 3:7-9), and also what Our Lord said: (For) him who has grasped the plow and plows backward, the kingdom of heaven is not suitable (Lk 9:62). Whoever does not go out and does not detach himself from all his goods cannot attach himself to me (Lk 14:33).
ወወጠነ ፡ ያጻሙ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ንጽሕተ ፡ በድንግልና ፡ ዘሃይማኖት ፡ ወፈኃረ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ለ ፳ምት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወሰቀለ ፡ ሕይወቶ ፡ በሕይወተ ፡ አምላክ ። ወነበረ ፡ በጾም ፡ መበጸሎት ፡ ወከመዝ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ አበ ፡ ረ ፡ ዘታፈቆሮ ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ ወዘያዓርፍ ፡ ጸጋከ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምርሓኒ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ወያልብሰኒ ፡ ዓጽፈ ፡ ቀዱሳኒከ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይደሉ ። ወሊተኒ ፡ ሥመር ፡ ብየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጹር ፡ ግብረ ፡ ቅድሳቲከ ። አጥባዕኩ ፡ ወኢናፈቁ ፡ ለዓቂበ ፡ ትእዛዝከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ አሥረቀ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እመንገለ ፡ ሰሜን ፡ ለመንገለ ፡ ደቡብ ፡ አበ ፡ ሔረ ፡ ዘያሓቱ ፡ ሱራሔ ፡ ሥኑ ፡ ምስብዒተ ፡ እምብርሃነ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ከዋክብት ፡ ወአበ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ አትዮጵያ ፡ ተክለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ አሥራተ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ነፍ ሳተ ፡ ኃጥኣን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንግደቱ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይንሥአነ ፡ ውሰተ ፡ መከፈልተ ፡
14 || He began to mortify his pure body with the virginity of the faith and betrothed himself to a virgin spouse, Christ, according to what Paul says (2 Co 11:2); he crucified his life by the life of God (Ga 2:19-20). He remained in fasting and in prayer, thus was his prayer: "Give me, my Lord and my God, the excellent father whom you love in justice and upon whom your grace rests, so that he may show me your way and clothe me with the mantle of your saints as is fitting. As for me, accept me so that I may endure the work of your holiness. I am ready and do not hesitate to keep your commandment (Ps 119[118]:60). When God heard his prayer, he made a light rise for him from the south toward the north, the excellent father whose splendor of beauty shone seven times more than the light of the sun, the chief of the stars and the father of the angels on earth, that is to say the pillar of Ethiopia Takla Haymanot receiving as a tithe, from his God, all the souls of sinners because of his pilgrimages. Likewise, may he receive us, all of us, children of his house, into the part of his inheritance, for ever and ever, amen.
ርስቱ ፡ ለኩልነ ፡ ልደ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ከመ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ሙላዱ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በሥምረተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተአ ምኖ ፡ ወሰዓሞ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ተክለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ አእመሮ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ያአምር ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይትኀብኦ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወኃይለ ፡ ዕበይ ፡ ወሥነ ፡ ሞገስ ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ እንተ ፡ ተአውዶ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ። ተነበየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ይከውን ፡ አበ ፡ አበው ፡ ክቡራን ፡ ወይበጽሕ ፡ ዝክሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወያድኅን ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ በጸሎቱ ።
15 Our father Madhänina Egzi', having heard that he had arrived nearby, in his native land, went to him with the assent of God: he greeted him and embraced him. He himself, our father Takla Häymänot, had known of his (coming) before he had arrived: he knew everything through the Holy Spirit and nothing was hidden from him of what was happening on earth. When he saw the light of grace, the power of greatness, and the beauty of the heavenly grace that surrounded him (= Madhänina Egzi'), he marveled and rejoiced greatly. He prophesied and said that he would be the father of illustrious fathers, that his fame would reach all the ends of the world, and that he would save many souls through his prayer.
ወዝንቱኒ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ አስ ኬመ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ ወልድየ ። ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ቅድ ስና ፡ ወአልበሶ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ መላእክት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኩን ፡ ቡሩከ ፡ በአፈ ፡
16 This holy father, Madhänina Egzi', said to him: "Give me the holy monastic habit." (Takla Häymänot) said to him: "Yes, my son." And he prayed over the garments of holiness and clothed him in the likeness of the angels (= monks).


[p. 406] {#po.51.p406}
ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወበአፉሃ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ ሙዳየ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወበአፉሆሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰማያዊያን ፡ ወምድራዊያን ፡ እለ ፡ ተሓርዩ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ ብዘኅ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ምሥጠረ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ ጸኑዕ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወከመ ፡ ጥቅመ ፡ አድማስ ፡ አውዳ ። ወነገሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወበቃሉ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ይፈሪ ፡ ኪሩባዊያነ ፡ ወሱራፌላዊያነ ፡ ፀሓያውያነ ፡ ጽዱሳላነ ፡ ወከዋክብተ ፡ ብሩሃነነ ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ጥፕልቄ ።
He said to him: "Be blessed by the mouth of the holy Trinity, by the mouth of Mary, receptacle of light, and by the mouth of all the heavenly and earthly beings who have been chosen." He gave him an abundant blessing, revealed to him the mystery that was upon him, and said to him: "Your prayer (is) like a solid column in the midst of the city and like a rampart of diamond around it." He narrated to him everything that would come to pass by his hand and by his word and how he would beget cherubic and seraphic beings, bright suns and countless luminous stars.

17 Having heard all this, our father Madhänina Egzi' bowed down on the top of his staff (Heb 11:21) and said in a humble voice: "(This) does not belong to me, a sinner, but, so that I do not make your word a lie, may what you have said to me come to pass according to the good pleasure of your God, O father," having heard what is said: "Listen, my son, to the commandment of your father: A wise son listens to his father (Pr 4:3), and again what is said: The wise heart welcomes the commandment; the mouth of the upright teaches wisdom; he who walks in integrity walks in safety; to whom is humble of spirit God provides glory (Pr 10:8, 31, 9; 29:23). Joy increased for him (= Madhänina Egzi') when he heard the humility of his word and the commandment with which he exhorted him. He himself was for him the eldest of all his (spiritual) sons, as Reuben had been the eldest for Jacob. (Takla Häymänot) narrated again to our father everything that would happen to him and strengthened him greatly in the faith of Christ. 18 After that, our father Takla Häymänot returned to his country in peace; he gave him (= Madhänina Egzi') to Gabra Wähed, an educated, pure (and) God-fearing man, so that he might teach him the Scriptures and the monastic yoke that is fitting. (The latter) taught him the Psalms of David, the New and the Old (Testament). All this, the Holy Spirit had taught him (= to Madhänina Egzi') and (the latter) had inscribed the interpretations in his heart in secret while he was a child, but he was learning to fulfill the commandment of his (spiritual) father. Having finished, he was ordained deacon then priest. After that, a thought rose in his heart which said: "My lamp will not light for me under the bushel that is in my body nor under the bed that is in the temporary world, but I am going to light it on the lampstand of my faith so that my light may shine before men so that they may see the beauty of my beautiful conduct and praise and glorify my Father who (is) in heaven (Mt 5:15-16)."

tears before God (Jb 16:20). Like the sea are my tears for me, my eyes are blinded by tears (Jb 17:7); who will give me a cloud of water for my eyes so that I may wash away my fault?" With this he gave thanks unceasingly to his God while rebuking his soul. It was not for himself alone that he prayed, but for all it was profitable, for the Holy Spirit who dwelt upon him compelled him to be a refuge for all through the power of his prayer. When his master saw that he was ready for the (spiritual) combat without turning back, he too prophesied three times for him and said to him: "You, my son, will be great and you will save the soul of many through your prayer; power and help will not depart from you. As for me, I am going to our father Takla Häymänot." He went and died in peace.

20 As for this father of ours, he remembered to give alms as before and, the sacks of wheat that he had, he distributed (them) to the poor, having learned what is said: God loves the cheerful and generous man; he who has pity on the poor is rich, for he has given his bread to the needy (Pr 22:9). He did everything like this while meditating on Scripture, and he saved a little to give to the needy. When he returned, he found the sacks untouched, as before. This is written: The blessing of God (is) in the houses of the righteous (Pr 3:33). He remained there ten years in the (spiritual) combat that he had begun.

21 When God - great (and) powerful (is) his name - saw him pursuing the righteous (who) have been raised by glory, he sent him his luminous angel Gabriel with great help. While this father of ours was praying as (was his) custom, (Gabriel) appeared to him, said to him: "Greetings to you, chosen of God!" and delivered to him the message he had brought. He, our father, believed him, for he saw the sign of the cross that (was) in his hand and the formidable power of God upon him. (Gabriel) said to him: "Come, let me lead you to where God has ordered me, so that I may show you a great mountain that will be for you, as a portion and inheritance, and also for your (spiritual) children after you." And he made him go up while guiding him, brought him to Dabra Bankwal and explained to him the hidden meaning of its name: "Know that Bankwal means 'Mountain of clemency', according to the word of the Creator. Indeed there, I will be clement to your chosen children, to the humble kings, to the governors and to all those who will have faith in your prayer (and) who will love your name - said God. Remain here until he indicates to you his will which you will execute." Having said that, the angel gave him the greeting and went up to heaven.
ወዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ። ወነበረ ፡ ህየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይፈርህ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ለቢዎ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሓዋርያ፡ ወዘሰ ፡ ይፈርህ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ በተፉቅሮ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፍርሃትሰ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ አጽኒዖ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ሲራክ ፡ ለፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይጌርሞ ፡ ወኢይደነግፅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተስፋሁ ። ብፅዕት ፡ ነፍስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ለፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢይረክቦ ፡ እኩይ ፡
22 (Madhänina Egzi') remained there fearing nothing, having meditated on the word of the apostle: He who fears is not perfected in love, for fear has punishment (1 Jn 4:18), and having also retained the Wisdom of Sirach: (As for) him who fears God, there is nothing that frightens him, and he does not tremble, for he (= God) is his hope. Happy the soul that fears God. He who fears God, evil does not touch him, except for one to be tested, and he will be saved from it (Si 34:14-15; 33:1). He said this while strengthening his body and

reciting the psalm of David: God shines for me and saves me: what will inspire fear in me? God (is) the protector of my life; what will make me tremble (Ps 27[26]:1), until the end.

23 As for this mountain, it had no tree that bore fruit to serve as food nor water to drink, for it was desolate. (Madhänina Egzi') remained there feeding on the meager grass of the mountain and the sycamores (that grew) below the side of it. If (it were) figs that he found, (it was) not for satiety, but to deceive (his) body, while he remembered the word of David: Cast your thought upon God and he will feed you (Ps 55[54]:23), and the word of Ecclesiastes his son: It is not the fruit of the tree that is sown - simply it makes man live -, but your word (which keeps) those who believe in you (Ws 16:26). He also kept the word of Peter: All your thought, cast it upon God, he will care for you. Be wise therefore and make your heart wise, for your enemy, the demon, arises like a lion and seeks whom to devour. Hold firmly the resistance in the faith (1 Pt 5:7-9).
ወአጋንንትኒ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ያጽሕብዎ ፡ በሚኖሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትሜሰሉ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ አርአያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍርህዎ ፡ ወያኅድጋዎ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ደብረ" ። ኢፈርሀ ፡ ወኢደንገፀ ፡ እምአስፍጦቶሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ አጽንዖ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘአ ቀመ ፡ በትእዛዙ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አምላኪየ ዘት ጤይቆ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ ወታአምር ፡ ልበ ፡ ወኩልያተ ፡ ፍታሕ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ፍት ሕየ ፡ ዘእምኀቤከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለከ ፡ ከሠትኩ ፡ ሕሊናየ ። አኮ ፡ በፈቃድየ ፡ ዘመጻእኩ ፡ ዝየ ፡ አላ ፡ በፈቃደ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ እዕረገኒ ፡ መልአክከ ፡ ር ፡ ወአብጽሓኒ ፡ እንበር ፡ ዝየ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ስምዓኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ርድአኒ ፡ በኃይልከ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወስድድ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ መስቴማ ፡ እለ ፡ ያመነድቡኒ ።
24 Demons pressed him greatly with their deception by transforming themselves under many aspects to frighten him and make him abandon this mountain. He was not afraid nor did he tremble before their deceit, for the Holy Spirit who had made him rise by his order strengthened him. Then our father prayed and said: "O Lord, my God, (you) who observe the righteous and know the heart and the kidneys, render for me my judgment which (comes) from you, for to you I have revealed my thought (Jr 20:12). It is not by my will that I came here, but by your will: your good angel made me go up and brought me so that I might remain here. Now, listen to me for your name, help me with your great power and chase away all the armies of the Aggressor, who torment me."
ወፈነወ ። ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይናዝዞ ፡ ወያድኅኖ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ምንዳቤሁ ፡ ወአግረረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘይትቃረኖ ። ወተፈሥሐ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በሀብተ ፡ ረድኤቱ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ በግናይ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ አንተ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወቡሩክ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ በረከት ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ወቅድስት ፡ ይባርኩከ ፡ ጻድቃኒከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘገበርከ ፡ ይባርኩከ ፡ መላእክቲከ ፡ ወኅሩያኒከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አስተፈሣሕከኒ ፡ በሣህልከ ፡ ልዑል ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነበረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እነዘ ፡ ይትቃተሎ ፡ ለሕሊና ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወሕሊና ፡ ሥጋሰ ፡ ፀሩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢይገኒ ፡ ለሕገ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ፀሩ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ። ወዘንተኒ ፡ ሞአ ፡ በሕሊና ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ተስዒኖ ፡ በአሣእነ ፡ ኀን ፡ ወብርት ፡ አስተዳለወ ፡ ኣሕፃሁ ፡ ወሠቀ ፡ ቀስቶ ፡ ተሠር ጊዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያዜር ፡ ዘስምዕ" ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዘትጸጊ ፡ ወዘታሓመለምል ፡
25 God sent him his angel so that (the latter) might console him and deliver him from all his torment, and (the angel) subjected to him everything that attacked him. Our father rejoiced in the gift of his help and said with humble gratitude: "Blessed (are) you, my Lord, and blessed with every pure and holy blessing! Your righteous bless you in everything that you have done; your angels and your chosen ones bless you, for you have rejoiced me by your most high clemency." After that, our father Madhänina Egzi' remained to kill the thought of the body: the thought of the body is the enemy of God, for it does not submit to the law of God. Our Lord said: The enemy of man (is) the man of his house (Mt 10:36). This (thought), he conquered it by the thought of the spirit; (this) being impossible for him with sandals of iron and bronze (Dt 33:25), he prepared his arrows (and) strung his bow, having adorned himself as with a crown of testimony (2 Kgs 11:12), with the garment of the faith that flowers and that turns green once with perfumes of


[p. 412] {#po.51.p412}
ዞ.412 ምዕረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አፈዋተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ኅብረ ፡ ቀይሐ ፡ ፀዓድዒደ ፡ ዘግቡር ፡ በመዓዛ ፡ ናርዶስ ። ወትፈሪ ፡ ጽፉቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኔቄጦር ፡ ወሣረራ ፡ ዘኢያዓ ብሮ ፡ ሓሩረ ፡ መርቄ ፡ ወነፋስ ።
garden, of light red color, which (is) adorned with the fragrance of nard. It bears fruits continuously like nectar and styrax, which the heat of the warmth nor the wind do not dry out.

### Miracle of the donkey and the lion
ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይትሜካሕ ፡ ወይትወረዘው ፡ መዊኦ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ መስ ቴማ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ፳ እምእለ ፡ ያፈቅርዎ ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ አድገ ፡ በሥምረተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርድዖ ። ወአቡነሰ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ተመጠዎ ፡ አእሚሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሀለዎ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ አድግ ፡ ወይፅዕኖ ፡ ኃምስተ ፡ ዝቃተ ፡ ወይፌጽም ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምፅእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ማየ ። እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይነድኦ ፡ ወይተልዎ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ወይበጽሕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወይቀውም ፡ ማእከሎ ። እስከ ፡ ይመልኣ ፡ ዝቃት ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ፡ ይትከ ነስ ፡ ወያቴሕት ፡ ዘባኖ ። ወእምከመ ፡ መልኣ ፡ አእሚሮ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ ወያ አቱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ። ወያአምርዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ አድገ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ በካልእ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ውእቶሙኒ ፡ ይቀድሑ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወይፌንውዎ ። በምስያጥሂ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይነከዮ ፡
26 While he was glorified and rejuvenated, having conquered the power of the Aggressor, one of those who loved him came to him, having taken a donkey, with the agreement of God and gave (it) to him so that it might help him. Our father Madhänina Egzi' accepted it, knowing that he was going to show many miracles with this donkey; he would load it with five waterskins and close (them) so that it might bring him water; without anyone leading it nor following it, (the donkey) would go down, arrive in the water and stand in the middle of it; until the waterskins that (were) on it were filled, it would stand (as) at church and bow its back. After they had been filled, knowing (it), it would come out and bring the water to our father. All those who saw it knew that it was the donkey of our father. If they met it another day, they would draw for it themselves and send it. At the market, likewise, no beast nor any man did it any harm.
ምንተኒ ፡ እምአራዊት ፡ ወሰብእ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተፈጺሞ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አድግ ፡ ቀተሎ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወይክሥት ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ። ሶበሰ ፡ ኢያእመረ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ይኩን ፡ እም ፡ ኢያቅተሎ ፡ በአፈ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ለአድግ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዘይክል ። አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይመውኦ ፡ በስልጣነ ፡ ዕበይ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ፡ ወፈ ነዎ ፡ ጽዒኖ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ አእሚሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ የዓቢ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ትእም ርቱ ፡ ለይኃሪ ፡ እምትእምርተ ፡ መልእክቱ ፡ ለአድግ ። ወጐንዲዮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ሖረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኀዝን ፡ ወይቴክዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘኢያእመረ ፡ ኃቢኦ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ዘአብሖ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይኩኖ ፡ ትምክሕተ ፡ ለቢዎ ፡ ትሕትና ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ከመዘ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አይቴ ፡ ቀበርክምዎ ፡ ለአልዓዛር ፡ ካልአኒ' ፡ እምአይቴ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ዘናበልፆሙ ፡ ወሣ ልሰኒ ፡ እምአይቴ ፡ አንተ ፡ ባሐ ፡ ዓርክየ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለይሁዳ ፡ ሓቢኦ ፡ ስልጣነ ፡ መለኮት ፡ በትሕትና ፡ አምላካዊት ። ወዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ፈጻሜ ፡ ትእዛዙ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በድኑ ፡ ለአድግ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ መኑ ፡ ቀተሎ ፡ ለአድግየ ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትለአከኒ ፡ ለዘ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ዘወሀበኒ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስትየኒ ፡ ማየ ። እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይትለአከኒ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ፍኖቱ ፡ ለማይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተናገረ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአድግ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ቀተለኒ ፡ ወበልዓኒ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ በመንፈሱ ፡ አቡነ ፡
27 After that, the days of the donkey being finished, a lion killed it on the order of God, to show upon it another miracle and reveal its story in all the country: if he had not known what was to happen, our father, who could (do it), would not have had the donkey killed, thanks to his prayer, by the mouth of the lion. He knew that he would conquer it (= the lion) through the power of the greatness that (was) upon him, he sent it (= the donkey), loading (it) according to his custom, knowing that the strength of the miracle of the end of the donkey would be greater than (that) of the miracle of its mission. Having delayed a little moment, our father set out while grieving and saddening as if he did not know, hiding the greatness with which the Holy Spirit had provided him so that it would not be for him a subject of vainglory, meditating on the humility of God which (is mentioned) in the Gospel - as if he had said: "Where have you buried Lazarus? (Jn 11:34)" A second (time): "Where (shall we buy) the loaves that we will give them to eat? (Jn 6:5)" A third (time): "Where (are) you? Greetings, my friend," he said to Judas (Mt 26:49-50), hiding the power of the divinity by a divine humility. 28 This father of ours, Madhänina Egzi', executor of his (= of God's) commandment, arrived at the corpse of the donkey and said: "Who killed my donkey that served me, (I) who have no disciple that my God has given me to water me? Indeed no one serves me and long is the road of the water." When he said that, one half of the body of the donkey spoke and said: "A lion killed me and ate me." At that moment, our father rejoiced in his spirit,


[p. 414] {#po.51.p414}
ወአልዓለ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እሴብሓከ ፡ ወኣአኩተከ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘትሁቦሙ ፡ ዓስቦሙ ፡ ለጻድቃኒከ ። ኣስተበቀዕ ፡ ወእገኒ ፡ ለጊሩትከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አርአይከኒ ፡ ዕፁበ ፡ ወገበርከ ፡ ሊተ ፡ መድምመ ። ወረከብኩ ፡ ጽሑፈ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ መጽሓ ፍከ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተናገረት ፡ አድጉ ፡ ለበላዓም ፡ በቃለ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ እምግርማ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡ ለመልአክከ ፡ ር ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአ ድግ ፡ ዘወሀብከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትለአከኒ ፡ እምሰሚዐ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ኀዘንየ ፡ ለገብ ርከ ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ ነበበ ፡ ወተናገረኒ ፡ ዘቀተሎ ፡ ወዘበልዖ ፡ በፈቃድከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትናዝዞ ፡ ለነፍስየ ። እባርከከ ፡ ወእዜምር ፡ ለስምከ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ዘይ ክል ፡ አእምሮቶ ፡ ዘጸዋዕከኒ ፡ ወአዕበይከኒ ፡ ለዘኢመጠንየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙረ ፡ ይኩን" ፡ በልብየ ፡ ፈቃደከ ፡ ለገቢር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወትየ ።
lifted his eyes to heaven and prayed saying: "I glorify you and praise you, my great God, (you) who give their wages to your righteous. I beg and thank humbly your goodness for you have shown me an astonishing thing and you have done for me a prodigy." I have found written at the head of your Scripture how the donkey of Balaam spoke with the voice of men thanks to the formidable majesty of the greatness of your good angel (Nb 22:22-27). This half of the body of the donkey that you gave me so that it might serve me, because it heard the word of my affliction, (to me) your sinful servant, spoke and told me who had killed it and who had eaten it, by your will, so that you might console my soul. I bless you and I sing for your (most) high name (Ps 9:3) that no one can know, (you) who have called me and made me great for what is not (according to) my merit, so that your will to do during all the days of my life might be known in my heart."
ወፈጺሞ ፡ ጸልዮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ በኃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ አድባር ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ተፈ ሊጦ ፡ እምብዙኅ ፡ አራዊት ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አንተኑ ፡ ዘቀተልኮ ፡ ለአድግየ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ነበበ ፡ በቃለ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ መይቤ ፡ እወ ፡ አነ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ አእኩተ ፡ በአስተብም'ዖ ፡ ወበተጋንዮ ፡ ጽፉቅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላክ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እባርከከ ፡ ወእነግር ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ስብሓቲከ ፡ ዘገበርከ ፡ ሊተ ፡ በብዝኀ ፡ ፍቅርከ ፡ ወከሠትከ ፡ መድምማተ ፡ ዕፁባተ ፡ ለተናግሮ ፡ በበድነ ፡ አድግ ፡ ወበተናግሮቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አንበሳ ። እሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ፳ ኃይለ ፡ ዕዘዘ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ዘአስማዕከኒ ፡ ዮም ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ በኅበ ፡ አድግ ፡ ወክልኤተ ፡ በኅበ ፡ አንበሳ ። ቡሩክ ፡ አንተ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ወይትባረክ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወእለ ፡
29 Having finished praying, our father Madhänina Egzi' called, by the voice of God, the lion out of the mountains; (the latter) came, having separated itself from many wild beasts, and bowed down before him under his feet. Our father said to him: "(Is it) you who killed my donkey?" The (lion) spoke with the voice of men and said: "Yes, (it is) me." Immediately (Madhänina Egzi') gave thanks again with abundant supplication and gratitude, praying to God saying: "I bless you and I recount the richness of your glory, (to you) who have acted for me in the greatness of your love and have made manifest prodigies astonishing to recount with the corpse of a donkey and with the words of this lion. These are five miracles of your power that you have made me hear today: three by the donkey and two by the lion. Blessed (are) you, my God, and may your holy name be blessed forever!" And (he pronounced) many prayers that (were) like that.
ከማሁ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ጸልዮ ። ወፈጺሞ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ነዓኬ ፡ ተለአከኒ ፡ ህየ ንቴሁ ፡ ለአድግየ ፡ ወተአዘዘ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ወአተወ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትለአኮ ፡ በክሂሎቱ ፡ ለአምላክ ። ወአቡነሂ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢኀብኦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያእምርዎ ፡ አላ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ በከመ ፡ አድጉ ፡ ጽዒኖ ፡ ዝቃተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አንበሳ ። ወሖረ ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይቀድኅ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቀድኅ ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀድሐ ፡ አድግ ፡ ዘቀተሎ ። ወነቦረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትለአኮ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይሬአ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ዘአሰፈዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአቡነ ። ወሰብአሰ ፡ እለ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ወአስተብፅዑ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ተኣም ሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢርኢነ ፡ ወኢሰማዕነ ፡ ቀዳሚኒ ፡ ነበረ ፡ አድግ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይረድኦ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ይትለአኮ ፡ ቀታሌ ፡ አድጉ ። ወከመዝ ፡ እንከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ዜና ፡ ዝክሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ። ወፈኃሩ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኖሙ ፡ አበ ፡ ወመምኅፃነ ።
30 Having finished, our father said to the lion: "Come then, serve me in the place of my donkey." (The lion) obeyed his word and returned with him to serve him by the power of God. Our father Madhänina Egzi' did not hide it, so that one might not know it; on the contrary he sent it publicly as his donkey, loading waterskins on the lion. (The latter) would go and bring him water; no one drew for it and it drew as the donkey that it had killed had drawn. It remained to serve him until our father saw the day that God had promised him. The people who saw it marveled and were stunned by the mode of the miracles of our father, saying: "We have (never) seen such a servant of God and we have not heard (of one): before a donkey helped him and here is a lion, murderer of his donkey, serving him!" Thus then the account of the fame of our father reached all the country. (The people) betrothed their souls to the gift of the prayer of our father so that (the latter) might be for them a father and a refuge; numerous (were) those who learned the power


[p. 416] {#po.51.p416}
ብዙኃን ፡ እለ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ግሩም ፡ ወጊሩታቲሁ ፡ መድ ምመ ። ወንሕነሰ ፡ ንበሎ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘየኀድግ" ፡ ዝክረ ፡ ለውሉዱ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ምክሐ ፡ ለዘመዱ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘተወከለ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ በረከቱ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውሉዱ ።
formidable of his miracles and his astonishing virtues. As for us, let us say to him: Happy the father who leaves a memory to his sons and is an object of glorification for his family! Happy also the man who has committed himself to God (Ps 84[83]:13): he practices the power of a father! His blessing manifests itself, happy, upon his children.
ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ወተኣምረ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ረከበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዥሉ ፡ ገጸ ፡ መጻሕፍት ። ወሜጠ ፡ እዝኖ ፡ ለሰሚዐ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ በልሳኑ ፡ ፈረየ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መዓዛ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ሕይወት ። ወእሎንተ ፡ ጽሑፋተ ፡ አንበበ ፡ ምዕዳነ ፡ አመ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ይወስክ ፡ ገድለ ፡ እለ ፡ ይብላሁ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወኢተሀብ ፡ ንዋመ ፡ ለአዕይንቲከ ፡ ወኢድቃሰ ፡ ለቀራንብቲከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትድኀኅን ፡ ከመ ፡ ወይጠል ፡ እመሥገርት ፡ ወከመ ፡ ዖፍ ፡ እመጽፈርት ። ጳውሎስኒ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ኢንኑም ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወኢ ኮነ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ እስመ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ንጎነ ፡ ንንሣእ ፡ ልብ ሰነ ፡ ወንትቀጸል ፡ ጌራ ፡ መድኃኒትነ ። ንንቃህ ፡ ወንጥበብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንትነ ከብ ፡ ዕራ ፡ መድኃኒትነ ። ንንቃህ ፡ ወንጥበብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንትረከብ ፡ ዕራ ቃኒነ ። ወቃለ ፡ እግዚእነሂ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ቅኑተ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሓቓክሙ ፡ ወኅትወ ፡ ማሓትዊክሙ ፡ ወአንትሙኒ ፡ ኩኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕደው ፡ እለ ፡ ይጸንሑ ፡ እግዚ ኦሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ የዓቱ ፡ እምከብካብ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ። መጺኦ ፡ ወጐድጐዶ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ያርኅውዎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ የአቱ ። ያነቅህ ፡ ልቦ ፡ በዘ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ተግሣጸ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይምዕዳሁ ፡ በጥበብ ፡ ወውእቱሂ ፡ አቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፌጽም ፡ ቃሎሙ ፡ ነበረ ፡ በትጋህ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወገድል ፡ ትሩፍ ፡ እምዘ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዘከርነ ።
31 After our father, light of the world, had obtained all this greatness and (these) miracles, he sent his thought into all the places of the Scriptures. He turned his ear toward the listening of wisdom (Pr 2:2); by his tongue blossomed the fragrant tree, the tree of life (Rv 22:2). He had as exhortation, when he wanted to redouble the (ascetic) combat, these writings which said to him thus: Do not give sleep to your eyes nor drowsiness to your eyelids so as to be saved like the gazelle from the net and like the bird from the trap (Pr 6:4-5). (It is) also Paul who says: Let us not sleep at night, we are not children of darkness, for we are children of the light. Let us take our garment and crown ourselves with the helmet of our salvation. Let us watch and be wise so that we are not found naked (1 Th 5:5-8). (There is) also the word of Our Lord who says: Let your loins be girded and your lamps lit. And you, be like men who wait for their lord until he returns from the wedding that (takes place) in heaven. Having come and having knocked, when he returns, let one open to him then (Lk 12:35-36). (Madhänina Egzi') kept his heart awake by such admonitions of the Scriptures, (these) exhorting him with wisdom; he himself, our father, fulfilling their word, remained in frequent vigil and in an (ascetic) combat more brilliant than that which we have mentioned previously.
ወእምዝ ፡ በአሓቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግ ዚእ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ መስመሪ ፡ ዘክርስቶስ ። ወናሁ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ጊዜሁ ፡ ዘእቤለከ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወንኡስ ። ወአንተሂ ፡ አስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ወተወከፎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተውህቡ ፡ ለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑከ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ወአን ተሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩኖሙ ፡ አበ ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይኃሪት ፡ ትውልድ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ለቃልከ ፡ ኦሆ ። ኦመልአክ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በምንት ፡ የሐይዉ ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ዘትቤለኒ ፡ ወአልቦሙ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘይሰትዩ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ኢትትቃወም ፡ ፍትሐ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ በረከተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእስከ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ይትከሀል ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘይሰአነከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀለወ ፡ ዝክርከ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ለልዑል ፡ ወይትአኩት ፡ በኀበ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወይትነከር ፡ በኅበ ፡ ኪሩብ ፡ ወየዓርፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስያሖሙ ፡ ለሱራፌል ። ወከመዝ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ውዳሴ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወአስተፍሥሖ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ሰማያተ ።
32 After that, one day, while our father Madhänina Egzi' was praying, Gabriel the angel came to him and said to him: "Greetings to you, man accepted by Christ! Here has arrived the time that I told you: the great and the small will gather to you; as for you, gather them and welcome them, for they have been given to you by God to be for you sons and for you, you to be for them a father, in justice, until the last generation." Our father Madhänina Egzi' said to him: "Yes to your word, angel of the Holy Spirit; on what will those live whom you told me would come here: they will have no water to drink?" The angel said to him again: "Do not stand against the sentence of God, for the blessing of God is upon your head; everything is possible through your prayer and there is no thing that is impossible for you. Indeed your reputation is great before the Most High, it is praised by the angels, admired by the cherubim and it rests in the cup of the seraphim." Thus the angel gave praise to our father Madhänina Egzi', rejoiced him and went up to heaven.


[p. 418] {#po.51.p418}
ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አኀዙ ፡ ይምጽኡ ፡ በበ፳ ፡ ወበበ፪ ፡ እስከ ፡ መልኡ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ብዙኃነ ። ወሶበ ፡ በዝ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ዘይትለአኮ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እንከ ፡ ኮነከ ፡ ጂ ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትለአከኒ ፡ ይእዜሰ ፡ ወሀበኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ እለ ፡ ይትለአኩኒ ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ሖር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዘፍጥረትከ ። ወባረኮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያኅጥኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይረስዮ ፡ ግሩመ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ አራዊት ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘአሥመሮ'' ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፳ እምሠናያን ፡ አርድእት ። ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡
33 From that day, (the people) began to come one by one and two by two until they multiplied and became numerous. When his children had become numerous for him, our father Madhänina Egzi' called the lion that served him and said to him: "Here now it is seven years that you have been serving me, you go into what (is) of your nature." And our father Madhänina Egzi' blessed it so that God would not make it lack its food and to make it more formidable than all the wild beasts that (are) in the desert, because it had been pleasing to our father Madhänina Egzi' as one among good disciples. The lion bowed down before our father Madhänina Egzi' in the presence of his children and went into its previous way of being.
መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ፡ ንብረቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ አብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ጥንተ ፡ ሃይማ ኖትየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ጽንዕ ፡ ለድካምየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘአ ቀምካ ፡ ለእግርየ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ በጽድቅከ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘመሀርከ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ለልብየ ፡ ወአርታዕከ ፡ ሕሊናየ ፡ በመዳልወ ፡ ሕግከ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘአዕረገኒ ፡ ውስተዝ ፡ ደብር ፡ በእደ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ጎር ፡ ወይእ ዜኒ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ ስእለትየ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለአቡከ ፡ መሓሪ ፡ ወበስመ ፡ መንፈስከ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ዘይዔሪ ። ወናሁ ፡ በዝ ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ፈኖከ ፡ ብስረተ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ርድኦሙ ፡ ወዕቀቦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዘዚአከ ፡ ህሉና ። አንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘኃረይኮሙ ፡ ወአምጻዕኮሙ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባርኩ ፡ ወይቀድሱ ፡ ስመከ ፡ ለዓለም ።
34 After that, the father prayed and said: "Jesus-Christ, principle of my faith; Jesus-Christ, strength for my weakness; Jesus-Christ, (you) who have set my foot so that it might be in your justice; Jesus-Christ, (you) who have taught wisdom to my heart and have straightened my thought with the balance of your law; Jesus-Christ, (you) who have made me go up on this mountain by the hand of the good angel, now, listen to my request in the name of your merciful Father and in the name of your Spirit who is equal to you. Here my children have multiplied according to what you sent the good news to me; help them and keep them in your protection. It is you who have chosen them and brought them here so that they might bless and sanctify your name forever."
መሶበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በሰረገላ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ በክልኤ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ተፈሣሕኩ ፡ ብከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘትገብር ፡ ሊተ ። ወናሁ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ኣስተፍሥሖ ፡ ለልብከ ፡ ወለ ምንት ፡ ትቴክዝ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንብረቶሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ማያት ፡ ዘይሰትዩ ፡ ደቂ ቅከ ። ኢሰማዕከኑ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ብክሙ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ መጠነ ፡ ሕጠተ ፡ ስናፔ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ትቤልዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሰግላ ፡ ተመልሒ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥርውኪ ፡ ወተተከሊ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ትትኤዘዝ ፡ ለክሙ ። ወለደብርኒ ፡ ፍልስ ፡ እም ዝየ ፡ ከሓ ፡ ወይፈልስ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ ብየ ፡ ዘአነ ፡ እገብር ፡ ይገ ብር ፡ ወዘየዓቢ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይገብር ። ተአምኖ ፡ ይባልሕ ፡ ወተወ ክሎ ፡ ይሴርሕ ። ስማዕ ፡ ኅሩይየ ፡ አነ ፡ እሁቦሙ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ አንቅዕተ ፡ ማያት ፡ እስመ ፡ በሥምረትየ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ወኢይጌሱ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ እምአፉየ ። ወአርአዮ ፡ በበገጹ ፡ ዘሂ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ምጥማቃተ ፡ ወዘሂ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ለተልእኮ ፡ ምሥ ጢር ፡ ወባዕደኒ ፡ እለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ ዘይከውና ፡ ለስቴሆሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ዓቀቡ ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ ሕግየ ፡ ወዓቀቡ ፡ ኪዳንየ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ለትውልደ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ወማያት ፡ ይከውና ፡ ለበረከት ።
35 When our father Madhänina Egzi' had prayed thus, Our Lord Jesus-Christ came to him on the chariot of the cherubim, as well as his mother Mary twice virgin. He said to him: "Greetings to you, my beloved! I have rejoiced concerning you in everything that you did for me. Here I have come to rejoice your heart; why do you sadden yourself about the living conditions of your children and the waters that they drink? Have you not heard what is said: If you had faith like a grain of mustard seed and if you said to this mulberry tree: Uproot yourself with your roots and plant yourself in the sea, it would obey you (Lk 17:6). And (if you said) to the mountain: Pass from here to over there, it would pass (Mt 17:20). Whoever believes in me, what I do, he (will) do, and what is greater than that, he (will) do (Jn 14:12). Faith frees and confidence makes one succeed. Listen, my chosen one: I, I give you twelve sources of water, for it is by my good pleasure that (your children) have gathered and what comes out of my mouth is not lying." And he showed him in detail that which would be the baptismal font, that which would be for the service of the (Eucharistic) mystery and the others that remained which would be for their drink. He said to him: "If your children keep my law and keep my covenant, (I will be) with them for the generations of generations and the waters will be a blessing."


[p. 420] {#po.51.p420}
ስምዑኬ ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ይከውኖሙ ፡ ለበረከት ፡ ለእመ ፡ ዓቀቡ ፡ ሕግየ ። ንትዓቀብ ፡ ንኅነኒ ፡ በአምጣነ ፡ ንክል ፡ ወመዊእሰ ፡ ወክሂል ፡ ዘአምላክነ ፡ ውእቱ ። ንኅሥሥ ፡ ወንትሉ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ሃይማኖቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትፍልስ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ መሐላሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ወንነብብ ፡ መጻሕ ፍተ ፡ ዘይጌሥፁነ ።
36 Listen therefore to what God said to our father: "(The water) will be for them a blessing if they keep my law." Let us keep ourselves, we too, as much as we can; as for the victory and the power, they are of our God. Let us seek and follow the trace of the faith of our father so that the sworn covenant of our father does not pass; we read the Scriptures that will correct us.
ከመዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ ለእመ ፡ ዓቀብከ ፡ ሕግየ ፡ ወሖርከ ፡ በትእዛዝየ ፡ እሁበከ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ርስት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትውኀዝ ፡ ሓሊበ ፡ ወመዓረ ፡ ወለደቂቅከ ፡ ከመሁ ፡ በድኅሬከ ። ወበይስሓቅ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አቀመ ፡ መሓላሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ በሂለጥና ፡ አቡሁ ። ወለያዕቆብኒ ፡ ተጋደሎ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይጸብሕ ፡ ወወለጠ ፡ ስሞ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ዘእግዚአብሔር ፡ ብሂል ። ወእምዝ ፡ በ፪ትውልድ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ወበቀለ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ደቂቆሙ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፀሮሙ ፡ ወአውፅዖሙ ፡ እምእቶን ፡ ንዱድ ፡ ወእምኀዩን ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። ወእምሰማይ ፡ መና ፡ ሴሰዮሙ ፡ ወማየሂ ፡ እምኩኩሕ ፡ አስተዮሙ ፡ ወአዝነመ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ከመ ፡ መሬት ፡ ዘኢርእዩ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ። ወባዕዳነሂ ፡ እለ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አመ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ በኪዳነ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ አቡሆሙ ። ወአ መሂ ፡ ክሕዱ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ኀዘነ ፡ ወገዓረ ። ወበውስተ ፡ ባዕድሂ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ዕቀብ ፡ ሕገገ ፡ አቡከ ፡ ወጻማ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ባዕድ ፡ ኢይባእ ። አክሊሎሙ ፡ ለአእሩግ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ውሉድ ፡ ወምክሖሙ ፡ ለውሉድ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ። ወሲራክኒ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዘየዓቅብ ፡ ቀምሖ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ፍሬሃ ፡ ለምድር ። ቀምሕሰ ፡ ጽጌያተ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዘጸገያ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ ወፍ ሬኒ ፡ ዘፈረያ ፡ ምግባራቲሁ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ልዑል ፡ እለ ፡ ይምፅዛ ፡ አፈዋተ ፡ ወተዘግባ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንበራ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይ ትነከያ ፡ ወኢይቀብላ ። ወከማሁ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ዕደው፤! ፡ አስመዩ ፡ በኃይሎሙ ፡ ወመካርያን ፡ በጥ በቦሙ ፡ ተደለዉ ፡ በሕይወቶሙ ። ወቦእምውስቴቶሙ ፡ ስመ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ እለ ፡ ኀደጉ ፡ ዘይትናገሩ"” ፡ ቦቱ ። እሉ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዘየዓቅቡ ፡ ሥርዓቶ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ በተግሣጸ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወረከቡ ፡ ሞገሰ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ከመ ፡ ስብሓተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ በቅድስት ፡ ደመና ፡ ወዘገቡ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ በዓፀደ ፡ መርዓ ፡ ወተሠርገዉ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ዘበሰማያት ።
37 Thus said God to Abraham: "If you keep my law and walk according to my commandment, I will give you a land of inheritance that flows with milk and honey, and to your children likewise after you (Gn 17:9)." With Isaac also he instituted his covenant when his father saw him at Hilatenä. Jacob also, he wrestled with him until it was day and changed his name to Israel, which means God (Gn 32:25-29). After that, at the fourth generation, he performed a miracle and took vengeance for their (= the patriarchs') children from the enemies of the latter; he brought them out of the burning furnace and tempered iron toward the desert (Jr 11:4). From heaven he fed them with manna, and with water also from the rock he watered them: he made meat rain for them, like dust, which their fathers had not seen (Ex 16:4-15). He also performed other (miracles) that (are) like those: when they walked in the covenant of Abraham their father and when also they were renegades, he brought upon them affliction and groaning. In another (place), he says: My son, keep the laws of your father (Pr 6:20). Let your pain not enter the house of the neighbor (Pr 5:10). The crown of the elderly, (these are) the sons of the sons and the glory of the sons, their fathers (Pr 17:6); in their way of living the blessing of the fathers is manifested. Sirach also says: He who keeps his harvest will eat the fruit of the earth (Si 27:6). The harvest, (these are) the flowers of the (spiritual) combat of our father that have blossomed in his body and the fruits, his actions that have borne fruit before the Most High, which exhale perfumes and have been stored in the houses of Paradise to remain forever, without being damaged nor exhausted. 38 Likewise our fathers (were) men who were renowned by their power and counselors by their wisdom. They were fortunate during their life. There are among them some who have left a glorious name of which one can speak (Si 44:3, 7-8). These (are) the children of our father, who kept his rule and walked according to the instructions of our father. They have found grace before the Trinity, like the glory of the light in the holy cloud; they have accumulated the richness of justice in the enclosure of the wedding and have been adorned with the garments of the kingdom that (is) in heaven.
ወንኅነኒ ፡ ንትዓቀብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንርከብ ፡ ርስተ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ እስመ ፡ አቀመ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ማእከሌነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ለእመ ፡ ዓቀቡ ፡ ደቀቅከ ፡ ሕግየ ። ወበዝ ፡ ቃል ፡ ንትዓቀብ ፡ ወንፍራህ ፡ ሥርዓቶ ፡ ለአቡነ ። ወውእ ቱሂ ፡ ኢረከበ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በመንኖ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሞትክሙ ፡ እምንብረተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወተሰቀልክሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡
39 We too, let us keep ourselves to obtain the inheritance of our fathers, for (God) has instituted a covenant in our midst by saying: "If your children keep my law." By this word let us keep ourselves and fear the law of our father. He too (only obtained it by despising his life, according to what Paul says: Indeed you are dead to the life of this world (Col 2:20, 3:3) and are crucified


[p. 422] {#po.51.p422}
ክርስቶስ ። በእፎ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ከመዘ ፡ ሕያዋን ፡ ትገብሩ ፡ ወይቤሉክሙ ፡ ኢት ግሥሥ ፡ ወኢትልክፍ ፡ ወኢትጥዓም ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ለሙስና ፡ በከመ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘያመስልዎ ፡ ጥበበ ። ወአንተሰ ፡ በትሕትና ፡ ልብ ፡ ንበር ፡ በኢምሒከክ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ አኮ ፡ በክብር ፡ አላ ፡ በጽጋብ ። ወዓዲ ። ዘነቢይኒ ፡ ለቢዎ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ አርዑቶ ፡ ወይጻሙ ፡ ወይነብር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። ፈጸመ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በጾም ፡ ጽፉቅ ፡ በአብዝኖ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ዘያደነግፅ ፡ አልባበ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ በቀዔተ ፡ ለውሉዱ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ተማኅፀኑ ፡ ቦቱ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወጻማ ፡ ትዕግሥቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰማፅያን ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ። ወበ8- እምጊዜያት ፡ አኀዘ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእምር ፡ ኅበ ፡ የሓንጽ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርሰቲያን ። ወጾመ ፡ ጓመዓልተ ፡ ወጓሌሊተ ፡ ወኢጥዕመ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ዕፀው ። አኮ ፡ በጾም ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ በቀዊም ፡ ወበሰጊድ ፡ ከመ ፡ መንኩራኩር ፡ በአንብዕኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነጸፍጻፈ ፡ ደም ፡ እስከ ፡ ይረክብ ፡ ተምኔቶ ፡ ዘፈተወ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ አጻመወ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ ኅቡአ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ግወዓልተ ፡ ወጓ ልሊተ ። አስተርአዮ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ትኩለ ፡ ዘይበጽሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወዲቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀስተ ፡ ደመና ፡ ወልሳነ ፡ እሳት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንበለብል ፡ ዓውዳ ፡ ወደብተራ ፡ እሳት ፡ እንጡሉዕ ፡ በፒለሰ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይሠዋዕ!' ፡ በግዑ ፡ ለእግዚአ
with Christ (Ga 2:19). How still do you act as if (you were) alive? One says to you: Do not handle, do not touch, do not taste: all that (is destined) for corruption, according to the commandments of the men of this world that they imagine to be wisdom (Col 2:20-23). As for you, remain in the humility of heart, without sparing the soul (Col 2:23), not in glory but in satiety. And again, meditating (on the word) of the prophet who says: (It is) good for man to bear his yoke, to be silent and to remain alone (Lm 3:27-28), (our father) accomplished this in a severe fast (and) by redoubling austerities that make hearts tremble. He was profitable to his sons who trusted in him. May the prayer, the blessing and the austerity of the discipline of this father of ours Madhänina Egzi' be with … and with all the listeners in the centuries of centuries, amen and amen. 40 One (time) among the times, our father Madhänina Egzi' began to pray, according to his custom, in order to know where he would build a church. He fasted forty days and forty nights and tasted only the fruit of the trees. He did not (remain) only in fasting, (but also) in standing, in prostration - like a wheel - and in tears - like drops of blood - until he obtained (the fulfillment of) the vow that he had made. When God saw that he had tested his body, he revealed to him a hidden mystery after he had accomplished the forty days and forty nights (of austerities). He made him see a planted column of light that reached up to heaven; upon it (were) like a rainbow and a tongue of fire burning around it, as well as a canopy of fire veiled by the door of the divinity, where is sacrificed the lamb of God who takes away the sin of the world.
ብሔር ፡ ዘያአትት ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወእንዘ ፡ ያነክር ፡ አቡነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ግሩመ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ እስመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ጸዋሬ ፡ ዜና ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱ ሳን ፡ ወአምዣፕ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በሓከ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ጥበብ ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ብስራት ፡ ነዓ ፡ ኣርኢከ ፡ ጥቅመ ፡ እሳት ፡ ቅጽራ ። ወአርአዮ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወፈከረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ብርሃንሰ ፡ ዘርኢከ ፡ ሃይማኖትከ ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ዘየሓቱ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በማእከላ ። ዘይበጽሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ዘተሰምዐ ፡ ዝክርከ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ስብሓተ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡ ለልዑል ፡ ወቅድመ ፡ መላእክቲሁ ፡ ጌራን ፡ በውስተ ፡ አርያም ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀስተ ፡ ደመና ፡ ዘዲቤሁ ። ደመናሰ ፡ ሥጋከ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ወቀስትኒ ፡ ምግባራቲከ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ወነበልባለ ፡ እሳት ፡ እንተ ፡ ተአውዶ ፡ ቃለ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ዘላዕሌከ ። ወደብተራኒ ፡ እንጡሉዕ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት ፡ መርዓተ ፡ በግዑ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፡ ዘሥርጉት ፡ በፒላሳሃ ፡ ለኢየሩሰሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ። ዛቲ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዓውደ ፡ በረከት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትዘግብ ፡ ስርናየ ፡ ቄቅሓ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ክርዳድ ። ዛቲ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ጺኦት ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡
41 While our father was marveling because he had seen a terrifying vision, Gabriel the angel came to him, for his custom (is) to bring an announcement to the saints; he greeted our father saying: "Greetings to you, seat of wisdom!" He said to him again with a word of annunciation: "Come, let me show you the rampart of fire (Zc 2:9), its (= the column's) wall." And he showed (it) to him on the order of God, explained to him the mystery and said to him: "The column of fire that you saw, (it is) your upright faith that will shine for the universe, in the midst of it; that which will reach up to heaven, (it is) your fame that has been carried before the glory of the greatness of the Most High and before his good angels in the heights of heaven, like a rainbow that (is) upon it. The cloud (is) your pure body, the arc, your luminous actions and the flames of fire that surround it (= the column), the word of the divinity that (is) upon you. As for the veiled canopy, (it is) the holy Church, the bride of the lamb of the God of hosts, which (is) adorned at the door of the heavenly Jerusalem. This is the threshing floor of blessing near which you will accumulate the wheat winnowed without tares. This is


[p. 424] {#po.51.p424}
ዘይትረዓዩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አባግዕ ፡ ጽጉባን ፡ እምክልኤ ፡ አጥባቲሃ ፡ ሐሊበ ፡ እሞሙ ። ዛቲ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ተድላ ፡ እለ ፡ ተተከሉ ፡ ዕፀወ ፡ ቄድሮስ ፡ ነዋኃት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ደመና ፡ እለ ፡ ይጸግዩ ፡ ዘዘዚአሁ ፡ ሱራሔ ፡ ስኑ ። ቦዘቀይሕ ፡ ወቦ ፡ ዘፀዓድዒድ ፡ ወቦ ፡ ዘኩሰኩሳን ፡ በአምጣነ ፡ ገድ ሎሙ ። ወመዓዛ ፡ ጽድቆሙ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ እምጽባሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዓረብ ፡ ወመስዕ ፡ ወባሕር ። ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ከዒሚቶ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ።
is the spiritual meadow where the lambs, satisfied with their mother's milk (coming) from her two breasts, will go to graze. This is the Garden of Delights, the cedars that have been planted (there) are high in the cloud, they who bloom with a splendor of varied beauty: there is one that is red, there is one that is white, there are some that are multicolored in proportion to their (spiritual) struggle. The fragrance of their righteousness has spread from the east to the west, the north and the south." Having revealed all this to our father, the angel ascended to heaven.
ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ኅዝንት ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ትክዝት ፡ ጸርሓት ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ስምዓኒ ፡ ወተሣሃለኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ተአምር ፡ ጻህቃ ፡ ወፈቃዳ ፡ ለልብየ ። እንበይነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ እገኒ ፡ ለከ ፡ ወእሴብሓከ ፡ ወእባርክ ፡ ለስምከ ፡ አግዚኦ ። ወፈጺሞ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ በብካይ ፡ ወበአንብዕ ፡ ውዑይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ኢይገብር ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ በሥምረተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ያቅድም ፡ ጸሎተ ፡
42 After this, our father Madhänina Egzi' prayed again, saying: "O my Lord, King of the whole universe, God of Israel, an afflicted soul and a saddened spirit have cried out to you; hear me and be merciful (Bar 3:1-2) to me, for you know the zeal and the will of my heart. Because of this, I humbly give you thanks, I glorify you, and I bless your name, O Lord (Sir 51:1)." He finished this prayer in tears and burning weeping; indeed, this was our father's custom: he did nothing of his own will without having first made a prayer and an adjuration.
ወምህላ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ጻህቆ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እግዚአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዘክሮ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ሰጊድ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ድንግል ፡ በክልኤ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ በየማኑ ፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ በፀጋሙ ። ወአእላፈ ፡ አእላፋት ፡ ይዜምሩ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወሰረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ያንበሰብስ ፡ በታሕቴሁ ። ኃይላት ፡ ይየብቡ ፡ ወሊቃናት ፡ ይቄድሱ ። ነቢያት ፡ ያነብቡ ፡ መዝሙረ ፡ ዘተነብዮ ። ወሐዋርያት ፡ ይጸውሩ ፡ ወንጌለ ፡ ዘትስብእቱ ። ሰማ ዕት ። ወጻድቃን ፡ ያዜክርዎ ፡ ሣህለ ፡ ወምሕረተ ፡ ለሰብእ ። ወወረደ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ክብር ፡ ወስብሓት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ ወወድቀ ። ወአንሥኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ አየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ፍቁርየ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አአምርከ ፡ ዘትገብር ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አመርክዎሙ ፡ ለአርዳእየ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወጳውሎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕንጹ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ለእምየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ ገበርከ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዘኢድልወትየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ ወአባሲ ፡ ሐመድ ፡ ወመሬት ፡ አነ ። ወዘ ንተ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያውኀዝ ፡ አንብዐ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ቅድመ ፡
43 When He saw his zeal—the manner in which he desired—Our Lord Jesus Christ—at the mention of whose name adoration returns—descended to him, as well as his twice-virgin mother Mary; with Him (there was) Michael at his right and Gabriel at his left. Myriads of myriads sang before Him and a chariot of light bounded beneath Him. The Powers exulted and the Watchers sanctified. The prophets recited the psalms of prophecy. The apostles carried the gospel of His incarnation. The martyrs and the righteous reminded Him of clemency and mercy toward men. (Jesus) descended to him in great pomp and glory. 44 When our father Madhänina Egzi' saw (this), he became afraid and fell. Our Lord Jesus Christ raised him up and said to him: "Do not be afraid, my beloved, I have come to show you what you are to do, just as I showed my disciples Peter and Paul that they were to build the house of my mother." Our father Madhänina Egzi' said to Him: "My Lord and my God, you have performed a great (act of) love toward me, who am not worthy of it, while I am a sinner and a transgressor, ash and dust." He said this while shedding tears like a son weeping before his father.
አቡሁ ። ወርእዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ብካዮ ፡ ናዘዞ ፡ ወአስተፍሥሖ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ኣርኢከ ፡ መጠና ። ወመጠነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኑኃ ፡ መሠረታ ፡ ወመሠረተ ፡ ግድማ ፡ ይምና ፡ ወፅግማ ፡ ወቆማሂ ። ወመጠነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ወለሊሁ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አየሱስ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ማእከላ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትከውን ፡ ቅድስተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ዳቤር ፡ እስመ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ካህናቲነ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ሓሚመ ፡ ሕማምነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ሓዋርያነ ፡ ዘተፈነወ ፡ ለመድኃኒትነ ። ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡
45 Seeing his tears, Our Lord consoled him, made him joyful, and said to him: "Come, let me show you its (= the church's) measurements." And He measured for him its length, the foundation of its width, its left, its right, and also its height. He measured for him before him; He Himself, the Lord Jesus Christ, stood in the middle of it, where the holy of holies would be, before the choir, for He (is) our high priest who can suffer with our sufferings, Jesus our apostle who was sent for our salvation; Jesus Christ, the fragrance of our balm


[p. 426] {#po.51.p426}
መዓዛ ፡ ዕፍረትነ ። ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘይቄድስ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ግበሪሃ ፡ ለእ ምነ ። ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ትምህርትነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንፈኑ ፡ ስብሓተ ፡ በአፈ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
Jesus Christ who sanctifies the tool of our mother's service; Jesus Christ, the head of our teaching; to Him let us address glory with all our mouths, forever and ever, amen.
ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለ ፳ ወ፪ካህናተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ በበማዕጠንታቲሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኡድዋ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ዘከርነ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ወረዱ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ በስብሓት ፡ ይኡድዋ ፡ እስመ ፡ ያፈቅራ ፡ ለመርዓቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ተሣየጣ ፡ በይሙ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወዓተባ ፡ በዕፀ ፡ መስቀሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፈሪ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሙሉደ ፡ እለ ፡ መን ግሥት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ግበር ፡ ለከ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ በስማ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ እምየ ፡ ወአዕርግ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ወደምየ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘጸለየ ፡ በስምየ ፡ ወበስምከ ፡ እሰምዖ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ወስእለቶ ፡ ወእብሎ ፡ ነየ ፡ አነ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ ወዘጸሓፈ ፡ መጽሓፈ ፡ ገድልከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትአመን ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ እሁቦ ፡ ምርፋቀ ፡ ወምሳሐ ፡ ዘ ፲፻ ዓመት ፡ ሕይወተ ፡
46 After this, Our Lord Jesus Christ ordered the twenty-four priests of heaven to go around it (= the church), each with his censer, and (to) all those whom we mentioned previously as having descended, He ordered them to go around it; indeed, He loved His bride, for He Himself had bought her with His glorious blood and signed her with the wood of His cross because He knew that she would bear for Him the children of the kingdom (of heaven). Our Lord said to our father: "Make for yourself an altar table in the name of Mary my mother and offer upon it my body and my blood; whoever prays in my name and in your name, I will hear his prayer and his request and will say to him: Here I am. Whoever shall make your commemoration and write the book of your life while trusting in your prayer, I will give him the banquet and the feast of a thousand years, (as well as) the life that (is) forever."
ዘለዓለም ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ባርካ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ቡርክተ ፡ ወሥርጉተ ፡ በስብ ሓት ፡ ከመ ፡ አድባራት ፡ እለ ፡ ተከሥታ ፡ በትንቢት ፡ ወተገብረ ፡ በውስቴ ቶን ፡ኃይለ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በትእምርት ፡ ወደኃሪ ፡ በአርአዮ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በምሥጢር ፡ ወደኃሪ ፡ በነገር ፡ ትኩን ፡ ቡርክተ ፡ ከማሆን ። ከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ኤዶም ፡ ካልእታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቀራንዮ ፡ ወደብረ ፡ ሲና ። ከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ታቦር ፡ ወደብረ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ወከመ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መድኃኒት ። ይኩን ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ከማሆሙ ፡ ወተሀሉ ፡ በስብሓትየ ፡ እስከ ፡ እመጽእ ፡ በፈቃድየ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሣህልየ ፡ ወምሕረትየ ፡ ዘይሜግባ ፡ ሰላምየ ፡ ወጽድቅየ ፡ ይኩን ፡ በመርኅባ ። በእንቲአከ ፡ አነ ፡ ኣዓ ቅባ ፡ ወኢይቀርብ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ኀቤሃ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘተገበረ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያመልክ ፡ ከያየ ፡ ወይትአመን ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ እሰሪ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አበሳሁ ፡ ወኢይዜከር ፡ ጌጋዮ ። እሁቦ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ኮነ ፡፣
47 Our father Madhänina Egzi' said to Our Lord: "My Lord, bless this church." (The Lord) said to him: "May it be blessed and adorned with glory like the mountains that were revealed by prophecy and upon which the power of heaven was accomplished, first by a sign, then by the manifestation of power; first by a mystery, then by the word; may it be blessed like them, like Mount Zion first and like Mount Edom afterwards, like Mount Golgotha and Mount Sinai; like Mount Tabor and the Mount of Olives and like Jerusalem, the city of salvation. May its fame be like theirs and may it be with my glory until I come by my will. May my clemency and my mercy surround my peace and may my justice be in its place. For your sake, I will guard it and no evil shall approach it. Whoever shall work therein by adoring me and trusting in your prayer, I will remit his faults and will not remember his sins. I will give him a reward with the righteous and, if he has been virtuous, I will increase the reward."
ጌረ ፡ ኣፈደፍድ ፡ ዕሜተ ። መይቤሎ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ምዕረፈ ፡ ሥጋከ ። ወለ እለ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እለ ፡ ከማከ ፡ እለ ፡ ያበርሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ከዋክ ብት ፡ በሥነ ፡ ጽድቆሙ ፡ ወይምፅዙ ፡ ከመ ፡ አፈወ ፡ ዕጣን ፡ በጻማ ፡ ገድ ሎሙ ። ወይትወለዱ ፡ ለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ አዕናቀ ፡ ክቡራት ፡ እለ ፡ ተዘግባ ፡ በአክዋለ ፡ ወርቅ ። ወለዛቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ኣልዕላ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ወአምዕዛ ፡ በገድሎሙ ፡ ወእቄድሳ ፡ በአዕፅምቲሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይ ብል ፡ ይከውን ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀለወ ፡ አዕፅምቲሆሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ደብር ፡ እንተ ፡ ወሀ ብኩከ ፡ ርስተ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ወከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ታቦር ፡ ሙራደ ፡ ቃል ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ እለ ፡ አሥመሩኒ ፡ ከማከ ። ይስምዑ ፡ ቃልየ ፡ ወይትፈሥሑ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኃለ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘወረድኩ ፡
48 Our Lord said to him again: "May this be the resting place of your body and of those who (are) like you, who will come in great numbers, who will shine like stars with the beauty of their righteousness and will be fragrant like the scent of incense through the austerities of their (spiritual) struggle; they will be begotten for you like precious stones that have been accumulated with golden apples. As for this church, I will raise it up for their sake, I will perfume it with their (spiritual) struggle and I will sanctify it with their bones, according to what is written: Light will come where the bones of the righteous are (Ps 97[96]:11?)." 49 Our Lord said again to our father: "As for this mountain that I have given you as an inheritance, may it be, like Mount Sinai and like Mount Tabor, a place of the descent of the Word to your children who will have pleased me like you. May they listen to my voice and rejoice, for I am the Word


[p. 428] {#po.51.p428}
ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ወተናገርኩ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ አፈ ፡ በአፍ ። ከማሁ ፡ ወረድኩ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ወተናገርኩ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ አፈ ፡ በአፍ ፡ በህየኒ ፡ አነ ፡ ወበዝየኒ ፡ አነ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አክበርክዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ደብር ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ወሉ ፡ ዘየኃድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕፅና ፡ እመሂ ፡ ተቀብረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ በጽሓ ፡ ጽላሎታ ፡ ዘሂ ፡ በምሥራቅ ፡ ወዘሂ ፡ በምዕራብ ፡ ይድኃን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኪዳን ፡ ዘተከየድኩከ ፡ ኢይጌስወከ ፡ ወእሙን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቃልየ ። ወለዘ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እሰምዖ ፡ ወእ ሁቦ ፡ ግረተ" ፡ ፀር ። ለወራዙት ፡ ዕቅበተ ፡ ለሕፃናት ፡ ልህቀተ ፡ ወስር የተ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ፡ ለቀደምት ፡ ወለደኃርት ። ለዘሰአለኒ ፡ በርትዕት ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትአመን ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እከድኖ ፡ በሣህልየ ፡ ወበምሕረትየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ደኃሪት ።
of the Father, (I) who descended upon Mount Sinai and spoke to Moses face to face (Ex 33:11). Likewise, I have descended here to you and have spoken with you face to face; there I was, here I am. Because of this, I have honored this mountain so that it might be like Mount Sinai; may whoever dwells in its bosom, if he has been buried where its shadow reaches—whether to the east or to the west—be saved thanks to the covenant that I have made with you; I do not lie to you and truthful is my word. As for the one who will pray in this church, I will hear him and give him the submission of the Enemy; (I will give) to the adolescents protection, to the little children, growth, forgiveness to the sinners, the first and the last. He who will have petitioned me with a right faith while trusting in your holy prayer, I will cover him with my clemency and my mercy until the last breath.
እንተ ፡ ተሓንጸት ፡ በስመ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እምየ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወንጽሕት ፡ እንተ ፡ ጾረተኒ ፡ በከርሣ ፡ አውራኃ ፡ ወአንሰ ፡ አፍቀርክዋ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ፍጥ ረት ፡ ዘበሰማይኒ ፡ ወዘበምድርኒ ፡ አርያምኒ ፡ ኢይልዕላ ፡ ወምድርኒ ፡ ኢይር ኅባ ። ወበስመ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ቀደስክዋ ፡ ወረሰይኩ ፡ ቡሩከ ፡ አትክልቲሃ ። ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ተፈሥሓ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ስብሓት ፡ ለከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ዘወሀብከኒ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይደልወኒ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ወአእኩቶ ፡ ብዙኃ ። እምድኅረ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሓት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ስብሓት ፡ ወክብር ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
50 "(This church) which will have been built in the name of Mary my mother, virgin and pure, who carried me in her womb for nine months, I will love it more than all the creatures that (are) in heaven and that (are) on earth; the celestial heights are not higher than it and the earth is not more extensive than it. It is in her name that I have consecrated it and I have made its plantations blessed." Our Lord Jesus Christ gave him this entire covenant. Having heard, our father Madhänina Egzi' rejoiced with great joy and said: "Glory to you, O Lord, who have given me this covenant while I am not worthy of it!" Having said this, he prostrated himself before his Lord and gave Him abundant thanks. After He had given him this entire covenant, (Jesus) ascended to the heavens in great glory—to Him befit glory and honor forever, amen.
ምንተኑ ፡ እሰምዮ ፡ ለጠባይዕከ ፡ ዘኢተምህረ ፡ ሀኬተ ፡ ለመሬቱ ፡ ኢመጾኦ ፡ ድካም ፡ ወለእሳትሰ ፡ ኢቄረ ፡ ውዕየቱ ፡ ኃያላኒከ ፡ ፪ነፋስ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወማይኒ ፡ ኢተሀውከ ፡ ለለዕለቱ ፡ ፪ እም ፪ ኢተዋከቱ ፡ ዘዘዚአሆን ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ኢያስተቱ ፡ ይፈርያ ፡ ለለዕለቱ ። እዌድሰከ ፡ አባ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ በአ ውታረ ፡ ማኅሌት ፡ ዓሠርቱ ። ረከብከ ፡ አባ ፡ መድፍነ ፡ ክቡተ ፡ ወአንተ ፡ ዘኅባእከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገራህትከ ። ወናሁ ፡ በቀሩዓቶሙ ፡ ለአዕርክቲከ ፡ ሕጠ ፡ ስናፔ ፡ ነኣስ ፡ ዘበቄለት ፡ በይዋሄ ፡ ወበርሕራሔ ፡ አሕመልመለት ፡ በመንግ ሥተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ አጽለለት ። ብፁዕ ፡ አንተ ፡ አባ ፡ እም ንዋየ ፡ ዓመፃ ፡ አጥረይከ ፡ አዕርክተ ፡ ከመ ፡ አመ ፡ ሓልቀ ፡ ይትወከፉከ ፡ በውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ ክሠተ ፡ ተአሚነከ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ ዘበብዙኅ ፡ ረከብከ ፡ ሚመተ ። ዕፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ አንተ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወትሬሲ ፡ ዕፀወ ፡ ከመ ፡
51 How shall I call your nature that has not learned laziness? Weakness has not come upon its land; as for fire, its heat has not cooled; ... and the water has not been agitated each day; (these elements) do not dispute two by two, they do not neglect each their power, they produce fruits each day. I praise you, abbä, for this with the ten strings of the harp (Ps 33[32]:2). You have found, abbä, a hidden treasure and it is you who have hidden it in your fields (Mt 13:44). Behold, a small mustard seed has profited your friends, which has grown in sweetness and, in tenderness, has turned green in the kingdom of heaven; it has overshadowed many souls (Mt 13:31-32). Blessed (are) you, abbä! Out of the goods of injustice, you have acquired friends so that, when you have perished, they might receive you into their houses openly; believing that little (is) what (is) in much, you have found a place. You (are) the tree of life that (is) in Paradise (Gn 2:9), you make the trees bloom and the dry woods bud and bear fruit, their fruit will remain with the angels. Concerning the planting


[p. 430] {#po.51.p430}

of the trees, so that they may sprout and grow, let us give a tribute so that their roots do not dry up, like the plant that has not taken root. That whose color (is) red will become white like wool. I glorify you, honored father; the sea of your praise (is) deep, which cannot be described. May your blessing remain upon us so that we may know the mystery of your glory, forever and ever, amen.
ድር ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ ስብሓቲከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ናእምር ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወእምዝ ፡ አኀዘ ፡ ይሕንጽ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በከመ ፡ አመሮ ፡ እግዚእነ ። ወፈጺሞ ፡ ሓኒጾታ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ አእባን ፡ አሠንዮ ፡ ኒቆታሃ ፡ ዘላ ፅሉ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተደሉ ፡ ሕለተ ፡ ለመዋጽሕቲሃ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቁ ። ወውእቱ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሓጋይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወዝናምኒ ፡ እምሰ ማይ ፡ አልቦ ። ወአንቅዕተ ፡ ማያትኒ ፡ ተእኅዙ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ማየ ፡ ዘይሰትዩ ፡ ወየብሰ ፡ ዕፀዊሃ ፡ ወዓረቀ ፡ አብያቲሃ ። ወሓሙ ፡ ሰብእ" ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለሞት ፡ በኃጢአ ፡ ማይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዋዕይ ፡ ወሓሩር ፡ ብሔሩ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይሣሃላ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ አእሚሮ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ኢኃብአ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ወይሁብ ፡ ተስፋሁ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘተውህቦ ፡ እምቀዳሚሁ ፡ ያእምር ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ክዋኔሁ ፡ ለፀሓይ ፡ ሙያጤሁ ፡ ወለወርኅ ፡ ሙላዳቲሁ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይትኅኀባእ ፡ እምላህበ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይገብር ፡ ከማሁ ።
52 After this, (Madhänina Egzi') began to build the church according to what Our Lord had shown him. Having finished its construction with beautiful stones, the arrangement of its treasure from above (?), then our father went to prepare the measuring rod for its beams with his children. These were the days of summer and no rain from the sky, the water springs were kept low: there was no water to drink, its (= the monastery's) trees had dried up and its houses had become empty (Is 24:12). The people had suffered and had arrived at the point of death for lack of water, for the weather (was) burning heat and scorching ardor. As for this father of ours, to show a miracle to his children and as he had pity on this region, knowing the power of his hand, he did not hide his grace and he placed his hope in the Holy Spirit who had been given to him from the beginning so that he might know the creation of the being of the sun, its revolution and the origin of the moon; (nothing) was hidden from the brazier of his thought. Fearing God, he acted thus.
ወደቂቁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ስእኑ ፡ ሐዊረ ፡ ወየብሰ ፡ ጐርዔሆሙ ፡ በጸምእ ፡ ወስእኑ ፡ ተንሥኦ ፡ እምድር ። ወአቡነሰ ፡ ዘጽሕደ ፡ በጠለ ፡ አርያም ፡ ከመ ፡ በቀልት ፡ ዘኀበ ፡ ሙኀዘ ፡ ማይ ፡ በከመ ፡ አድባረ ፡ ኤርሞን ፡ እማ ያተ ፡ ሳኔር ፡ ዘይሰቀይ ፡ ኢጸምአ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ አጥብዐ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትረአይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ተከዘ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ ወተኃየለ ፡ በመንፈሱ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ሓዋጺሃ ፡ ለነፍስ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ኃይልከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴየ ፡ ወአዝዛ ፡ ለኩኩሕ ፡ በቅድሜየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀብ ፡ ማየ ፡ ዘኢይጠፍእ ። ርኢ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሓሙ ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ አኮ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ዘእብለከ ፡አላ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘነፍስ ፡ እለ ፡ የኃድሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ምድር ። ተሀውከ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ለገብርከ ፡ ተሣሃሎሙ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ በአስተብቀዖትየ ፡ አቅመኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እኤዝዛ ፡ ለኩኩሕ ። ወቆመ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወመርሖ ፡ አሶተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ወዘበጣ ፡ ለኩኩሕ ፡ በበትረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ለአምላ ክኪ ፡ ሀቢ ፡ ማየ ፡ ዘውስቴትኪ ። ወውኅዘ ፡ ማይ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወእምድምፀ ፡ ውጊዙ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ተመናት ፡ ወአናብስት ፡ ወአናምርት ፡ ወሉ ፡ አራዊተ ፡
53 The children of our father could not walk, their throats dried up from thirst and they could not rise from the ground. But our father, who had been moistened by the dew of the celestial heights like a palm tree that (is) beside a stream, like the mountains of Hermon that are watered by the waters of Senir (Ct 4:8), did not thirst (even) a little; on the contrary, the power of his hand was constant, so that it might show itself. When he saw that his children had fallen, he was saddened in his body and was strong in his spirit; he said: "My Lord and my God, God of all that (is) flesh (Jer 32[39]:27), overseer of the soul, may your power be in my hand and command the rock, before me, to give water that does not run dry (Ex 17:6). See, O Lord, that my children have suffered; it is not (only) for them that I speak to you, but for all the souls who dwell in this land. My heart, O (I) your servant, is agitated; be merciful to them, O Lord, through my supplication, and make me stand where I will command the rock." 54 Our father then stood where there was no trace of water; the power of the spirit guided him, he struck the rock with the staff in his hand (and said:) "By the word of your God, give the water that is in you." The water flowed out immediately; because of the sound of its flowing, the great serpents, the lions, the leopards, all the wild beasts and the birds that fly gathered, just as they had gathered in the belly of the ark, where Noah had


[p. 432] {#po.51.p432}
ገዳም ፡ ወአዕዋፍ ፡ ዘይሰርራ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ኖኅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይድኃኑ ፡ እመቅሠፍት ። ወኢያሕሰመ ፡ በላዒ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ወመብልዕኒ ፡ ኢነፍጸ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ በላዒሁ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ አዕርቅተ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ወስንእዋነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትኤዘዙ ፡
made (a refuge) in order to save himself from divine punishment. He who eats did not harm him who is eaten and he who is eaten did not flee either before the mouth of him who eats him; all were friends and united by peace, until there was a year, by obeying Noah (Gn 7-8).
ለኖኅ ። ወከማሁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ጫመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስተዩ ። ወውእቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ወአስተዮሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወአንከረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡ ወግዘፉ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ተመን ፡ ዘያንቀለቅል ፡ አድባረ ፡ ወበ ቄሉ ፡ ፅፀው ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ግዘፉ ፡ ለተመን ። ወደቂቁሰ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ፈርሁ ፡ እምግርማ ፡ ምጽአቱ ። አሜሃ ፡ ተኃየለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብ ፡ ትምክሕተ ፡ ክብር ፡ ለውሉዱ ፡ ወገሠጾሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አራዊት ፡ በኃይል ፡ ዘተውህበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ አዳም ። ወተአዚዞሙ ፡ ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካናቲሆሙ ። ወትእዛዘኒ ፡ ዘይቤሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያሕስሙ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወእንስሳ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፍጻሜ ፡ ዓለም ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ ሀለወ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዮም ፡ ወአልቦሙ ፡ አሕስሞ ፡ ለአራዊት ፡ እመሂ ፡ በገዳም ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብሔር ፡ በኃይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡
55 Likewise our father Madhänina Egzi' placed the word of the miracle upon them when they arrived to him to drink. He, our father, made a blessing and gave them to drink with his children, together. Our father was greatly astonished at the size and the mass of a large serpent that shook the mountains; trees grew upon the large serpent because of its mass. The children of our father were frightened by the fearsome danger of its arrival. Then our father gathered his strength to give a subject of glory to his sons and admonished before his children all the wild beasts by the power that had been given to our father Adam: obeying and listening to his word, they went away to their places. The order he had expressed to them was that they should do no harm to people and domestic animals until the end of the world. This power of the miracle exists until today: the wild beasts do not harm, neither in the desert nor at this water point, the people of this country, by the power of the prayer of our father Madhänina Egzi'.
እግዚእ ። ኦአብ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘላዕሌከ ፡ ወስብሓቲሁ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ቃልከ ፡ እራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ይፈርሁ ፡ ለባሕርየ ፡ ዓጽምከ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አሓቲ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትክል ፡ ከማሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ በርእሳ' ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትሔ ድስ ። ናሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወከመ ፡ ናርዶስ ፡ መዓዛሁ ፡ ወአዳም ፡ ሱራሔሁ ፡ ወያምዕዘነ ፡ ለእሊአሁ ፡ አፈዋተ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
56 O father, joy of God that is upon you, and glory of Him, because of this word of yours, the wild beasts fear the grace of the essence of your bone (7); while (it is) one, it can nevertheless do everything, while (it is) in itself, it renews everything (Wis 7:27). Behold, its aroma (is) like the flower of the garden and like nard, pleasant (is) its splendor; the perfumes of his (= Madhänina Egzi's) history perfume us, (we) his own, forever and ever, amen.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ አሠነየ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአስተና በረ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቅድሳቲሃ ፡ ወመጻሕፍተኒ ፡ አስተዳሊዎ ፡ ለአንብቦ ፡ ትምህርት ፡ ወቀደሰ ፡ ወአምዓዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወአዕሪጎ ፡ ዮር ባነ ፡ በጸሎት ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወበስብሓት ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኣአኩተከ ፡ አበ ፡ ብርሃናት ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘይወፅእ ፡ እምአፉከ ፡ ዘአጽናዕካ ፡ ለምድር ፡ በኃይልከ ፡ ወአርታዕካ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በጥበብከ ፡ ወሰፋሕኮ ፡ ለሰማይ ፡ በምክርከ ። ሰማዒ ፡ አንተ ፡ ለአለ ፡ ይስእሉከ ፡ ወወሀቢ ፡ ለእለ ፡ የኃሥሠከ ፡ አርኃዌ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይጐደጐዱከ ። መዝገበ ፡ ረድኤት ፡ ምስያመ ፡ ትፍሥ ሕት ፡ ስማዕ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ጸሎትየ ፡ ዘሰማዕከ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ገብርከ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ተከላ ፡ ለደብተራ ፡ ወመላእከ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ጢሰ ፡ ስብሓቲከ ፡
57 After he had well arranged everything that was appropriate for the church, he deposited all the sacred vessels of it and, having prepared the books for the reading of the teaching, he performed the consecration and diffused the aromatics according to the rule of the apostles, having offered the sacrifice with numerous prayers and with a glorification. Then he prayed and said: "I give you thanks, Father of lights, (for) the light that comes out of your mouth, (you) who have made the earth solid by your power, established the world by your wisdom and stretched out the sky by your intelligence (Jer 10:12); you hear those who supplicate you, you give to those who ask you, you open the kingdom to those who knock. Treasure of help, granary of joy, hear, O Lord, my prayer, (you) who heard the prayer of Moses, your servant, the day of the erection of the Tent; you filled it with the smoke of your glory and your spirit covered (it) in the likeness of a cloud of
