ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘንእዶ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሎ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ወእምየ ፡ ወእ ኅትየኒ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ለአቡየ ፡ ፈቃዶ ። ወአብርሃም ፡ ተመከረ ፡ ወተ ንእደ ፡ በ፲መከራ ፡ ወይስሓቅ ፡ በ ፡ ወያዕቆብኒ ፡ በ፲፪ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ረከብነ ፡ እምኦሪት ፡ ወኩፋሌ ፡ ለውዳሴሆሙ ፡ ዘተዓገሱ ፡ በእምነቶሙ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢረከብነ ፡ ዕመቆ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ ፍጻሜሃ ፡ ለሃይማ ኖቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜጥን ፡ ጻማ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ትእዛዝ ። አላ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ ንዜኑ ፡ እምተረፈ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ወጻማሁ ፡ ለውዳሴሁ ። ከመ ፡ አብው ፡ ተንእደ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ክብረ ፡ አፈድፈደ ፡ በሓዋርያት ፡ ተጸምደ ። በወንጌል ፡ ነገደ ፡ ወኢነገደ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘኢያርብሓ ፡ አሐደ ፡ በፍኖተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ኦደ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ናየድዕ ፡ ዕበያቲሁ ፡ ፳፳ደ ።
and we cannot report (these exercises) each according to its order. Blessed is God whom he celebrated by saying of him: "My father, my mother and my sister" (Jb 17:14), he who did the will of my father! Abraham was tempted and celebrated by ten temptations, Isaac by seven and Jacob by fourteen; we have found this in the Law and the Jubilees to their praise, they who were constant in their faith. As for this father of ours, Madhänina Egzi', we have not found the bottom of him, he who took the perfection of his faith by measuring asceticism above the commandment, but we will narrate a little of the rest of his history and his asceticism for his praise. Like the patriarchs he was celebrated, like the prophets he accumulated glory, to the apostles he joined himself, with the Gospel he traveled, he did not travel toward what was of no profit, he traveled the path of justice. How will we make known his greatness one by one?
ወአሜሃ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ለፊቅጦር ፡ ወለገብረ ፡ ኢየ ሱስ ፡ ላእካኑ ። ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ዳዕሮ ፡ ወአቅማሓ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሴሰይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘየአምሮ ። ማእደሰ ፡ ያመጽኡ ፡ ወያነብሩ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወኢይጥዕም ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ወይሴስሉ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ መጠውምዎ ፡ ይሁብ ፡ ወይከፍል ፡ ለአእሩግ ፡ ለእንግዳ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለድውያን ። ወዘተርፈ ፡ ይኤዝዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰዱ ፡ ለአኃዊሆሙ ። ወእምፍሬ ፡ ዳዕሮሰ ፡ በበ ፡ ሰናብት ፡ ወበበዓላት ፡ ይጥዕም ፡ መጠነሰ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘየአምሮ ፡ ወይትረከብ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይትነከይ ። ወጽዋዐ ፡ ማይኒ ፡ ዘአንበሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ይረክብዋ ፡ ምልእተ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ያስተርእዮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘበልዐ ፡ ወሰትየ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይጥዕም ፡ እምፍሬ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ወማይ ። ከመ ፡ ኢያ እምርዎ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ይጠበቦሙ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ኃፍረታ ፡ ለጽድቅ ፡ ወይትዬዋህ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ርግብ ። ወነበረ ፡ በዘ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ገድል ፡ እስከ ፡ ቋ ዓመት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አእመርዎ ፡ ትሕርምቶ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ዓመ ታተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጌደስ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አኮ ፡ በመብልዕ ።
121 Then our father called in secret Figtor and Gabra Iyasus, his servants, and commanded them to bring sycamore fruits and desert herbs so that he would feed himself without anyone knowing. They would bring and place a table before him: he would taste nothing of it and they would withdraw after having presented (it) to him; he would give and distribute (this food) to the old men, to travelers, to the poor and to the sick. What remained, he commanded them to carry (it) to their brothers. As for the sycamore fruits, every Sunday and during feasts, he would taste them; as for the quantity he ate, no one knew it: (the food) was found not to be diminished; and the cup of water that they had arranged for him, they found it full. But he himself appeared to them as if he had eaten and drunk much while he did not taste the fruit of the tree and the water. So that they would not discover him, he showed himself very prudent toward them, because of the modesty of justice, and made himself for them simple as the dove (Mt 10:16). He remained in such an (ascetic) struggle for thirty years. After his children had known his abstinence, he remained still many years being regenerated by the power of the Holy Spirit, not by food.
ወእምዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ፳ መነኮስ ፡ ዘአንበሮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በፍቅሩ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀተልኩ ፡ ጻፄ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ማኅደርየ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ለባሴ ፡ ትምክሕት ፡ አሔረወ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ማእምረ ፡ ኅቡኣት ፡ ወከመ ፡ ግያዝ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ኤልሳዕ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወከመ ፡ አካን ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ሰራቄ ፡ አስከሬን ፡ ተመሰለ ፡ ወኢያእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወይፈትን ፡ ሕሊናተ ፡ ወይከሥት ፡ ዕሙቃተ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ኅቡእ ፡ ዘይትኀብኦ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአእመረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ ያውደቆ ፡ በትምክሕት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዝሰ ፡ ቀሊል ፡ እምዝ ፡ ዘየአኪ ፡ ኢይርከብከ ።
122 After that came to him a monk whom our father had placed in his love; he said to him: "Give me a penance, for I have killed a moth in my dwelling." This (man) clothed in vainglory made himself virtuous before the knower of secrets, like Gehazi before Elisha the prophet (2K 5:20-27) and like Achan before Joshua (Jos 7:18-26); he was similar to Judas, the thief of the purse (Jn 12:6). He did not know that (our father) saw what (is) in the heart of men, scrutinized thoughts and discovered the depths: nothing hidden was hidden from him, by the Holy Spirit. Our father knew that the Enemy had acted so as to make him fall into vainglory. Our father said to him: "That (is) mild, may what is worse than that not touch you."
ወአመከሮ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስርቅ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወን ዋየ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ወአልባሰ ፡ ክቡራተ ። ወአቡነሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጠረጴዛ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰርቅ ፡
123 The Enemy tempted (the man) again so that he would steal the property of the monks and the property of the church: books and precious garments. As for our father, while he was standing at the (holy) table, he saw the monk in the act of stealing. He did not

ኢገሠጾ ፡ አላ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ሠውሮ ፡ ኢይርአየኒ ፡ ወኢይደንግጽ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ። ወሖረ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ካልአ ፡ አኀዝዎ ፡ በህየ ፡ ወአምጽእዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኢይቤለከኑ ፡ ተዓቀብ ፡ ወአእመርኩ ፡ ብከ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ እምጸላኢ ። አይኑ ፡ የዓቢ ፡ ቀቲለ ፡ ፃፄኑ ፡ ወሚመ ፡ ሠሪቀ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወንዋየ ፡ አኃው ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ፡ የዓቢ ፡ እምኃጢአትከ ፡ ይስረይ ፡ ለነ ፡ ለኩልነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኃጢአተነ ፡ ዑቅ ፡ ተዓቀብ ፡ እምዝ ፡ ዳግመ ፡
reprimand him, but said for him: "O Lord, conceal him; may he not see me and tremble." (The monk) took everything he wanted. He went to another country, he was apprehended there and brought to our father. When our father saw the monk, he said to him: "Did I not tell you: 'Guard yourself', and I knew what would happen to you from the Enemy? Which is greater: killing a moth or stealing the property of the church and the property of the brothers? Only my sin is greater than your sin. May God forgive us, all of us, our sin; watch, guard yourself after that not to fail again."
ኢተአብስ ። ወበዝንቱ ፡ ንዜከር ፡ ምሕረቶ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ታዜከር ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ኦሪተ ፡ ወነቢያተ ፡ ወሓዋርያተ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይስሕብ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ለብፁዓን ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለነቢይ ፡ እዜከር ፡ ምሕረተከ ፡ ዘአመ ፡ ንእስከ ፡ ወፍቅረከኒ ፡ ዘአመ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ዘተለውኮ ፡ ለቅዱሰ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወንኅነኒ ፡ ንዜከር ፡ ምሕረቶ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ያዜክረከ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ አብ ፡ ደኃሪት ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ቀዳሚተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በንእሱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለወ ፡ በቤተ ፡ አቡሁ ።
124 By this we remember the mercy of our father, just as the Gospel remembers the Law, the prophets and the apostles. Likewise the history of the blessed entails, according to what God says to the prophet: I remember your mercy when you were small and also your love when you were great, you who followed the Holy One of Israel (Jr 2:2). We too remember the mercy of our father; here is the last mercy of (this) father reminding you of the first mercy that he had done in his childhood while he was in his father's house
ወተሳነኖ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ፳ ብእሲ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ተስናነ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትባአስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ምክረ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወሓለየ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተጋብእ ፡ አፍሓመ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ያሞርር ፡ መዓቶ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ይዕርብ ፡ ፀሓይ ። ነሥአ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወጽዋዐ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ያእምር ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘለአኮ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በረ ከተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርኅብከ ፡ ዮም ፡ ወጸማእከ ፡ ወአድከምኩከ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ባእስ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ይቤለከአ ፡ አቡከ” ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ኢአምኖ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ተዘ ኪሮ ፡ መዓተ ፡ ወቅንአተ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ። ወአገበሮ ፡ ለኅፃን፤፤ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ መን ፈስ ፡ ወአራኅርሖ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ በልዐ ፡ ወሰትየ ፡ ዘወሀቦ ፡ ተሠውጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ትሕትና ፡ ወይዋሄ ። ወጌሰ ፡ በጽባሕ ፡ ሓዲጎ ፡ ዓመፃ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሰላመ ፡
125 A man engaged in a great dispute with his father. This father of ours, when he saw (the man) entering into conflict with his father, there arose in his heart a design of peace; he conceived a thought of justice: that he would heap coals of fire on the head (Pr 25:22) of this man and that he would cool his anger before the sun set. He took bread and a cup (of water) and went to him, without his father knowing, as if (the latter) had sent him; he said to him: "Take this food, for you have been hungry today, and thirsty, and I have tired you with many words of dissension - your father tells you." But the man did not believe him quickly, remembering the anger and irritation which (were directed) against him. (Our father) constrained the young-minded adolescent, softened him by a word of peace, gave him (the food) and returned to his home. As for the man, after he had eaten and drunk what he had given him, humility and sweetness returned to his heart. He rose early in the morning, having abandoned injustice, and made peace with his father.
ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዕንቄ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ ዘማእከለ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ዘያዋኪ ፡ እምፀሓይ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንከ ፡ እስትንፋሱ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ ዘይፈትን ፡ ከመ ፡ መዛግብት ፡ ከመ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ጽሩይ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ጽንሓሕ ፡ ኅሩይ ። እምንእሱ ፡ ዘገበ ፡ እምብዝኀ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ወእምብዝኀ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ ወተመክሮ ። እፎ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ለበወ ፡ በናእሱ ፡ ምዕዳነ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ዘይብላ ፡ ይቴጌይስ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ኀቢአ ፡ መዓት ፡ በአትሕቶ ። ብእሲ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ዓሪቀ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ አኃው ። ብፁዕ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘየዓቅብ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ሰላመ ። ወቃለ ፡ መንግሥትሂ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቢጾሙ ፡
126 This is our father Madhänina Egzi', pearl in the midst of the abyss, light of God which shines more than the sun; his breath (comes) from heaven, (he is) like the lamp which tests, like a treasure, like pure gold and like a chosen offering. Since his childhood, he accumulated, thanks to the abundance of wisdom, knowledge and, thanks to the abundance of knowledge, suffering and trial. How precisely he meditated in his young age the exhortation of the Scriptures which say: It is better, more than anything, to hide anger by being humble (Pr 16:19). The wise man wants friendship between brothers (Pr 17:9). Happy the man who observes justice (Pr 29:18) and practices peace! (He meditated) also the word of the kingdom: Happy those who practice

እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይሰመዩ ፡ ወይከውኑ ። በዘ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ትምህርት ፡ ገብረ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዘኃረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ እሙ ፡ ወቀደሶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኤርምያስ ።
peace with their neighbor, for they will be called sons of God (Mt 5:9)! With such teaching he did all this; This (is) our father whom God chose from the womb of his mother and consecrated like Jeremiah (Jr 1:5)

127 || He showed the sign of salvation—the cross on his chest and on his back—the day he was born, as a man not born, but transformed by the grace of God who chose him and filled him with the Spirit like John from his mother's womb (Lk 1:15); he was not nourished by his mother's breasts, but by the wisdom of God who can do all things (Jb 42:2). His father and mother trembled when they saw the majesty of God upon him—which it is not possible to describe—just as Lamech trembled at the birth of Noah when the house cast a shadow like the sun and his (= Noah's) eyes shone like the morning star; he went and spoke to Enoch the grandfather. The latter prophesied concerning him (= Noah) that he would be, alone, a sign for the earth and a descendant for the world (1 En 106). Likewise, this father of our father went to his father and narrated the wonder that had taken place at his birth. Seeing (this), the latter prophesied much concerning him and said to him, "Raise him in the fear of God."
አኮ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ዘተነበየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ያነክሩ ፡ ወይትኔበዩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ አበ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ወያዕቆብኒ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ደንገፀ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ኖሎት ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ነገረ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወነገሮ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ተክለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ረከብኩ ፡ ሕፃነ ፡ ዘኢርኢኩ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ወአንተ ፡ ኩኖ ፡ አበ ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ይከውን ፡ አበ ፡ ለብዙ ኃን ። በበይነዝ ፡ ጥይቅና ፡ በጽሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያኃሥሦ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ተክለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወአልበሶ ፡ በከመ ፡ ንቤ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ዜና ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሚጠተ ፡ ደኃሪት ፡ ከመ ፡ ንፈጽማ ፡ ለቀዳሚት ፡ ሚመጠጠን ፡ ግብሩ ፡ ወእስፍንቱ ፡ ጊሩቱ ፡ ዕፁብ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ ዘአመ ፡ ንእሱ ፡ አትሓተ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ወአፈድፈደ ፡ ልቡናሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ በጥበበ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ።
128 || was not the only one who prophesied concerning him, but all those who saw him were astonished and prophesied, saying: "There is no one like this child, who will become a father (of many?), on earth!" Jacob also, the metropolitan, trembled when he saw the sign while (our father) was on the road with many shepherds; he too spoke abundantly concerning him. This metropolitan also spoke to our father Takla Haymanot, saying: "I have met a small child whose equal I have not seen on earth. You, be a father to him, and he himself will be a father to many." Thanks to this information, our father Takla Haymanot arrived, while searching for him, at our father Madhanina Egzi' and clothed him (with the monastic habit), as we have said previously. This story of our father (is) the turning point toward the end, so that we may complete the beginning. How great (was) his action and what his virtue! Astonishing is the knowledge of his wisdom, (to) him who, during his childhood, humbled his thought and increased his intelligence by fighting with the wisdom of his soul and his body!

129 Let us return then to the action of our father. After that, there came to him the power for (the exercise of) the authority of his word: a donkey stopped and refused to move forward when the disciple ordered it (to do so), according to what our father had said to him: "Do not take it out of here, bring it back quickly, at the ninth hour." The man who had received the charge from our father grew very tired striking it to force it out beyond what our father had set for him; he could not make it go out. || marveled at the action of our father, untied

ትግባእ ። ወአተወት ፡ ባሕቲታ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወረድእኒ ፡ አተወ ፡ በሰርክ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ለአኃው ።
(the donkey) which he had loaded and sent it back so that it would return: it returned alone, according to what our father had said; as for the disciple, he returned in the evening and told this to the brothers.
ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ተፈሥሔሑ ፡ በቲሩተ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ አኮ ፡ ባሕ ቲቱ ፡ በተአዝዞቱ ፡ ለአድግ ፡ አላ ፡ በብዕራይኒ ፡ ጠዋይ ፡ ዘይቤሎሙ ፡ ሚጡ ፡ ወንግሩ ፡ ጥውየቶ ፡ ወዘፈቀደ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ወዘተሣየጦሂ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሰብእሰ ፡ ይሠይጥ ፡ እኩየ ፡ በትምይንት ፡ ወአንትሙሰ ፡ ታቀድሙ ፡ ነጊረ ፡ ጥውየቶ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አዘዘነ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወሰ ሚዖ ፡ ዜና ፡ አቡነ ፡ ተሣየጠ ፡ ጠዋየ ፡ ብዕራየ ፡ በተአምኖ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአ ቡነ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተአመኖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወባዕራይኒ ፡ ጠዋይ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ርቱዐ ፡ በዘ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ። ወበዝንቱኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አንከርዎ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሚመጠን ፡ አፍቅሮቱ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዘኢያሠነየ ፡ ለቤቱ ፡ ለባዕዳን ፡ ይገብር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ሶበሰ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ንዋይ ፡ እምአርትዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትገበር ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ለቤቱ ፡ ጠዋየ ፡ ብዕራየ ፡ ወአስተብፅዕዎ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ። ወንሕነኒ ፡ ንትፈሣሕ ፡ ዮም ፡ ከመ ፡ ጣዕዋ ፡ ጽጉበ ፡ ሐሊብ ፡ በሰ ሚዐ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዓይገ ፡ ጥብብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ስቴ ፡ ወይን ፡ ዘይስሕብ ፡ ወእመብልዕ ፡ ዘያጸግብ ፡ ወያጌብር ፡ ለውዳሴሁ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ነቢብ ፡ ዕመቁ ፡ ወኑ ፡ ኢይትረከብ ። ዘንተ ፡ ኅዳጠ ፡ ጸሓፍነ ፡ በቅሩዔተ ፡ ዘይሁብ ፡ ዘቦ ፡ እዝነ ፡ ልብ ፡ ለይዝግብ ። ወበአሐዱ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሠርዖሙ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕየዉ ፡ በግብረ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ቦዘይክሉ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ወቦዘ ይክሉ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ለግብረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወአዕሩግሰ ፡ ዛእለ ፡ ኢይገብሩ ፡ ምን ተኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ወምስኪናን ፡ በእንተ ፡ እቤራት ፡ ወእጓለ ፡ ማውታ ። ወለመበለታትኒ ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ዘአልበሶን ፡ አቡነ ፡ ነበራ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ንጹሓን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትለአካ ፡ ወኢይጌልያ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርስሓ ፡ ሥጋሆን ፡ ወዕደውኒ ፡ ኢይጌልዩ ፡ ከማሆን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርስሑ ፡ አልባሲሆሙ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትዐወቅ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መነበሩ ፡ በአርአያ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ አለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ሐሊብ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ቱስሕት ።
130 Having heard, they rejoiced at the virtue of their father who had acted not only through the obedience of the donkey, but also through a deformed ox, he who had said to them: "Sell (it) and state its deformity; let him who wishes take (it)." The one who bought it was astonished and said to them: "As for man, (usually) he sells the bad (quality) by ruse, but you, you state its (= the ox's) deformity first!" They said to him: "Thus our father Madhanina Egzi' prescribed to us." Having heard the story of our father, (the man) bought the deformed ox, trusting in the prayer of our father. Learning that he had trusted in him, our father blessed him, and the deformed ox became straight for him because he had taken it, (trusting) in the power of the prayer of our father. Because of this also, his children marveled at him, saying: "How great (is) his love for men: he who did not beautify his house, to others he does good! If he had had a love for goods, he would have made the deformed ox straight to work with him at his house." And they celebrated him by saying this. 131 We too, let us rejoice today, like a calf satiated with milk, to hear the life of our father, a lake of wisdom, which carries away like the absorption of wine, which satiates more than food and compels one to praise him; (even) with many words, his depth and his length are not reached. We have written this little that gives profit, let him who has the ear of the heart gather it. 132 One day among the days, our father instituted again for his children that they should live from the work of their hands; some mastered science and some possessed the strength for the work of the saints. As for the old men, (they were) fifty who did nothing outside of prayer—for themselves, for the poor and the needy, for the widows and the orphans. As for the many nuns whom our father had clothed (with the monastic habit), they remained with his pure children by doing service and by not thinking at all of defiling their bodies. The men also, like them, did not think of defiling their garments, but that this power of the prayer of our father might be manifest, and they remained in the likeness of the angels like little children who are without evil and like milk which is without mixture.
እስመ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ መስተቃርነ ፡ ዘያስሕት ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ወያዜሙ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አሰርኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ መስቴማ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምግባሪከ ፡ ፍትው ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያስሕቶሙ ፡ ለደቂቅከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወትከ ። ንሣእ ፡ ለሊከ ፡ ቅንዮ ፡ ወኩንኖ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኢሃለወ ፡ ዝጸላኢ ፡ እምኢዘመወ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አላ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ስሕተት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአስሓቶሙ ፡ ለክልኤ ፡ ፻ መላእክት ፡ ውስተ ፡
133 Indeed, Gabriel the angel chained for him the Adversary who seduces the world and makes the thought fornicate. The angel said to our father: "Behold, I have chained for you Mastema because of your pleasing actions so that he would not seduce your children during all the days of your life. Take him yourself, subdue and punish him; if this enemy did not exist, the whole world would not fornicate, but he is the angel of seduction who seduced the two hundred angels on Ardis through the women of men (1 En 6:6)." Having said that, he


[p. 482] {#po.51.p482}
አርዲስ ፡ በአንስትያ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ሐደጎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽዕረ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይሰምዑ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ድምፀ ፡ ሰናስሊሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ጽሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ሓይወ ፡ በዓለም ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ በድንግልና ፡ ዘሃይማኖት ፡ ወበእ ንቲአሆሙ ፡ ሠርዓ ፡ ይኅርሠ ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ነበረ ፡ በትኅርምቱ ፡ ወኢኀኅደገ ፡ ኢበርሥዕ ፡ ወኢበደዌ ፡ አላ ፡ ይዌስክ ፡ እንተ ፡ ጸብሓት ፡ ገድለ ፡ ወትኅር ምተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘኢለብሰ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወጠግአ ፡ አማዑቲሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ተቀሠፍኩ ፡ ወየብሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ልብየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተረሥዐኒ ፡ በሊዐ ፡ እክል ። እምቃለ ፡ ገዓርየ ፡ ጠግአ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አዕፅ ምትየ ። ወይዜክር ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ቃለ ፡ መድኅን ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ይር ኅቡ ፡ ወይጸምኡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይጸግቡ ። ቦአ ፡ እንተ ፡ ጸባብ ፡ ወመቃዓን ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ወተመከረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርከብ ፡፣ መርኅበ ፡ ተዓገሣ ፡ ለመጽብብ ፡ ወሜመ ፡ ምግባራቲሁ ፡ ትሩፈ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አምላክ ።
(= Mastema) left him in the torment of the earth; all heard the sound of his chains—because of the honor of the prayer of our father—as long as (the latter) lived in the world. 134 Because of this, the children of our father remained in the virginity of the faith and, for them, he instituted that they should feed themselves. But he himself remained in his abstinence, he did not cease either in old age or in sickness, on the contrary he increased every day the (ascetic) combat and the abstinence as if he were not clothed in flesh; his entrails adhered to his belly, according to what David says: I have been struck and my heart has withered like grass, for I have forgotten to eat my bread. By the voice of my groaning, my flesh has adhered to my bones (Ps 102[101]:5-6). He also remembered the word of the Savior who says: Blessed are those who hunger and thirst for the sake of justice, for they shall be satiated (Mt 5:6). He entered through the narrow (Mt 7:13) and constricted gate and was tested there; to find the spacious place, he endured the constricted space and placed his virtuous actions before God.

### Second apparition of Samuel of Waldebba
ወበአሐዱ ፡ እምነ ፡ ጊዜያት ፡ መጽአ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያልብሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወአልበሶ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ ቅድስተ ። ወርእዮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጽሕብዎ ፡ አዝማዲሁ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እስመ ፡ ያጽሕብሣዎ ፡ አዝማዲሁ ፡ ለአቡየ ፡ ምንተ ፡ እግበር ፡ ኦአባ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃ ኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አኮ ፡ በሥምረትከ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ አላ ፡ በሥምረቱ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሊተኒ ፡ አመረኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግበር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ንሥኦ ፡ ለአቡከ ፡ ወሖር ፡ መሀሮ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዚአከ ። ወባረኮ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ ለትውልደ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይባርኮ ፡ ዘልፈ ። እስመ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ ወየአ ምን ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወተነበየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሓዋርያት ፡ ወበረከተ ፡ ትጉ ሃን ፡ መላእክት ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእስከ ፡ ወኩን ፡ ስሙዐ ፡ ዜና ፡ ከመ ፡
135 One (time) among the times, the father of our father Samuel came and (the latter) led him to our father so that he might clothe him with the monastic habit. He clothed him and gave him the holy habit. Seeing that his parents who were near his father were pressing the latter, Samuel said to him (= to Madhanina Egzi'): "My father's parents are pressing him, what should I do, O abba?" Our father Madhanina Egzi' said to him: "It is not by your will that (this) has happened, but by the will of the Holy Spirit. To me, he indicated to do this; take your father, go, instruct him as your own (disciple)." He blessed him with a great blessing that was for the generations of generations, as he always blessed him; indeed he saw—and believed (in it)—by the Holy Spirit the grace that dwelt upon him and he prophesied for him everything that happened to him. He said to him: "May the blessing of the prophets and the apostles and the blessing of the watching angels be upon your head; be renowned like Anthony and Macarius, the luminous stars."
እንጦንስ ፡ ወመቃርስ ፡ ከዋክብት ፡ ብሩሃን ። ወዘንተ ፡ ቡራኬ ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወመርሖ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ሳፍ ። ወረከቦ ፡ ፳ ገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በህየ ፡ ወአእመሮ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምጽ
136 Having received this blessing, (Samuel) went away and the Holy Spirit guided him like a pillar of light and brought him to the land of Saf. A servant of God found our father Samuel there and taught him that his coming (was) the work

of God, for he had seen the brightness of the grace that shone upon his face. He said to him: "Where (are) you from?" He said to him: "From Dabra Bankwal, the monastery of our father Madhanina Egzi'; I have come on his order." This virtuous (man) made known to him the glory of this monastery and said to him: "While I was here, I saw a veil of light surrounding it like a tent." Having heard, our father Samuel marveled at the grace that had been given to his master and knew that because of the justice of the latter this place had been elevated, and he himself (= Samuel) had sprouted there like a beautiful vine that comes out of its vineyard. Our father Samuel said to the servant of God: "Pray for me so that (God) may guide me and teach me what I must do." He said this with the humility that his father had taught him, so that vainglory might not come to him; but he knew the conduct of all as if (it were) oil in glass. When (the man) had prayed for him, he saw him like a pillar of light planted between heaven and earth: it illuminated everything like the sun. He marveled at the greatness of his grace and remained there until (Samuel) went away with the approval of God, where he wanted. Last acts of Madhanina Egzi' as monastic superior
በሥምረተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፈቀደ ። ወእምዝ ፡ በአሓቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አሓቲ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ከመ ፡ የኀውጽ ፡ ድውያነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለባስያነ ፡ ንጽሕ ። ወበጺሖ ፡ ህየ ፡ ከልእዎ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ እስመ ፡ የሓውራ ፡ ምስ ሌሁ ፡ መበለታት ፡ ወኢያእመሩ ፡ ዘጸሐፍነ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መበለታት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ዘያስሕት ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ተአዘዘ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በትሕትና ፡ ልብ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ እሳት ፡ ትኩለ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወወረደ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰብሓት ፡ ወሰጊድ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእአየ ፡ ትሕትናሁ ፡ ወተአዝዞቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ደንገፀ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ቅድመ ፡
137 After that, one day, our father Madhanina Egzi' went to a monastery to visit the sick, with his children clothed in purity. When he had arrived there, (the monks) forbade him the Eucharistic sacrifice, because nuns were going with him and they did not know what we have written previously concerning the nuns—how (Gabriel) had chained for him the angel who seduces. He, our father, obeyed them with humility of heart, went out (of the church) and stood (turned) toward the east; he prayed and saw a pillar of fire planted before him. Then our Lord Jesus Christ—to him be glory and adoration!—descended to him when he saw the humility and obedience of our father; our father trembled and fell before his God.
አምላኩ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ፍቁርየ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ እክፍልከ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ኣስተፍሥሕከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አውፅኡከ ፡ ዝየ ፡ በግፍዕ ። እስመ ፡ ሕሊናሆ ሙሰ ፡ ለእጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ውስተ ፡ እኩይ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወልሳኖሙሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጐሕሉት ፡ ይተሉ ፡ ኢትሕዝን ፡ በዝንቱ ። ይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃ ኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ የአክለኒ ፡ ሊተሰ ፡ ዘረከብኩ ፡ ሞገሰ ፡ በቅድሜከ ፡ ወርኢኩከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይደልወኒ ፡ ወኀወጽከኒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢመጠንየ ፡ ወናዘዝካ ፡ ለልብየ ፡ በቃልከ ፡ ማሕየዊ ፡ ወበምሕረትከ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ክፍሎሙ ፡ ለደቂቅየ ፡ እለ-፡ ተገፍዑ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ወለእሎንቱኒ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ሥረይ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወኢትረሲ ፡ ጌጋየ ፡ በእንቲአየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ርኅራጌከ ፡ ወምሕረትከ ፡ እከፍለከ ፡ ወለደቂቅከ ። ወከፈሎ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሞ ፡ ወለደቂቁኒ ፡ ከፈሎሙ ፡ በኀበ ፡ ሀለዉ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወወ ሀቦ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ እምቀዳሚ ፡ ወዐርገ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ስብሓት ።
138 (The latter) said to him: "Do not be afraid, my beloved, I have come to make you share in my body and to make you rejoice, because they have made you go out from here by injustice. Indeed the thought of men (is turned) toward evil at all times and their tongue goes in pursuit of deception; do not be afflicted by this." Our father Madhanina Egzi' said to him: "It is enough for me to have found grace before you and to have seen you while (this) does not (suit) my merit, and you have consoled my heart by your life-giving word and by your mercy. Only, share (it) with my children who have suffered injustice with me; as for these monks, forgive them and do not impute fault (to them) because of me." Our Lord said to him: "For your meekness and your mercy, I share (it) with you, and with you and your children." And he shared his body and his blood with him, and with his children also he shared (it) where they were. The Lover of men did all this for him, he gave him an abundant grace, which was greater than before, and ascended (to heaven) from him in great glory.

139 A man of God from this monastery saw all this by the spirit and described to his companions the grace of our father, according to what he had seen; he said to them: "You have failed and sinned because you made the man of God go out; he, he received communion from the hand of his God with his children. You, go, submit yourselves and prostrate yourselves before him so that he may ask for pardon for you, for it has been prescribed against you a punishment from God because of him." Having heard, they refused him and the Enemy of good hardened their hearts. As for our father, he forgave them what they had done against him and returned to his monastery in peace. God of the great design and vigorous action, who knew their agitation and the stiffness of their neck from the beginning and the end, sent his anger and punished them, some by death and some by sickness, until those who remained did penance for what they had done.
ርፉ ፡ በዘ ፡ ገብሩ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በአሓቲ ፡ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ከመ ፡ ወድአ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ብድብድ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ወአንበበ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ቡራኬ ፡ ዘሙታን ፡ እስመ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈደፍድ ፡ ጸጋሆሙ ፡ በዝ ንቱ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሑሩ ፡ ኀውጽዎሙ ፡ ወቅብርዎሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ሞቱ ። ወሰ ሚዖሙ ፡ ዘይቤሎሙ ፡ ወረዱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትባደሩ ፡ ወጉልቆሙ ፡ ፳ እሙንቱ ፡ ወበጺሖሙ ፡ ህየ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወርእዮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያወርዱ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ መላእክት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስብሓት ፡ ዘተፀፍረ ፡ በእደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያዐርግዎሙ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ በክብር ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ፍድፉደ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ሞቱ ። ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ወሥልሰኒ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ወሞቱ ፡ እሉኒ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወአውረዱ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወአስተብፀፅዐ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ሞገሰ ፡ ዘተውህ
140 And again, having also learned, one (day) among the days, that a plague had already broken out in the vicinity of his monastery, our father called his children and sent them so that they might be martyrs; he recited over them the prayer of the blessing of the dead, for he had known that their grace would increase with this; he said to them: "Go, visit them and bury those who have died." Having heard what he had said to them, they descended (from the monastery) hurrying in rivalry; they were sixty in number. Arrived there, they all died and were martyrs for the love of men. Having seen angels descending for them a crown of glory, which had been woven by the hand of God, while making them ascend to heaven in honor, our father rejoiced with a great glory and described to his children the abundant grace he had seen for those who had died. He sent (others) a second time and a third time likewise; they all died also and (the angels) descended (crowns) for them as previously. Our father also declared his happiness concerning them, for he had seen the grace that had been given to them for having obeyed his word and for having preferred death to life.

141 See such an abba: while he knew the death of his children, he sent them so that they might earn a reward; just as he himself preferred the treasure that (is) in heaven (Mt 6:20), he preferred for them the painful trial of death: they did not doubt eternal life. May their blessing and the blessing of their father shine upon us in the centuries of centuries, amen.

142 After that, a miracle occurred for our father: when the flour container ran out, he prayed to God so that the blessing might fill (it) and there flowed torrents of pure wheat flour. A child heard them flowing like rain and did not tell anyone, for our father had told them previously: "If you see a miracle, do not tell (it) and do not shout, so that the blessing may not disappear." The child began to sing and say: Blessed is the man who does not walk according to the counsel of the impious (Ps 1:1), up to where it is said: He guided them by day by the cloud (Ps 78[77]:14). flowed and he told our father that the flour container had overflowed, outside and inside, up to the porch of the house. 143 Having heard (this), our father Madhanina Egzi' glorified his God and said: "I bless you and I proclaim your goodness, for you have done for me what has not been done by hand and what man has not labored for, with the treasure of your hidden wisdom, so that I may nourish my children with your superabundant blessing. You have made the bread of life descend by the voice of my prayer, (to me) the sinner—to you be glory and victory!" After that, he prescribed to his children to eat (of it) and to give (of it) to the poor, for these were days of famine in all the land; he satiated many (people) with his blessing from the month of hamle until the wheat harvest. They gave thanks to God to whom (belong) glory and praise in the centuries of centuries, amen.
ንግበር ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተካየደኒ ፡ አመ ፡ ወሀበኒ ፡ ምድረ ። ወገብሩ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ላሐ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይረክባ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ድኅንተ ፡ ወስርጉተ ፡ በሞገስ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ተዝ ካሮ ፡ አመ ፡ ሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ፡ እስከ ፡ አርብዓሁ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ልኡከ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወመነኮሳ ትኒ ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አድባራት ፡ አንጐርጐሩ ፡ ወአንሥኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ማዕበለ ፡ ልሳን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ይገብር ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ልኡከ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘአምጽኡ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አቡነ ፡
144 After that, while he was praying one night, he saw by the Holy Spirit who (was) upon him that King Sayfa Ar'ad was dead; our virtuous father wept alone for him until it was day. When it was morning, he called his children and said to them: "The king who gave me his soul and his body is dead," and he ordered the bell to be rung. All his sons gathered and his children said to him: "O abba, do not act thus, for no good will come to us from the people, they will slander us to the governors and will devastate our land; may it not be as you have said." Our father said to them: "I have trusted in God and I do not fear people, for the days of my life have been for me a sword (against) the Enemy until I arrived at a beautiful old age. Do not you know that we loved each other with human love? For me, I am afflicted for his soul and I will not wait until the king's envoy arrives who will announce the death of the king, according to what you have told me. Do not be afraid, as for you, for God will save us from all evil. Weep for him and pray for him, in favor of his soul; make the preparations so that we may perform his commemoration, according to what was agreed with me when he gave me the land."


[p. 490] {#po.51.p490}
እስመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ዛሕነ ፡ ወመርሰ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዘአንሥኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ማዕበለ ፡ ቃል ። ወእምዝ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ልኡካነ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወነገሩ ፡ ሞቶ ፡ በከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አቡነ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሜጡ ፡ ቃሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ሓመ ይዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙነ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወተ የድዓ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አክሱም ። ወአስተብፅዕዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ወተጋነዩ ፡ ለኃይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወአክበርዎ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወፈርህዎ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወሞገስ ፡ ዘተውህቦ ።
145 They made a great lamentation and our father began to pray until he found the soul of the king safe and adorned; he performed his commemoration from the third day until the fortieth, according to the command of the apostles, before the king's envoy arrived. Having heard (this), all the people and the monks who (were) in the mountains murmured and raised against him the arrow of the tongue saying: "Why does he act thus before the king's envoy has arrived?" They did not bring any evil upon our father, for the
ለዘ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አብ ፡ አይ ፡ አፍ ፡ ወአይ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ይዌድሶ ። ስብሐተ ፡ በአስተንፍሶ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንጽሐ ፡ ዘአልበሶ ፡ አብሖ ፡ ይርአይ ፡ ኅቡኣተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቄድሶ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይክል ፡ ጥይቅና ፡ በዘ ፡ ይኤርሶ ። ኦአባ ፡ ለበረከትከ ፡ ዘልፈ ፡ ነኃሥዎሦ ፡ እመናስወ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኩነነ ፡ መርሶ ፡
power of the prayer of our father had transformed into tranquility and a haven the arrow of the word that they had raised against him. After that, the king's envoys arrived and announced his death, according to what our father had seen. After that, those who had disparaged our father changed their word when they heard that the king's death was effective and that it was announced from Axum. They celebrated our father, paid homage to the power of his prayer and honored him greatly. They feared him because of the gift of grace and favor that had been given to him.
ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ናሁኬ ፡ አይዳዕናክሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጻማሁ ፡ ወገድሎ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ብነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይጌስዉ ። ብፁ ዓን ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙን ፡ ቃሎሙ ፡ ለኅሩያን ፡ ነገሩነ ፡ እሉ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ወኢኅ ብኡ ፡ እምደቂቆሙ ፡ ለካልእ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ሐደጉ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ። ወአቡነሂ ፡ ይበዝሕ ፡ ግብሩ ፡ ዘኢነገሮሙ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ግሩማተ ፡ ወኅቡ ኣተ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ስልጣኑ ፡ ለመለኮት ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እምብዝኅ ፡ ያፈቅሮ ፡ ለቢዎ ፡ ጽሑፈ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ለኃ ቢእ ። ወግብሮሰ ፡ ይከሥትዎ ፡ በክብር ፡ ኅዳጠ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ወአርአዮሙ ፡ እምዕበየ ፡ ጸጋሁ ። ውእቶሙኒ ፡ ሠናያነ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ወሰምዑ ፡ ሓደጉ ፡ ለነ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ዝክረ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ወንሕ ነኒ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ሰማዕነ ፡ ኅዳጠ ፡ ጸሓፍነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይኩን ፡ ሀኬት ፡ ለሰማ ዕያን ።
146 Such a father, what mouth and what tongue will praise him? By breathing glory (into him), God who clothed him in purity allowed him to see hidden things by sanctifying him. He was capable of subtlety in . O abba, we will always seek your blessing; Far from the temptations of the world, be for us a haven In the centuries of centuries.
ወእምዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ አመ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፍልሰቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ወንግደቱ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ባሓ ፡ ፍሥሕ ፡ ምዕረፈ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እምይእዜሰ ፡ በጽሓ ፡ ዕረፍትከ ፡ እምስራኅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ናህይ ፡ እምሓዘን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ እምጻማ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ተምኔታ ፡ ለነፍስከ ። ወናሁ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወላዲቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርገ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሓራ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ በክብር ፡ ወበዕበይ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይተርፍ ፡ ለተቀብሎትከ ። አበው ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ለተቀብ ሎትከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ረኣዬ ፡ ሓቡኣት ፡ ወተነበይከ”፤ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
147 Behold then that we have made known to you all his labor and his (spiritual) combat that he accomplished from his childhood until now, while we have truthful witnesses who do not lie. Blessed (are) the elect, for their word (is) true! These fathers of ours told us and did not hide from their children; to another generation, they left all this. As for our father, multiple is his action, he who did not tell them what God did for him as wonderful things, nor the hidden things that the force of the power of the divinity did for him in the greatness of his love for him, meditating on the writing that says: The mystery of God (is) beautiful to hide. As for his action, let it be revealed with honor (Tb 12:7, 11) he told them little and made them see of the greatness of his grace. As for them whose memory is beautiful, they left us this little among what they saw and heard so that it might be a memory for all; we too, from what we have heard we have written (only) a little so that there might not be weariness for the listeners. Sickness and death of Madhanina Egzi'


[p. 492] {#po.51.p492}
ከማሆሙ ። ወሓዋርያትኒ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ሰባኪሃ ፡ ለጽድቅ ፡ ከማሆሙ ። ሰማዕትኒ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጾርከ ፡ ክበዳ ፡ ለዕለት ፡ በትዕግሥ ቶሙ ፡ ዘፈጸምከ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ከዊነከ” ፡ ከማሆሙ ። ወጻድቃንኒ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ እስመ ፡ አስተጋባእከ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ጻማሆሙ ፡ ወፈጸምከ ፡ ገቢሮታ ። ወማኅበረ ፡ ደናግል ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለበስከ ፡ ንጽሐ ፡ ወቅድስና ፡ ከማሆሙ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ኣዕርጋ ፡ ለሥጋከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ሠላስ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ታዎጎሎስ ፡ ረኣዬ ፡ ኅቡኣት ፡ ወከመ ፡ ኣርኢከ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘኒ ፡ እግዚእየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረጎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኀኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይዜምሩ ፡ ወአመሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኅቡኣተ ፡ ዘውስቴታ ። ወአብጽሖ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ እምድ ኅረ ፡ አርአዮ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ግሩመ ፡ ለለሰማዩ ። ወፈርሃ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ በድንጋፄ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ስብሓተ ፡ እግዚአብሕር ፡ ዘኢይጤየቅ ፡ ለዓይን ፡ ወሱራሔሁ ፡ ይበርቅ ። ወአጽንዖ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወአቅረቦ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ጽሑፈ ፡ በአክናፊሆሙ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግርማ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኢይትነገር ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ሱራፌል ፡ ወኦደ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዓጥን ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ሰርጐ ፡ ቅዳሴሆሙ ። ወሰምዐ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሎ ፡ ተፈሣሕ ፡ ፍቁርየ ፡ ወተሰልጠት ፡ ብከ ፡ ሥምረትየ ። ወናሁ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ፈቃድከ ፡ ድልው ፡ ለከ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ዘአስተዳሎኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ። ወሀሎ ፡ ይፀንሓከ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስብሓት ፡ ዘተፀፍረ ፡ በእዴየ ። ወተወሰኮ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ አእኩተ ፡ ወሰብሖ ፡ ለአምላኩ ፡ በስብሓተ ፡ አርያም ፡ ዘኢይትአመር ፡ ዘተከሥተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በህየ ፡
and you have prophesied by the Holy Spirit like them. The apostles will come, for you have been a preacher of the truth like them. The martyrs will come, for you have borne the burden of the day with their constancy, you who have accomplished martyrdom having been like them. The righteous will come, for you have gathered (the account of) their ascetic combat and you have led this completely. The assembly of virgin monks will come to you, for you have clothed yourself in purity and holiness like them. However come, that I may make your body ascend to heaven like the three prophets and like (John) the Theologian spectator of the mysteries, and so that I may show you all the mysteries, according to what my Lord has commanded me."
ከመ ፡ እለ ፡ ዘከርኖሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰዶ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ተድላ ፡ በትእ ዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ ማኅደሮሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ዘዘ ፡ ዚአሆሙ ፡ መካናቲሆሙ ፡ ወዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ሥነ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡ ወዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ሱራሔ ፡ ጽጌሆሙ ፡ ወአፈወ ፡ መዐዛሆሙ ፡ በበ ፡ ዕበዮሙ ፡ ወክ ብሮሙ ፡ ለቀደምት ፡ ወለደኃርት ፡ እለ ፡ ፈለሱ ። ወዓዲ ፡ አርአዮ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘማእከለ ፡ ኤዶም ፡ ወፈለገ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ፀዓዳ ፡ ዘይሰቅያ ፡ ለገ ነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ አትክልቲሃ ፡ ወኅቡኣቲሃ ፡ አመሮ ። ወድኅረ ፡ አርአዮ ፡ መካነ ፡ ልዕልተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትትሌዓል ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ወሕንጽተ ፡ በአእ ባነ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ ወወርቅ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ግበሪሃ ፡ ዘአዳም ፡ ለተናግሮ ፡ ወዕፁብ ፡ ለነጽሮ ። አመሮ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝሰ ፡ መካነ ፡ ዕረፍትከ ፡ ዘአስተዳ ለወ ፡ ለከ ፡ አብ ፡ እስመ ፡ አፍቀረከ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በሥምረቱ ።
149 After that, he made our father Madhanina Egzi' ascend into the heavenly Jerusalem: he made him see the sons of light, where they were singing and showed him all the hidden things that (were) there. He brought him before the throne of the Trinity after having made him see the fearsome power of each heaven. Our father became afraid and fell into trembling, for he saw the glory of God that is not perceived by the eye, and his splendor was bursting forth. The angel strengthened him, made him approach under the cherubim and made him see his name written on their wings. Our father rejoiced at all the majesty of the greatness of God that cannot be described; he saw where the seraphim stood and went around three times while incensing with them in all the adornment of their sanctification. He heard the voice of the God of hosts saying to him: "Rejoice, my beloved, my good pleasure has been accomplished in you. Behold, your will has been accomplished; it is ready for you the kingdom of heaven that I have prepared for you before the world was created. A crown of glory that has been woven by my hand awaits you." The joy was increased for our father, he gave thanks and glorified his God with the glorification of the heavens that is not known, which had been revealed to him there like (to) those whom we have mentioned.


[p. 494] {#po.51.p494}
ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰዶ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ መካናቲሆሙ ፡ ዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ፃዕሮሙ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ጽራሖሙ ፡ ወብካዮሙ ፡ ወርእዮ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ አንብፆሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደይን ፡ እም ብዝኀ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወመቅሠፍት ፡ በከየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወአንብዐ ፡ በርኅራሔ ፡ ልብ ፡ ዘልማድ ፡ ወበባሕረ ፡ ይዋሄሁ ፡ ዘኢይትረከብ ። ሰአለ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ስእነ ፡ አርምሞ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ብካዮ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አብሓኒ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወፄወውኩ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጉልቀኑ ፡ ወአባእክዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ዕረፍት ፡ በሥምረቱ ፡ ለአምላኪየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አርአዮ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘኢያርአዮ ። ወእመሰ ፡ ጸሓፍነ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እምኢሓደግነ ፡ ንፃእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ጽሒፍ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ ሜጦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወአግብኦ ፡ ግብተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደሩ ።
150 After that, the angel led him into the Garden of delights on the order of God and made him see the dwelling of all those who (were) in Christ, the dwellings of each one, the beauty of the action of each one, the splendor of the flower of each one, the odors of the perfume, each according to his greatness and his glory, of the first and the last who had died. He also made him see the tree of life that (is) in the middle of Eden and the white river of life that irrigates the Garden of joy and all its plants, and he showed him its hidden things. After, he made him see an elevated place that was higher than everything, its construction in precious stones and gold and its ways that (are) pleasant to describe and astonishing to look at. The angel showed him and said to him: "This (is) the place of your rest that the Father has prepared for you, for he has loved you much by his good pleasure."
ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ለክቡራነ ፡ ስም ፡ ወሠናያነ ፡ ዝክር ፡ እለ ፡ ሓደጉ ፡ ሞገሰ ፡ ለውሉዶሙ ። እስመ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ወሀቦሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአበው ፡ ብዑላን ፡ ዘየአክሎሙ ። ወዝውእቱ ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ። አባ ፡ አኖርዮስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ኢየሱስ ። አባ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ። ወአባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ አምላክ ። አባ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚእ ። ወአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዕንቄ ፡ ክብር ፡ ዘገዳመ ፡ ዋሊ ። አባ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ዮሓንስ ። አባ ፡ ታዴዎስ ፡ ወፍሊሙና ፡ ወልዱ ። አባ ፡ መርቆርዮስ ፡ ወኣባ ፡ አሮን ። አባ ፡ ኢዮስያስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ማማስ ። አባ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወአባ ፡ ዮሓ ንስ ። አባ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ወአባ ፡ ማማስ ። አባ ፡ ድምያኖስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። አባ ፡ ንድኃን ፡ በክርስቶስ ። አባ ፡ ያፍቅረነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወአባ ፡ መሓሪ ፡ አብ ። አባ ፡ ሲላስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ እድርያስ ። አባ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ መርቆርዮስ ፡ ካልእ ። ወእሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሥዑላን ፡ እለ ፡ ኢተረስ ዓት ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ጽድቆሙ ፡ ወትቀውም ፡ ጽድቆሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ውሉዶሙ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ፡ ወእሉ ፡ ተርፉሂ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እለ ፡ አስመዩ ፡ ስመ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አቡሆሙ ። ወእሉ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ለነ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ በዘርእዩ ፡ ወሰምዑ ፡ ወበዘ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጽሑፋተ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ለአቡነ ።
151 After that, he led him again into the land of sinners and made him see their dwellings, the torments of each one. Our father hearing the sound of their cries and their weeping and seeing the river of tears of those who (were) in the punishment, because of the greatness of the torture and the torment, our father (therefore) wept and shed tears in the habitual meekness of (his) heart and in the sea of his sweetness that is not found (elsewhere). He solicited and prayed before God, for he could not be silent concerning them. Hearing his voice, the God of mercy saw his tears; our father said to him: "Allow me … then; I have made captive many souls, without number, and I have made them enter into the place of rest by the good pleasure of my God." After that, the angel made him see everything; there is nothing that he did not make him see. If we were to write everything he saw that (is) in heaven, we would not cease from departing into another writing. After that, the angel made him turn and brought him back suddenly to his dwelling.
ወአልቦ ፡ ሓሠተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ማኅደሮ ፡ ለወንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኩሎሙ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ በርሥዓን ፡ ጥሉል ፡ ወጠለ ፡ በቅብእ ፡ ሲበቱ ። ወደወየ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተመንደበ ፡ በገደለ ፡ ጸማ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወይቀውም ፡ ከመ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ ትኩል ፡ እምሴት ፡ እስከ ፡ ይሠርቅ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ወበመዓልትሂ ፡ ከማሁ ። ወነበረ ፡ ጭ ዓመተ ፡ እምአመ ፡መንኩሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸልኣ ፡ ወይገድፋ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ወየዐቅባ ፡ ለሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወይጸውም ፡ ጾመ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ አፈድፊይዶ ፡ ወአልባሰኒ ፡ ኢወለጠ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ወሓይወ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ወላእክት ።
152 Our father Madhanina Egzi' told all this to the (monks) of honored name and beautiful memory who have left grace to their sons; indeed God has given them much honor and (they are) rich fathers whom (their honor) provides well (Si 44:6). Here are their names: abba Anoryos and abba Gabra Iyasus, abba Isayeyas and abba Gabra Amlak, abba Warada Qala Egzi', abba Samuel, the precious stone of Gadama Waali, abba Margos and abba Yohannes, abba Tädewos and Felimuna his son, abba Margoryos and abba Aron, abba Iyosyas and abba Mamas, abba Figtor and abba Yohannes, abba Dan'el and abba Mamas, abba Demyanos and abba Gabra Krestos, abba Nedhan ba-Krestos, abba Yafgerana Egzi' and abba Mahari Ab, abba Silas and abba Edreyas, abba Estifanos and another abba Margoryos. They are the images whose justice has not been forgotten, their justice will last always with their sons; as for his (= Madhanina Egzi's) remaining children, (they are) many who have acquired a beautiful name like their father. These have been witnesses thus for us; with what they saw and heard and with what he told them, (we have constituted) the written activity of our father. There is no lie in their mouth, for they were all the dwelling of the Holy Spirit.


[p. 496] {#po.51.p496}
ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ባረክ ዎሙ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወሙሴ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ለለ ፡ እምሮቤል ፡ እስከ ፡ ብንያም ፡ አመ ፡ ይመውቱ ፡ በትእምርት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትረክቦሙ ።
as Jacob and Moses blessed the children of Israel, one by one, from Reuben to Benjamin when they were dying, with the sign that suited them (Gn 49:1-28; Dt 33).
ከማሆሙ ፡ አቡነሂ ፡ አስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ስምዑኒ ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሞትየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አብሰረኒ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ንዑ ፡ አሀብክሙ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ዘይጌይስ ፡ እምህርካ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትረክበ ክሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዳግም ፡ ልደት ። ባረኮሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ነገር ፡ እንተ ፡ በጽሓቶሙ ፡ ወሀለወት ፡ ዝየ ፡ ታስተርኢ ፡ በረከቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሰማዩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወካዕበ ፡ መዓዶሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ በዘ ፡ ተመሃርክሙ ፡ ብዝ ፡ በአኩቴቱ ፡ ዑቁ ፡ ኢይጊቲጡክሙ ፡ በተጥባበ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘይዬውሁ ፡ ለከንቱ ፡ በትምህርተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ለስሒተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ተጸወኑ ፡ ተቀነዩ ፡ ለተስፋክሙ ፡ ተሳተፉ ፡ ለአፍቅሮ ፡ ነግድ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዕቁበ ፡ ወውሱነ ፡ ግበሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርከቡ ፡ በረከትየ ፡ ወአንቅዕተ ፡ ማያትኒ ፡ ኢይውሕደክሙ ፡ ወድኅረ ፡ ትረክብዎ ፡ ለነገርየ ።
154 Like them, our father gathered his children and said to them: "Listen to me, my children, behold the day of my death has approached: the angel has announced it to me; come, that I may give you a blessing that is worth more than a beautiful booty, which will return to you until the second birth." He blessed them with all the power of the word that had returned to them; his blessing appeared here like the sun in the heaven of God. He exhorted them again and said to them: "According to what you have learned, redouble in praise toward him (= God); watch, that those who deceive in vain with the teaching of men to seduce this world do not deceive you with an artifice of language (Col 2:7-8). Take refuge in God, serve your hope, attach yourselves to loving the stranger (cf. Rm 12:11-13). Do everything that is reserved and determined (1 Co 14:40), so that you may obtain my blessing. The sources of water will not diminish for you and, after, you will obtain (the realization of) my word."
ወእምዝ ፡ ከብደ ፡ ደቄ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወአልጸቀ ፡ ይሙት ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በሰረገላ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ማር ያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ በክልኤ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ተዓይነ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወውሉደ ፡ ምድር ፡ በበ ፡ ሥርዓቶሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጸሓፍነ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በምስካቡ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ፍቁርየ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ተፈሣሕ ፡ ወተሓሰይ ፡ ናሁ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ መርዓከ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጸውዕከ ፡ ለከብካብ ፡ ዘበ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ እፈልሰከ ፡ በስብሓትየ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አስተዳሎኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ማኅደረ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አብሓኒ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ እንብብ ፡ በቅድሜከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ንብብ ፡ ፍቁርየ ፡ እገብር ፡ ለከ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘትቤለኒ ፡ ወአዘከሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ መሓር ፡ ሊተ ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይጹውዑ ፡ ስምየ ፡ ባርክ ፡ ዝሕርየ ፡ ወባርክ ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ ወመኃሮሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ያስተሓምሙ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ተዝካርየ ፡ ወለእለ ፡ ይጐደጐዱ ፡ ዝኅርየ ፡ መሓሮሙ ፡ ለርጐቃን ፡ ወለቅሩባን ፡ ለእለ ፡ ነገዱ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ በተአምኖትከ ፡ ለዘይጽሕፍ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድልየ ፡ ወለዘ ፡ ይሰምዖ ፡ በተአምኖ ።
155 After that, the sickness weighed heavily upon him and he was near to death; our Lord Jesus Christ came to him on the chariot of the seraphim, his mother Mary twice virgin with him, (as well as) the armies of heaven and the sons of the earth, each according to his order, as we have written previously. When our father Madhanina Egzi' saw him, he rejoiced and prostrated himself before him on his bed. Our Lord said to him: "Greetings to you, my beloved! Do not be afraid, rejoice and exult; behold I have come on the day of your marriage to call you to the wedding that (is) in the heavens. I am going to transfer you with my glory to where I have prepared a dwelling for you." Our father said to him: "Allow me, my Lord, to speak before you." (Jesus) said to him: "Speak, my beloved, I will do for you everything that you tell me." (Our) father reminded him of everything and said to him: "Have mercy for me, because of me, on those who will invoke my name; bless my tomb and bless my children. Have mercy on those who are assiduous on the day of my commemoration and on those who will clap their hands before my tomb. Have mercy on those who are far and on those who are near, who will have come on pilgrimage to me having trusted in you, on him who will write the book of my (spiritual) combat and on him who will listen to it with faith."

mercy. He who has been diligent on the day of your commemoration, he who has clothed the naked, he who has satisfied the hungry in your name, he who has received the stranger and visited the sick, (all these,) I will save them, I will show them mercy, and I will give them a reward in my kingdom. 157 "I will cause the water of life to flow for you where your body will have been buried, so that it may be a reward for those who will clap their hands for you; it will not appear to everyone, but (only) to my beloved. To those who will have come on pilgrimage, from far and near, to your tomb, I will give a reward and I will make them rejoice in my kingdom. I will bless your children and I will be merciful to them every day, and I will not let any suffering or temptation approach your tomb, if your children keep what you have told them. Those who will have been tormented either at sea or by suffering, women if they have been tormented in childbirth, the barren who have no fruit, if all solicit me in your name, I will hear them and I will arrive quickly to them to save them because of you. To those who will present as an offering a lamp, incense, and a sacred ornament, I will give double honor and I will gird them with a crown of joy (Sir 6:31)." (Jesus) blessed for him the heavens, the earth, the springs of water, the seeds, and everything that is visible; he blessed him for our father because of his limitless ascetic struggle. Having heard, our father rejoiced, gave thanks to God, told all this to his children, and blessed them.

158 Immediately his soul left the citadel of his body; a pearl, it illuminated the world like the sun. Our Lord made it lie upon his breast (Jn 21:20), he kissed three times the pure soul that had not been defiled since it had been breathed into him (= the body). He made it ascend, on the back of the cherubim, to the heavenly Jerusalem in the thunderous roar of the angels. His death took place on the twenty-eighth of the month of Dios in the Roman calendar and the third of Hedar in the Ge'ez calendar.

159 How great (were) the weeping and the lamentations that came from his children where they were gathered! Indeed, their lamp had disappeared; they shed their tears like the weeping of Israel on the day of Atonement (Lv 23:27) and like the weeping of Rachel on the day when the desire to give birth tormented her (Jer 31[38]:15). The monks who (were) in the monasteries and had come with their censers wept; the priests and deacons in service wept for him; the widows and orphans wept for him; all those who, from far and near, had learned (of the event) wept for our father. He was buried with honor and interred where Our Lord had shown him. Having died, the righteous one left repentance to his children on earth. O Abba, how has your tongue that taught wisdom fallen silent? And your hands that

hastened to give! Behold, you have been set aside (like) the pure gold of Taprobane and the resplendent sun of spring. 160 May the blessing, the gift of grace, and the memory of the covenant of our father Madhanina Egzi' be great upon us all Who have gathered in this asylum Like the bow of the cloud of heaven, Like the dew of mercy of summer, Like the north wind which (is) moderate, Like the smoke of glory that fills the palace of Adonai; They will sprinkle us with the hyssop of the Spirit so that we may be whitened from the stain of fault (Ps 51[50]:9).
