እፎ ፡ አዕበየ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ለአግብርቲሁ ፡ መን ክር ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ። ወአሜሃ ፡ እለ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ቅሩባን ፡ ወእለሂ ፡ ኢርእዩ ፡ ርሑቃን ፡ ደንገፁ ፡ ወፈርሁ ፡ ወአኀዞሙ ፡ ረዓድ ፡ እምትግርምተ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ ወተአምሪሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በእደ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ይትነከር ፡ ይትነከር ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ዓቢያተ ፡ በግብጽ ፡ ወመንክረ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ካም ፡ ወግሩመ ፡ በባሕረ ፡ ኤርትራ ። ዮምኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ለማኅበረ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ቤተ ፡
has God raised the practice of power for his servants! Admirable (is) God by his saints (Ps 68[67]:36)! Then those near who had seen and those far who had not seen trembled and were seized with fear; a shuddering gripped them before the terrifying character of the prodigy and miracle of God that he had performed by the hand of his beloved, our father Madhänina Egzi' renowned and just. They said: "Admirable, admirable (is) God who has done great things in Egypt, prodigies in the land of Ham, and a terrible thing at the Red Sea (Ps 106[105]:21-22)." Today in truth he has done the same for the community of his saints in a place of sojourn of glory, the house of God.
እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ ወርዕደ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘነፍስ ፡ እምት ግርምተ ፡ ኃይላ ፡ ወዕበያ ፡ ለደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ቅድስት ፡ በከመ ፡ ፈርሁ ፡ አእላፈ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ እምገጸ ፡ ግርማሃ ፡ ለደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ዘኮሬብ ፡ እስከ ፡ አዘዙ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እግረ ፡ ደብር ፡ እመኒ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትወገር ፡ በእብን ፡ እምብዝኃ ፡ ፍርሀቶሙ ፡ ኪያሃ ።
213 After this, all flesh and all soul were seized with fear and trembled before the terrifying character of the power and greatness of Dabra Bankol the holy, just as the myriads of Israel were seized with fear of old before the formidable majesty of Mount Sinai which (is) Horeb, to the point that they ordered that (any being who) approached the foot of the mountain—whether man or animal—be stoned, in the greatness of their fear regarding it (Ex 19:10-13).
ወንጉሥሂ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወርቱዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ፋሲለደስ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ወተደመ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ደንገፀሂ ፡ ወፈርሀ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ እመዜንዋን ፡ ዜና ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈደየ ፡ ለደብሩ ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ተ ፡ ፻ሸማ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ አግዘመ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዕፀወ ፡ ጽሕዳቲሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ደብር ፡ በእደ ፡ ሐናጽያን ፡ በኢያእምሮ ። ወካዕበ ፡ አጥፍአ ፡ ጸባሕተ ፡ ተኩስ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ከልአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይብጽሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጐልታቲሃ ፡ ሥዩማን ፡ መኳ ንንት ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ።
214 As for the God-loving and right-believing king Fäsiladas, he marveled and was greatly astonished; he also trembled and was afraid when he heard from messengers the account of the miracle of our father Madhänina Egzi', the great wonder-worker. After this, he compensated the great monastery of the latter with seven hundred togas (chammä) in restitution for what he had previously caused to be felled, by the order of his voice, the junipers of this monastery by the hand of his builders, (being) in ignorance. He also suppressed many levies of… he also forbade the governors in office from ever entering his landed possessions.
ንግባእኬ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አትክልተ ፡ አውልዕ ፡ እለ ፡ አቅደምነ ፡ ዘክሮቶሙ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ንባብነ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ሥርፀቶሙ ፡ ወፀአቶሙ ፡ በኢልማድ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ ጐንደ ፡ ጽሕድ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ዘተገዝመ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ነዮሙ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ዓበይተ ፡ አምጣን ፡ ወግዙፋነ ፡ ራእይ ፡ ወስፉሓነ ፡ አዕፁቅ ፡ ወነዊኃነ ፡ ዓቅም ፡ ከመ ፡ አዕዋመ ፡ ጽሕድ ፡ ዓበይት ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኖሙ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወከደኑ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ደብር ። ወኮኑ ፡ አዕፃዳተ ፡ ሞገስ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወለአብያተ ፡ መነኮሳት ። ወካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ዘያስተርእዩ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ እምርሔሑቅ ፡ ብሔር ፡ በዕበይ ፡ ወልዕልና ፡ ከመ ፡ ዓርዘ ፡ ሊባኖስ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወበዝኩሉ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአ ቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ኪያ ሆሙ ፡ ወዘሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ያነክር ፡ ወትረ ፡ ወይዴመም ፡ ዘልፈ ፡ በህቁ ፡ ወይወርድ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፍርሀት ፡ ወረዓድ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እምዕበየ ፡ ግርማሁ ፡
215 Let us now return again to the wild olive plantations that we mentioned previously on the occasion of our discussion (relating to) the matter of their germination and their unusual growth from the middle of the trunk of the many junipers that had been felled. Behold, now their dimension has become great, their appearance vigorous, their branches extended, and their size tall, like the great junipers that were in their place previously, and they have covered the summit of the mountain (Ps 80[79]:11a). They are areas of grace for the house of God and for the houses of the monks; they are also visible to everyone, from a distant region, by their greatness and height, like the great cedars of Lebanon (Ps 80[79]:11b). With all this, they are the memory of the miracle of our father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just. Whoever has seen them and heard their story always admires and is incessantly extremely amazed; fear and a great trembling descend upon him before the greatness of the formidable majesty of this father of ours, renowned and just, Madhänina Egzi'


[p. 540] {#po.51.p540}
ለዝኩ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዘበኃይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ።
of whom, by the power of God, the miracle of the great power (was manifested) to the whole universe.

### Miracle of the axe at the same period
ወካዕበ ፡ ንነግር ፡ ወንዜኑ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ፳ ብእሲ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘዓርገ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ተአዘዝኩ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ። እጎቲዞ ፡ ሐናጽያነ ፡ አክሱም ፡ ዘዘከርናሆሙ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወዓሪጎ ፡ ህየ ፡ አግዘመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አዕፃዳተ ፡ ጽሕድ ፡ በእደ ፡ እሉ ፡ ሐናጽያን ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አግዘመ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ብሔሮ ፡ ተእኅዘ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ግብተ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽንዓ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ርእየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ራእዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘመፍርህ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወግሩም ፡ በህቁ ። መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ እኀዝ ፡ ማኅፄ ፡ በሊሕ ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ገዘመ ፡ አባለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በክልኤ ። ወነገረ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ወአትለወ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ምሥጢሩ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወእዴሁ ፡ እደ ፡ መዓቱ ፡ ወማሕፄሁ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ፃዕር ፡ ግሩም ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወተገዝሞቱኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ተፈልጦተ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ። ወሰብእሰ ፡ እለ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ወተ ደሙ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወአኀዞሙ ፡ ፍርሀት ፡ ወረዓድ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እምትግርምተ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘበኃይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻ
216 We speak again and narrate the story of the miracle of this glorious father of ours, which he performed upon a man; it is he who had previously gone up to Dabra Bankol saying: "I have received an order from the king." Having taken the builders of Axum that we mentioned previously and having gone up there, he had caused all the juniper plantations to be felled by the hand of these builders. After having caused (them) to be felled, while he was returning to his country, he was suddenly seized by a violent illness in the middle of the road; he fell there and, while he (was) in the violence of the illness, he saw in a vision like a great monk who (was) very frightening and extremely terrible. (He) came to him, a sharp axe held in his hand, and cut the limbs of his body in two. (The man) recounted this to the people and also gave them subsequently the explanation of the mystery of this: the great monk who had come to him (was) our father Madhänina Egzi', his hand, the hand of his anger, and his axe, the illness, terrible pain, which had fallen upon this man; as for the fact that he was cut in two, it was the separation of his soul and his body; and it happened to him according to what he had said. As for the people who had heard this, they marveled and were greatly astonished; fear and a great trembling seized them before the terrifying character of the great miracle—which had taken place by the power of God—of this father of ours, renowned and just, Madhänina Egzi'.
ድቅ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ። ኦዝመንክር ፡ ወዕፁብ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ርኢክሙኑ ፡ ኦሰማዕያን ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንከረቶ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ምድር ፡ በምልኣ ። ወዝኒ ፡ ማኅፄ ፡ መቅሰፍት ፡ አኮ ፡ በዘኃለፈ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ዘይትኤዘዝ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አላ ፡ በእለኒ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ለእመ ፡ ተዓደውዎ ፡ በተሐብሎ ፡ ዓብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ይትኤዘዝ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
217 O this prodigious and very stunning (thing)! Have you seen, listeners, community of a great assembly, the greatness of the miracle of this holy father of ours Madhänina Egzi', which he performed by the power of God his Creator to the point that the entire earth was astonished regarding him? This axe of punishment, it is not only against him who had expired that it was prescribed by God, but also against those who will come, forever, if they offend God by using arrogance toward his churches and toward his saints: it has been likewise prescribed by this living Creator—to whom (is due) glory—unto the ages of ages, amen.
ናሁ ፡ ጸሐፍናሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግሩ ፡ ዜና ፡ ዕበያ ቲሃ ፡ አበው ፡ ለደቂቆሙ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ያእምር ፡ ካልእ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ እለ ፡ ይትወለዱ ፡ ወይትነሥኡ ፡ ወይዜንዉ ፡ ለደቂቆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስዩ ፡ ትውክልቶሙ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወከመ ፡ ኢይርስዑ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ኃይሎሂ ፡ ወመንክሮሂ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግ ዚእ ፡ አበ ፡ ከዋክብት ፡ ነባብያን ፡ ወሊቀ ፡ ድርገታት ፡ አእላፍ ። ጸሎቱ ፡
218 Behold, we have written this miracle of the great power of this father of ours, renowned and just, Madhänina Egzi', so that the fathers might recount to their sons the story of these greatnesses and so that another generation might know, children who will be born, will rise and will narrate to their children so that (the latter) might put their trust in God and so that they might not forget the work of God nor the miracles and the prodigies that he has done for our father Madhänina Egzi', the father of the intelligent stars and the chief of ten thousand


[p. 542] {#po.51.p542}
ወበረከቱ ፡ ወረድኤተ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወምስለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ የማነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ሰማዕያን ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
communities. May his prayer, his blessing, and the help of his God be with our king Däwit, with our father Yamäna Krestos, and with us all, the listeners, his children and his beloved, unto the ages of ages, amen.

### Miraculous intervention of Madhänina Egzi' in favor of Sayfa Ar'äd
ተአምረ ፡ ኀይል ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘመንክር ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወመድምም ፡ በህቁ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ከሀሌ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ለፍቁረ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ አርዓድ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አመ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ደብሩ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ የማነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ሰማዕያን ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወፍቁራኒሁ ፡
219 Miracle of great power which (is) very prodigious and absolutely stunning that our father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just, performed by the power of his omnipotent God for our king dear to his soul Sayfa Ar'äd, son of King Amda Seyon, while he (was) living in Dabra Bankol, his monastery. May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear Yamäna Krestos and with us all, the listeners, his children and his beloved, unto the ages of ages, amen
ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ አርዓድ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ትዕይንቱ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሸዋ ፡ በአስተጋብኦ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ እምጽንፍ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጽንፍ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብዝጥሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኖጥፃ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ዘኢይትኃለቀ፦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሑር ፡ ወይመርድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ርሑቃን ፡ እለ ፡ ይሰመዩ ፡ ሶባ ፡ ኖባ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፅብፆሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ናግራን ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይፀብፅዎሙ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አረማውያን ፡ ለእሉ ። ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ህየ ፡ ወይሜርድ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ አክሱም ፡ ዘቅርብት ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወበህየ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዜና ፡ ትሩፋ ቲሁ ፡ ወሥነ ፡ ጽድቁ ፡ ወዕበየ ፡ መዓርጊሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ዜና ፡ ነደ ፡ ልቡናሁ ፡ በእሳተ ፡ ፍቅሩ ፡ አምላካዊ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወብዙኃ ፡ ጽሕቀ ፡ ተራክቦቶ ፡ ወርእዮቶ ፡ ገጸ ፡ በገጽ ፡ ወተናግሮቶ ፡ አፈ ፡ በአፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስማዕ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ምዕዳን ፡ ወምክር ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ በአፉሁ ፡ ለሐዊረ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ርሑቅ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይትማ
220 During the days of this saint, our king Sayfa Ar'äd rose from the land of his camp, in the land of Shoa, gathering the troops of his kingdom from one border to the (other) border to the point that the number of these was like the sand of the sea which cannot be counted, in order to go and make an incursion into the land of distant enemies who were called Sobä (and) Nobä, to fight them because of the Christian inhabitants of Najran; indeed, these infidels were attacking them. While he was going there and advancing rapidly, he arrived in the land of Axum, near which is Dabra Bankol the holy; there, when the king heard the account of the virtues, of the beauty of the justice, and of the greatness of the dignity of our father Madhänina Egzi' (from the mouth) of storytellers, his mind burned with the fire of a divine love for this saint; he desired ardently to meet him, to see him face to face, and to speak to him head to head to hear from him a word of exhortation and counsel, to be blessed by his mouth, in order to travel a long road, and to entrust himself to his holy prayer in order to obtain power and victory where he would have gone to fight, in the land of distant enemies.
ኅፀን ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርከብ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ወመዊዓ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ለተፃብዖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ርሑቃን ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ለአከ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወአምኃ ፡ ለቅ ድስናከአ ፡ ኦአባአ ፡ ክቡርአ ፡ ባሕቱአ ፡ እስመአ ፡ ብየአ ፡ ጻሕቅአ ፡ ለተራክ ቦአ ፡ ምስሌከአ ፡ ወርእዮተከአ ፡ ገጸ ፡ በገጽአ ፡ ወተናግሮተከአ ፡ አፈ ፡ በአ ፍአ ፡ ከመ ፡ እስማዕአ ፡ እምኅቤከአ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ምዕዳንአ ፡ ወምክርአ ፡ ወከመ ፡ እትመኃፀንአ ፡ በጸሎትከአ ፡ ቅድስትአ ፡ ከመ ፡ እርከብአ ፡ ኃይለአ ፡ ወመዊ ዓአ ፡ በኀበ ፡ ሖርኩአ ፡ ለተፃብፆአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ አፅራርአ ፡ ርሑ ቃን ፡ ወከመ ፡ እትባረክአ" ፡ በአፉከ ፡ ቅዱስአ ፡ ለሐዊረ ፡ ፍኖትአ ፡ ርሑ ቅአ ። ወይእዜኒአ ፡ ኦአባአ ፡ ክቡርአ ፡ እምጻእኑአ ፡ ለልየአ ፡ ኀቤከአ ፡
221 Because of this, he sent him (a message) saying: "Peace and salvation to your holiness, O glorious abbä! However, in fact, I have the desire to meet with you, to see you face to face, and to speak to you head to head to hear from you a word of exhortation and counsel, to entrust myself to your holy prayer in order to obtain power and victory where I will have gone to fight, in the land of distant enemies, and to be blessed by your holy mouth, in order to travel a long road. Now, O glorious abbä, shall I come to you


[p. 544] {#po.51.p544}
ወሚመአ ፡ ትመጽእኑአ ፡ ለሊከአ ፡ ኅቤየአ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሜጠ ፡ አውሥኦተ ፡ መልእክት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ኦአ ፡ ንጉሥአ ፡ ክቡርአ ፡ ምንትኑአ ፡ አነአ ፡ ከመአ ፡ አንተአ ፡ ትምጻእአ ፡ ኀቤየአ ፡ ዳእሙአ ፡ አነአ ፡ እመጽእአ ፡ ኀቤከአ ። ወብሂሎቱሰ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዝው እቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔርኒ ፡ ፍርህዎ ፡ ወለንጉሥኒ ፡ አክብርዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ልማዶሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ፈጽሞ ፡ ትእዛዝ ፡ ወገቢረ ፡ ትሕትና ፡ በውስተ ፡ ትሩፋት ፡ ወተጋድሎ ።
or will you come to me?" Having heard this, our glorious father returned in response a letter to the king saying thus: "O glorious king, what (am) I, that you should come to me? On the contrary, I will come to you." As for the fact that he said this, it is to fulfill what the Scripture says: Fear God and honor the king (1 Pt 2:17). Indeed, such (is) the habit of the just: to execute an order and to practice humility (figure) among the virtues and the (spiritual) combat.
ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ ወጾርዎ ፡ አርድእቲሁ ፡ በዝንግሪር ፡ ከመ ፡ መስብክተ ፡ ወይን ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘአመ ፡ ምሳሐ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፅቡሰ ፡ አዕፅምት ፡ ወኅሉቀ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አነዳሁ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወብዙኃ ፡ ርስዓን ፡ አሜሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ አብጽሕዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐይ መተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እምላፅዕለ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወአምኖ ፡ በከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለአበዊነ ፡ ሊቃውንተ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ እሙራን ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ይትባ ረክ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኢከልአኒ ፡ ጸሎትየ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ተራክቦትየ ፡ ምስ ሌከ ፡ ወነጽሮትየ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ በቲሩተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወብዙኃ ፡ አክበሮ ፡ ወአፍቀሮ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትገሐሥ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ኩሉ ፡
222 After this, his disciples took him and carried him in a zengerir (which is) like a large wine jar that (is used) during the meal of many soldiers, for he had dried-up bones, the body dissolved except for his skin only, and a then advanced old age. When they brought him into the king's tent, the king rose from his seat, prostrated himself before him, and embraced him as Emperor Constantine had done of old for our famous fathers, heads of the council. Immediately the king said to our father: "Blessed be God who has not refused me my prayer, that is to say, that I might meet with you and that I might contemplate your face, in his goodness." And he honored him and loved him much. Then the king ordered that everyone move away from him. When they were both alone, they said there between themselves how and of what sort (was) their existence.

223 After this, the king said to our father Madhänina Egzi': "O my father, behold, in fact I (am) ready to go into the land of the distant enemies of whom I made you aware previously, in my letter that I sent you, that I would fight them because of the Christians of Najran, for they were attacking them and taking them captive. Now, I pray you, speak to me, and show me a counsel: is the fact that I go there profitable and good?"

224 The saint answered and said to him with the voice of hope: "Do not let anxiety enter your mind, O king, nor a word of doubt because of the fact that you are going there, to where you said, you; indeed you will encounter nothing there of what would frighten you, simply say to God: You (are) my refuge and my asylum, my God and my helper, and I trust in you. Indeed he will deliver you from the snare of the fowler and from terrifying things; he will protect you with his sides, you will take shelter under his wings. Justice with the shield will surround you. You will not fear the terror of the night, the arrow that flies by day, the thing that walks in darkness, the calamity and the demon of noon. At your side will fall a thousand, ten thousand at your right, but to you they will not approach; only you will see with your eyes and you will see the retribution of the wicked (Ps 91[90]:2-8). That he will do this for you (is) true: my God—to whom (is due) glory—appeared to me regarding you, saying thus: "Since he has

trusted in me, I will save him; I will protect him, since he knows my name. He calls upon me and I answer him, I am with him in his distress; with long days I will satisfy him and I will make him see my salvation (Ps 91[90]:14-16) to my anointed Sayfa Ar'äd." As I said at first, I say to you a second time again, O king, that nothing bad will happen to you in the land of war. 225 "As for the army of the enemies from there, it will perish and disappear for you rapidly, like smoke, by the power of God—may his name be blessed—; there will remain of it only one man who will recount the story of their loss to each of their cities. As for your army, there will perish of it only one woman; the cause of the death of this one (will) not (be) the spear of the enemy, but the press of the army making its way. Regarding this prophecy of my mouth that I have told you now, so that you might obtain a sign of its fulfillment, you yourself by the voice of the herald, while you make your way toward there, order the people of your army, each one, that they each take a stone from the earth and that they pile (them) together on the side of the road. When you have turned back from the land of war, order them again by the voice of the herald to take each one of the stones that they will have piled up previously; after all of them completely will have taken, there will remain one stone only and one will not find who must take it, for she who will have thrown it previously onto the pile of stones (is) this woman who will have died. Behold then that you will know that day, by the sign of this stone, that no one of your army will have perished, neither by the spear of war nor in the course of the road, except this woman only, according to what I have prophesied for you now.
ዘእንበለ ፡ እንታክቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ባሕቲታ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተነበይኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወዓዲ ፡ እነግረከ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ አፉየ ፡ ኦንጉሥ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጻሕከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ፀብዕ ፡ ትረክቦሙ ፡ ለፀባዒተ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ዘህየ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ጉልቀሞነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡እምፀባዒተ ፡ ኃይልከ ፡ ወእምትግርምተ ፡ ብዝፕሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ትደነግፅ" ፡ አንተ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ጽናዕ ፡ ወኢታጽንዕ ፡ ድን ጋፄ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብከ ። ዳእሙ ፡ አስተብቅዖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ ስሙ ፡ በአዘክሮ ፡ ስምየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ አምላከ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ርድአኒ ፡ ወአድኅኒ ፡ እምእደ ፡ እሉ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ ይወፅኡ ፡ እምነ ፡ ልበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ፅዕድዋን ፡ አንህብት ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ጉጐልሞ ፡ ወመሪራነ ፡ ኅምዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ሕለት ፡ ወአቃርብተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወይነድፍዎሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ አፅራር ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አንህብት ፡ በሕምዘ ፡ አፉ ሆሙ ፡ መሪር ፡ ወየኃልቁ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወኢይተርፍ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፳ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይነግር ፡ ዜና ፡ ኅልቀቶሙ ፡ ለለአህጉራቲሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተነበ
226 "I am going to tell you another prophecy of my mouth, O king: when you arrive in the land of war, you will find the army of the enemies from there in much greater number than the army of your forces; you will then tremble with terror before their number, but be strong and do not reinforce the trembling in your heart. On the contrary, supplicate God—blessed be his name!—with the mention of my name, saying: "God of Madhänina Egzi' my father, help me and save me from the hand of these enemies (Ps 109[108]:26)." Immediately, by the order of the Creator, will come out from the heart of the earth white bees in great number and with venom as bitter as (that of) the beasts of the reeds and the scorpions of the desert. These bees will sting these enemies (and will infect them) with the bitter venom of their mouth; all will perish and, of them, there will remain only one man who will recount the story of their loss to each of their cities, as I have prophesied for you previously."
ይኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ እምቃለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በልቡናሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ይኩነኒ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤለኒ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እምይእዜሰ ፡ ትብጽሐኒ ፡ በረከትከ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ከመ ፡ እሔሑር ፡ ወአርትዕ ፡ ፍኖትየ ፡ በሰ
227 Having heard this prophecy of hope by the voice of our glorious father, the king rejoiced in his mind and said immediately: "O my father, may it happen to me according to what you have told me." After this, the king said to our glorious father: "O my father, may from now your holy blessing touch me so that I may go and direct my path rightly in peace." Our father began immediately to bless him, saying: "May God bless you, be merciful to you


[p. 548] {#po.51.p548}
ላም ። አቡነሂ ፡ አኃዘ ፡ ይባርኮ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይባርከ ፡ ወይሠሃልከ ፡ ወያርኢ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ወሕየው ። ወተባሪኮ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ በዘከመዝ ፡ ቃለ ፡ በረከት ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ወመረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጸብእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ ወይትኃሠይ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ በተ ስፋ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ዘሰምዓ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡር ። ወአቡነሂ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ወበህየ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ ሕያው ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አድኅኖ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወስምዓነ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ንጴጹውዓከ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ወልታ ፡ ወኩናተ ፡ ወተንሥእ ፡ ለረዲኦቱ ፡ ምላሕ ፡ ሰይፈከ ፡ ወእግቶሙ ፡
and show his face upon you, and be safe." Having been completely blessed by such a word of blessing, the king rose from there and made an incursion into the war, rejoicing and exulting much with the army in the hope (brought) by the prophecy that he had heard from the mouth of this glorious father of ours. As for our father Madhänina Egzi', he returned to Dabra Bankol, his monastery, and there he made a supplication for the king before his living Creator, saying: "O Lord, save the king and hear us the day we call upon you; take the shield and the spear and rise to his help; draw your sword and block (Ps 35[34]:2-3) the enemies of our king Sayfa Ar'äd when he goes out and when he returns."
ለጸላእቱ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ አርዓድ ፡ በፀአቱ ፡ ዐበግብአቱ ። ወንጉሥሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ህየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ተዘከረ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ጽዱቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይትኃጐል ፡ ምንትኒ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ትዕይንት ፡ በኅበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ባሕቲታ ፡ እምጻዕቀ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ በው ስተ ፡ ፍኖት ። ወለትእምርተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘይረክቦ ፡ ድኅረ ፡ በዕድሜሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ የአዝዝ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ዓዋዲ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ትዕይንቱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንሥኡ ፡ ፳8፳እብነ ፡ እምነ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወየአልዱ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ አሐተኔ ፡ ለፌ ፡ እምነ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከማሁ ።
228 As for the king, while he was making his way toward there, he remembered the words of our father Madhänina Egzi' that (the latter) had told him previously by the voice of a just prophecy, namely that none of the people of the army would perish in the land where he would have gone, except one woman only, because of the press of the army on the road. As for the sign of this thing, that he would obtain later, in its time, (it was) that he himself would order by the voice of the herald to all the people of his army, each one, that they each take a stone from the earth and pile these together on the side of the road; the king acted thus.

229 After this, he went and directed his path rightly. When he had arrived in the land of war, he found the army of the enemies that he was seeking; it was in much greater number than the army of his forces, according to what this glorious father of ours had said previously by the voice of the prophecy, regarding the multitude of these. He (= the king) then trembled before the terrifying character of the multitude of these enemies, this according to the prophecy of our father. Also according to what this father of ours had counseled him previously, the king then addressed a supplication to God his creator, in trembling, with the mention of the glorious name of our blessed father, saying: "O God of my father Madhänina Egzi', help me and save me now from the multitude of my enemies; do not let me fall today into the hand of these numerous enemies." At the very moment, by the will of God, came out from the heart of the earth white bees in great number and with venom as bitter as (that of) the beasts of the reeds and the scorpions of the desert, according to what (the prophecy of) our glorious father (had announced) previously. After this, these bees stung (and infected) with the bitter venom of their mouth this army of the enemies, so much so that they caused them all to perish by the death of the venom. There remained of them only one man who would recount the story of their loss to each of their cities, according to what (the word of) the prophecy of our glorious father (had announced) previously regarding this matter.
ደመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ትንቢተ" ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ። ወአሜሃ ፡ እስመ ፡ አእመረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በጸጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ድኅነተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ እምእደ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ ወኅል ቀተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አፅራር ። ይቤ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እንዘ ፡
230 Then in truth our father knew by the grace of God the rescue of the head of the king with all his army, out of the hand of the enemies, and the loss of all his enemies. Our father Madhänina Egzi' said, while he was at Dabra


[p. 550] {#po.51.p550}
ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ አእመርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አድኃኖ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ አድኅኖተ ፡ የማኑ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ተሠጥዎ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ መቅደሱ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ፀብዕ ። ርኢክሙኑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኦሰማዕያን ፡ ሕዝብነ ፡ መንክርኬ ፡ ወዕፁብ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በብሔር ፡ ርሑቅ ።
Bankol, his monastery: "Now, I know that the Lord has saved the king by the saving power of his right hand where he heard him, from heaven his sanctuary, the day of the battle." Have you seen this, listeners our people: admirable indeed and very stunning (is) the knowledge of the grace of this glorious father of ours who was in a distant land
ንጉሥሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሠልጠ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዘአቅደመ ፡ ነጊሮቶ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ከመ ፡ ሀለዎ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ዘተገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ወተደመ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ አምጣነሁ ፡ ወመዓርገ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ በኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተአምረ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ ዘበእንተ ፡ ጽድቁ ፡ ወተአምራቲሁ ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አዕበዮ ፡ ወአስተብፅዖ ፡ ወአፍቀሮ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በልቡናሁ ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ ወለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በአንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ዘገ ብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ፀብዕ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ሰብሖ ፡ ወአእኩቶ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ታዓብዮ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወትትሐሠይ ፡ መንፈስየ ፡ በአምላኪየ ፡ ወመድኃኒየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ወመሲሑ ። ናሁ ፡ እምይኤዜሰ ፡ ያስተበፅኡኒ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ዓቢያት ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ስሙ ።
231 As for the king, when he saw that the prophecy of our father Madhänina Egzi' that he had told him previously by the voice of the prophecy had been accomplished and realized with complete accuracy, namely that it would return to him that there occurred, later, all this greatness of power that had been put into action for him, then he marveled and was very astonished at the greatness of his (= Madhänina Egzi') merit, at the degree of his grace before God, at the miracle of his power which (was due) to his justice, and at his many miracles. Because of this, he exalted, celebrated, and loved much in his spirit this father of ours renowned and just; as for God, because of all the greatness of the power that he had put into action for him at the moment of the battle in the land of the enemies, he glorified him and gave him thanks then, saying: "My soul exalts God and my spirit exults in my God and my Savior, for he has seen the suffering of his servant and of his anointed. Behold, from now on all generations will proclaim me blessed, for he has done for me great things and holy is his name (Lk 1:46-49)."

232 Then all the people of Najran celebrated the king and called him savior of their city, for he had annihilated for them all their enemies who were always attacking them and making them captive incessantly. After this, having commended himself to these Christian people of Najran, the king turned back with his army toward his kingdom. While he was returning, when he arrived at the stones that he had caused to be piled up previously because of the sign of which our father Madhänina Egzi' had spoken to him, he ordered himself again, by the voice of the herald as before, that all the people of his army, each one, take (his) stone one by one. After all of them, completely, had taken it, there remained only one stone; there was not found (anyone) who took (it), for she was dead, the only (victim), the woman who had thrown it previously with all the people of the army who had piled up the stones. By this sign, our king Sayfa Ar'äd knew that no one of the people of his army had perished, except this only woman who had died on the road because of the press of the army. At this accuracy of the prophecy of this glorious father of ours, and (at) his truthful words, the king was astonished in his mind and was very astonished. As for the moment of the death of this woman, it is not known to us—to recall (it)—and it is not found in the account; I do not know if (it was) while the king was going into the land of war or if (it was) while he was returning from there—God alone knows (it).


[p. 552] {#po.51.p552}

233 After this, while the king (was) still on the road far away, when the news of his return with his army—thousands (of men)—was heard in joy and in peace, having defeated and dominated the army of strong enemies, important in number and from a distant land, then joy filled the mouth of the whole universe and the tongue of all the people, far and near, exulted—especially before our father Madhänina Egzi' and before all his children, the community of holy monks who had been with him in great supplication and in making many prayers for our king Sayfa Ar'äd so that the Lord might give him power and victory at the moment of the battle in the land of the enemies and so that he might bring him back from there with his army in joy and in peace. Indeed he (= the king) had told them previously, while he was commending himself to them and was being blessed by them to go into the land of war: "If God gives me power and victory by your prayer and if he brings me back from there with my army in joy and in peace, I will be a son to you and I will make your monastery much greater than before."
ለክሙ ፡ ወአዓብያ ፡ ለደብርክሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምቀዳሚ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ዓዲሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ አቅደመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፈንዎ ፡ መል እክት ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወኅበ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ማኅ በረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ብስራት ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይመ ጽእ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ምስለ ፡ መኳንንቲሁ ፡ ወመሳፍንቲሁ ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ትዕይንቱ ፡ አእላፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአይ ፡ ደብ ሮሙ ፡ ወገጸ ፡ ዚአሆሙ ፡ አምጣነ ፡ ተነድፈ ፡ በሐፀ ፡ ፍቅሮሙ ፡ ወፍቅረ ፡ ደብሮሙ ፡ ልዕልት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መዊአ ፡ ወዕበየ ፡ ኃይል ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ፀብዕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ምሉኣነ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ወብዙኃነ ፡ ጐልቀኑ ፡ ወርሑቃነ ፡ ብሔር ፡ በኃይለ ፡ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወብዝኃ ፡ ቀኖናሆሙ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ።
234 While he (was) still on the road, the king sent ahead a message to our father Madhänina Egzi' and to all his children, the community of holy monks, with the words of a great happy news, namely that he himself was coming to them at Dabra Bankol with his governors, his dignitaries, and the people of his army—thousands (of men)—to see their monastery and their face, insofar as he had been struck by the arrow of love for them and of love for their eminent monastery—because of the fact that God had given him victory and the greatness of power at the moment of the battle in the land of enemies full of power, important in number and from a distant land, by the power of their prayer and the greatness of their supplication in his favor.
ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ተሀውኩ ፡ ወኀዘኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ በእንተ ፡ መጽንሑ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወለሠራዊቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ ብሑእ ። ወአቡነሰ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ተሀውኮቶሙ ፡ ወኅ ዘነ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መጽንሕ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ አስኩ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ሚመጠን ፡ ብክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አልብነ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ ብሑእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ልሕኩት ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለምንትኑ ፡ ተኀዝኑ ፡ ወትቴክዙ ፡ በልብክሙ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ ለመጽንሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ኢተአምሩኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ባረከ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በእደ ፡ ኤል ያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ለአሐቲ ፡ መበለት ፡ ዘሰራጵታ ፡ ኅዳጠ ፡ ሐሪጸ ፡ ድራር ፡ ዘአሐቲ ፡ ዕለታ ፡ እስከ ፡ መልአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቤታ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ በላዕለ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ አአላፍ ፡ በጌጋየ ፡ አክአብ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሰማርያ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተአፅወ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ዝናመ ፡ ሰማይ ፡
235 Having heard this message of the king's arrival at their place, the community of monks was agitated and greatly afflicted because of the reception of the king and his army; in fact, they had only a little leaven in the house. As for our father Madhänina Egzi', when he saw their agitation and the affliction of their hearts regarding the reception, he said to them: "See then how much you have in the house." They said to him: "We have only a little leaven in a clay vessel." He said to them: "Why are you afflicted and saddened in your heart because of this little for the reception of the king and his army? Do you not know that God once blessed, by the hand of Elijah the prophet, for a widow of Zarephath, the little flour of her one-day meal, to the point that he filled all the vessels of her house in a single moment, while there was then a great famine over Israel—thousands (of deaths)—because of the fault of Ahab, king of Samaria; in fact, at that time, the rain of heaven had been prevented from falling there on the earth for three years and six months (1 Kgs 17:1, 10-12; Lk 4:25). And do you not know that, in days even later, he blessed and satisfied myriads in the desert


[p. 554] {#po.51.p554}
፻ጀተ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ወ፪5 አውረኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይዝንም ፡ ህየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ። ወበደኃሪ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኢተአምሩኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ወአጽገበ ፡ አእ ላፈ ፡ በገዳም ፡ እምኅዳጥ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወኅዳጥ ፡ አሣት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ አምላክነ ፡ አመ ፡ አስተርእዮቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘእማርያም ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ለአድኅኖ ፡ ትዝምደ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኢታብዝሩ ፡ ተሀውኮ ፡ ወኢትኅዝኑ ፡ ደዩ ፡ እዴክሙ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ወተገበሩ ፡ ዘብክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤት ፡ ለመጽንሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዘአሜሃ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ይባርክ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ወዘአሜሃ ፡ አሥመረ ፡ ያሥምር ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ዮም ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ቀዳሚኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ዘይገ
with a little bread and a little fish, he, the Lord our God, during his manifestation in the world, in the holy flesh that (comes) from Mary the holy, to save the human race (Mt 14:13-21//). Now, do not increase the agitation and do not be afflicted, simply put your hands (to work) and prepare what you have in the house for the reception of the king and his army; in fact, may he who blessed then bless for you now, may he who was pleasing then be pleasing to you today; is God not first? Now, it is God alone who does everything by the power of his might."
ብር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ በሥልጣነ ፡ ኃይሉ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሰሚዖሙ ፡ እምቃለ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ኀድኡ ፡ እምተሀውኮ ቶሙ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ስእኑ ፡ አግምሮቶ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጌበሩ ፡ ለበረከተ ፡ ቤቶሙ ፡ ዘእምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበአስተብቀ'ዖቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ክቡር ። ንግባእኬ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ፡ ነገር ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ አርዓድ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ ወሰብአ ፡ ትዕይንቱ ፡ አእላፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወበጺሖ ፡ ህየ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወተአምፕ ፡ በተጋንዮ' ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ማ ኅበረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ተአምጥሙ ፡ በአምኃ ፡ ቅድሳት ። ወእሙንቱሂ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተአምኅዎ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ በአምኃ ፡ ፍቅር ፡ ወሰላም ፡ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሐዊሳ ፡ ለምጽአትከ ፡ ኦንጉሥነ ፡ ወፍቁረ ፡ ነፍ ስነ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ስብ ሐት ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዘአጽንዓኒ ፡ ወአብጽሐኒ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወበዳኅና ፡
236 Having heard this from the word of the mouth of our father, the community of monks was calmed regarding its agitation and they did as he had prescribed for them. After that, it was for them as it was, according to what our father had told them, to the point that they could not then enclose, (while) working (them), the blessed goods of their house which (had come) from God through the prayer and supplication of our glorious father. 237 Let us return then to the previous topic: after that, our king Sayfa Ar'äd went up with his soldiers and the people of his army—thousands (of men)—to the holy Dabra Bankol. Having arrived there, he entered to our father Madhänina Egzi', prostrated himself before him, and embraced him with great gratitude; he kissed all his children, the community of holy monks, with a holy kiss. They too, in their turn, kissed him likewise with a kiss of love and peace and rejoiced greatly over him, saying: "Welcome, O our king dear to our soul!" He too, in his turn, rejoiced likewise over them, saying: "Glory to God who gave me strength and made me arrive in peace and safe until this hour of your love, O my holy fathers!"
እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ፍቅርክሙ ፡ ኦአበውየ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተስእሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ሖረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ፀብዓ ፡ ኪያሆሙ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እመከራ ፡ ወእምግባረ ፡ ሁከት ፡ ወኀዘነ ፡ ልብ ፡ አው ፡ በሰላምኑ ፡ ወበዳኅና ፡ ወበጥዒና ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሥምረተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ በኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፍና ፡ ብጽሐቱ ። ወምስለ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አትለወ ፡ ተስእለቶ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ትንቢታቲሁ ፡ ወትእምርታተ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ በአእምሮ ፡ ወበለብዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘተነበየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወዘወሀቦ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ነገር ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግብር ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ዘሀለዎ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወመረደ ፡ ብሔረ ፣ ፀብዕ ፡ ወቀትል ። ተስእሎቱሰ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አኮ ፡ በኢያእምሮቱ ፡ አላ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የአምር ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘኢያእመረ ፡ ለአጽድቆ ፡ ነገር ፡ በአፈ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ክቡር ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ማኅበረ'' ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ከመ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ይሰባሕ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ዓቢይ ።
238 After that, our father Madhänina Egzi' asked him before the whole community of monks how he had gone into the land of the enemies, how he had fought them, and how he had returned from there; had any trial, cause of trouble, or affliction of heart happened to him, or had he accomplished in peace, safe and sound, all the desire of his heart on all the roads of his coming? With that, he continued to question him about the accuracy of his prophecies and the signs (given) by his word; in fact, it was through the knowledge and understanding of God that this glorious father of ours had prophesied for the king, and the oral sign that he had given him beforehand (was) for the beautiful event that was to happen to him where he would have gone and broken into the land of war and battle. All these questions of our father were not (asked) out of ignorance, but, while he knew, he spoke as if he did not know in order to show the accuracy of (his) words by the mouth of the glorious king before the community of the saints so that, through this, God would be glorified in the midst of a great assembly.


[p. 556] {#po.51.p556}

239 Let us return then to the previous topic: after that, our king Sayfa Ar'äd delivered a response to the questions of our father Madhänina Egzi' and recounted that neither trial nor cause of trouble and affliction of heart had happened to him—only salvation and peace—neither in going into the land of the enemies, nor where he had fought them, nor in returning from there. As for the prophecies and oral signs of our father, the king spoke again and made it known that there was nothing that had been false and that had not been realized truly and truthfully among all that—on all the paths of the king's coming. Having heard this, our father and the whole community of the saints stretched out their hands and glorified their Lord, together with the king, saying: "We glorify God the glorified who has been glorified, to him belongs glory in Zion (Ps 65[64]:2) because of the greatness of his power and the fullness of his goodness that he has put into practice for men." The king remained there three days and three nights speaking of the greatness of God and his benefits with our father Madhänina Egzi' and the whole community of the saints; being blessed by them, commending himself to them, and being comforted by their word, he rejoiced and exulted with his soldiers and the people of his army—thousands (of men)—while the saints presented him with blessed goods from their house, as a reception, without them lacking anything.
ቅዱሳን ፡ እምበረከተ ፡ ቤቶሙ ፡ መጽንሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘየኃጥኡ ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መዋዕላተ ፡ የዓውድ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አዕዋ ዳቲሃ ፡ ለደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ወምዕረ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ መራህብቲሃ ፡ ይበ ውእ ፡ ወይወፅእ ፡ ምዕረ ፡ መንገለ ፡ አፍአሃ ፡ ወምዕረ ፡ ውሣጤ ፡ መራህብ ቲሃ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢጸግበ ፡ እምራአእየ ፡ ሥና ፡ ወሥነ ፡ አዕፃዳቲሃ ፡ ወመዓዛ ፡ ረድኤታ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ደብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዓባይ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አመ ፡ ሣል ስት ፡ ዕለት ፡ እምአመ ፡ ተዓየነ ፡ ህየ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ አርዓድ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒኒ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መኳንንቲሁ ፡ ወመሳፍን ቲሁ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ተወለድኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ወለደብርከ ፡ እምይእዜ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሞትየ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ወሀብኩከ ፡ ይኩንከ ፡ ጐልተ ፡ እምአኅጉራተ ፡ ራህባ ፡ ለም ድረ ፡ ትግሬ ፡ ዘርእየት ፡ ዓይንከ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ ቆምከ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ልዕልናሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ደብርከ ፡ ልዕልተ ፡ አምጣን ፡ አምጣነ ፡ ኮንከኒ ፡ አበ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ወተራዳእከ ፡ ኪያየ ፡ በኀበ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ፀብዕ፡ እስከ ፡ ሞእኩ ፡ ኪያየ ፡ ወአጥፋእኩ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ፀባዒተ ፡ አፅራር ፡ ዘህየ ፡ በስእለትከ ፡ ወበአስተብዮ'ዖትከ ፡ በእንቲአየ" ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበዝየኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ፍዳ ፡ ገበርከ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወለሠራዊትየ ፡ ብዝኃ ፡ ቲሩት ፡
240 Then the king also spent days traversing the possessions of Dabra Bankol; once he walked in the midst of its spaces, he entered and exited once outside and once inside its spaces. In fact, he did not tire of the spectacle of its beauty, the beauty of its possessions, and the fragrance of the help of this great monastery of God. After that, on the third day since he had pitched his tents there, our king Sayfa Ar'äd said to our father Madhänina Egzi' before all his governors and dignitaries: "O my father, I have become a son to you and to your monastery, from now until the day of my death; behold, I have given you, so that it might be a fief for you, from among the regions of the surface of the land of Tigray, (what) your eye will have seen from where you stand on top of this mountain of yours with the high summit (?), in proportion to how you have been a spiritual father to me and have come to my aid where I went before, in the land of war, so that I overcame myself and annihilated the entire army of the enemies from there through your request and your supplication for me to God. And here again now, you have made compensation for me and for my army with an abundance of good deeds and goods."
ወሠናያት ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ኦመሲሐ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ክቡር ፡ ኪያየኑ ፡ ታነግሥ ፡ ሥዒረከ ፡ ርእሰከ ፡ መሲሓዌ ፡ ዘመጠነዝ ፡ ምኩናነ ፡ ትሁበኒ ፡ ይእዜ ። ኅድግሰ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሐተኒ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ መካነ ፡ ምዕራፍ ፡ ኢየኃሥሥ ፡ ጐጉልተ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ መነኮስ ፡
241 Having heard this, our father Madhänina Egzi' said to the king: "O glorious anointed of God, do you make me a king, having resigned yourself, (you) the anointed, that you give me now such a government? Leave all that, therefore, (I desire) only one thing in truth: a place of rest. I do not seek a fief, am I not


[p. 558] {#po.51.p558}
አነ ፡ ዘትርጓሜ ፡ ስምየ ፡ ምውት ። መጠነዝሰ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ለእግዚ እናከ ፡ ሊተሰ ፡ ለመነኮስ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ የአክለኒ ፡ አፀደ ፡ ምኔትየ ፡ ዘሀሎኩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርድእትየ ፡ መነኮሳት ።
a monk, (I) whose name in translation is 'dead (to the world)'? Such a government suits your lordship; for me, a solitary monk, the enclosure of my monastery in which I am with my monk disciples is sufficient."
ወንጉሥሰ ፡ ሰአሎ ፡ ወአስተብቀ'ዖ ፡ በአገብሮ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሆ ፡ ይበሎ ፡ ወይትወከፍ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ቲሩቱ ፡ አቡነሰ ፡ እመኒ ፡ አገበሮ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ኢሠምረ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወኢፈቀደ ፡ በልቡናሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጥብዓተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወኢብሂሎቶ ፡ እሆ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ኢፈቀድኮ ፡ ለራኅበ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ ዘወሀብኩከ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ በለኒ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅር ፡ አሀብከ ፡ ጉልተ ፡ በአጽብቦ ፡ እምጽንፈ ፡ መረብ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምድረ ፡ እገሌ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እለ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ እምድኅሬከ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መነኮ ሳት ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኖሙ ፡ ንብረተ ፡ ወሊተኒ ፡ ይኩነኒ ፡ ዝክረ ፡ ሩት ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ኦአቡየ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይእዜ ፡ መዋ ዕል ፡ ዕብሬተ ፡ ዘመን ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ድኅረ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ቦጊዜ ፡ ዘይትፈቀድ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ጐልተ ፡ አህጉራት ፡ ወአዕፃዳት ፡ በኀበ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ድኅረ ፡ ዘመን ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ በለኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጻሕቀ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ወበ እንተ ፡ ንብረቶሙ ፡ ለደኃርተ ፡ ዘመን ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ መነኮሳት ። አቡነሰ ፡ ዘን ተኒ ፡ ኢሠምረ ፡ ወኢፈቀደ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ በግብር ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሥምረተ ፡ ልብ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አፍቅሮቱ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ድካመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወተውላጠ ፡ ዘመን ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ድኅረ ፡
242 The king prayed and begged him with much insistence so that he would say yes to him and accept for himself the gift (befitting) his virtue. But our father, although he pressed him much, did not consent to that nor did he want it in his thought. When the king saw his decision of heart and that he was not saying yes, he said to our glorious father: "O my father, if you do not want the extent of government that I have given you, say yes to me now out of love, that I may give you a fief by limiting it from the border of the Marab to such and such land, in favor of the community of monks, your children who will come after you, so that it may be a good for them and that, for me, it may be a souvenir of liberality forever. O my father, will the sequence of time that will come after be like the days of now? Will there not be a moment when a fief of cities and domains will be necessary for the monks who will come after in time? Now, say yes to me because of the desire of my heart and because of the situation of your children, the monks of the later time." Our father did not consent to that either and did not want it, but by force, while he did not have the consent of his heart, he then said yes to the king out of affection for him and because of human weakness and the change of time that would come after; in fact, our father knew that it would be so.

243 After that, the king rejoiced greatly regarding our father Madhänina Egzi' because he had said yes. This fief, a gift from the king, is guaranteed and confirmed until today at Dabra Bankol. Then also all the royal governors of the land of Tigray, when they saw that their lord the king was guaranteeing and confirming a fief to our father Madhänina Egzi', prayed and begged the latter much so that he would also say yes to them and accept from each of them each time a fief in each of their lands and in each of their governments so that it would be for them the sign of (their) condition as sons in relation to him and so that they would receive a blessing from him. Seeing the decision of their heart, the beauty of their justice, and their love for him, our glorious father accepted the gift of the vow of these governors and, there, gave them a complete blessing. This gift from these governors is a fief guaranteed and confirmed at Dabra Bankol in each of the lands and in each of the governments of theirs until today.

244 After that, our king Sayfa Ar'äd said again to our father Madhänina Egzi' and to the whole community of the saints of Dabra Bankol: "I have been given to you as a son from today until the day of my death; so that you may know my condition as a son, behold, I am going to plow the fields of your community, like a plowman, before you." Then he girded his

በቅድሜክሙ ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ቀነተ ፡ ሐቃሁ ፡ ወአስተሠነየ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ማኅ ረስ ፡ በሐብለ ፡ ሐሪር ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ጸመደ ፡ አባዕረ ፡ ወዘርአ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገራህት ፡ ከመ ፡ እክል ፡ ለዜና ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ዕርፈ ፡ ወሐረሰ ፡ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ወብእሲቱኒ ፡ አስተናጽሐት ፡ ገልጐሎ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ገራህት ፡ በእዴሃ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ጐለተ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በርእሰ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ከመ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ጠሊ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ዳእረካ ፡ ለመባአ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡
loins, adorned all the plowing instruments with a silk rope, put oxen under the yoke with it, sowed gold in the fields like wheat—so that the generations would recount (it)—, seized the plow, and plowed before them. As for his wife, she removed the weeds (?) from the fields with her hand. This king also gave them as a fief, with a lion's head like a goat's head (7), the land of Dä'rakä, as a present to the house of God, for this (was) a land for wheat and a flourishing vineyard.

245 After that, having been blessed by them and having taken leave of them, the king went with his army into the land of Shewa; in fact, this (was) the region of his (permanent) camp. After he died at his hour, the people of Dabra Bankol held a feast for him with hymns and chants and by proceeding to a great commemoration on the fifteenth of the month of Genbot on which he had died—until today. May the prayer and blessing of these beloved ones, our father Madhänina Egzi' and our king Sayfa Ar'äd, be with us all, the listeners, their children and their beloved ones, in the centuries of centuries, amen. Other miracles 246 Miracles of our father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just. May his prayer and his blessing be with our king Däwit, with our father Yamäna Krestos, and with us all, the listeners, his children and his beloved ones, in the centuries of centuries, amen.


[p. 562] {#po.51.p562}
ወበ ፳ እመዋዕል ፡ መጽአ ፡ 8ዕውር ፡ እምነ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይመርሆ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ዓሪገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ለተማኅፅኖ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብርህ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዑረተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግረ ፡ ደብር ፡ ኅደጎ ፡ ህየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይመርሆ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እስመ ፡ አምከሮ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዕውር ፡ ህየ ፡ ግዱፈ ፡ ሠሉሰ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወሠሉሰ ፡ ለያልየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸርሕ ፡ ወይብል ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ቲሩትከ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዑረተ ፡ አዕይንትየ” ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ይትከሀለከ ፡ በዘአክህለከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተከሥታ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ይባቤ ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ አእኩትዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በዘወሀቦ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡
247 One (day) among the days, a blind man came from a (distant?) land, with a man who guided him, wanting to go up to the holy Dabra Bankol to entrust himself to the tomb of our father Madhänina Egzi' so that (the latter) would give light to the blindness of his eyes. When he arrived at the foot of the mountain, the one who was guiding him abandoned him there and went where he wanted, for Satan had persuaded him (to do so). After that, the blind man remained there abandoned for three days and three nights, crying out and saying to our father Madhänina Egzi': "O abbä, may your goodness manifest itself upon me regarding the blindness of my eyes; in fact, everything is possible for you, according to what God—glory to him!—has given you the power to do." After that, his eyes opened: he saw and went up the mountain, pushing out exclamations of joy. Having seen (this), the community of monks and all the people praised God for what he had given our father Madhänina Egzi' to perform miracles and wonders.
እግዚእ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ። ወዓዲ ፡ በ ፳ እመዋዕል ፡ መጽአ ፡ ሰራቂ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወሰረቀ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ እምቤተ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ሰራቂ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘህየ ፡ ኀዘኑ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ኀዘነ ፡ ወእምብዝኃ ፡ ኀዘኖሙ ፡ ቦኡ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ሱባኤ ፡ ወጸርሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በስመ ፡ አቡነ ፡መድኃኒነ ፡
248 And again, one (day) among the days, a thief came, went up to Dabra Bankol by night, and stole all the sacred vessels in the house of God. Having seen what had happened on the part of the thief, the assembly of monks from there was afflicted with a great affliction; because of the greatness of their affliction, they entered into a supplication of a week. They cried out to God in the name
እግዚእ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርእዮሙ ፡ ተረክቦተ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቅድሳት ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ሰራቂ ፡ አውሪዶ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እግረ ፡ ደብር ፡ አንበሮ ፡ ለንዋየ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምኅባእ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ፍኖቶ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይነሥኦ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ እምህየ ፡ በግብአቱ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ሖረ ። ወበተፍጻሜተ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ሱባኤሆሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ኮነ ፡ መኃትወ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወአሜሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምበሐውርት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ በዓል ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለ፳ ብእሲ ፡ እምኔ ሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ በኅበ ፡ ከበቶ ፡ ሰራቂ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጥቃ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአርአዮሙ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ለአብያጺሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወያነክሩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተአምር ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ደብር ፡ ተቀበልዎ ፡ በዝማሬ ፡ ወበማኅሌት ፡ እስከ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ወአብእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወምስለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ የማነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
of our father Madhänina Egzi', his beloved, so that he would show them where these sacred vessels were. As for the thief, having brought the sacred vessels down to the foot of the mountain, he deposited (them) in a hiding place and went his way, until he would take them back from there upon his return from where he would have gone. At the end of the week-long supplication of the monks of the monastery, it was the eve of the feast of our father Madhänina Egzi'; then, many people coming from the (surrounding) lands to Dabra Bankol to celebrate the feast, the sacred vessels were discovered by a man among them, where the thief had hidden them—for they were beside the road—, and he showed them to his companions. After that, they took them with them, rejoicing and marveling at the miracle that our father Madhänina Egzi' had performed. Having learned (this), immediately, all the people of the monastery came to meet them (= the vessels) with hymns and chants as far as the road and brought them into the sanctuary, as before. May the prayer and blessing of this father of ours, renowned and just, be with our king Däwit, with our father Yamäna Krestos, and with us all, his children and his beloved ones, in the centuries of centuries, amen.
ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወምስለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ የማነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
249 Miracles of our father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just. May his prayer and his blessing be with our king Däwit, with our father Yamäna Krestos, and with us all, his children and his beloved ones, in the centuries of centuries, amen.
ወበ እመዋዕል ፡ መጽአ ፡ ፳ ብእሲ ፡ ዘፅውስ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ እምነ ፡ ብሔሩ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ወበጺሖ ፡ ተማኅፀነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አንብዕ ፡ ጽፉቅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦአባ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይትከሀለከ ፡ ፈውሰኒ ፡ ዮም ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ርቱዓ ፡ ወጸንዓ ፡ ሐኃሁ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ ረድኤቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ።
250 One (day) among the days, a man with paralyzed feet came from his land and went up to Dabra Bankol; having arrived (there), he entrusted himself to the tomb of our father Madhänina Egzi' and begged the latter with pressed tears, saying: "O abbä, surely (this) is possible for you: heal me today." Immediately he stood straight and his loins were firm by the power of the help of our father. This man who had been healed lived for many days.
ወነበረ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘሐይወ ። ወዓዲ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ፳ ሕፃን ፡ ወበከ የት ፡ እሙ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰደት ፡ እማየ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወደ የት ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ እዘኒሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ መድኃ ኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ አንሥእ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ በከመ ፡ አንሥኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለአልዓዛር ፡ ዓርኩ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ መቃብር ። ወሶበ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወልዳ ፡ በረድኤተ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጸድቅ ።
251 And again, on the day of the glorious feast of our father, a small child died; his mother wept bitter tears. After that, she brought holy water and put it in his mouth and in his ears, saying: "O my father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just, resurrect for me my son who has died, just as our Lord Christ resurrected Lazarus, his friend, from the tomb (Jn 11:1-44)." When she had said thus, quickly the small child, her son, resurrected through the help of the power of our father, renowned and just.
ወካዕበ ፡ በ እመዋዕል ፡ መጽአ ፡ ፳ ሐንካስ ፡ እምካልእ ፡ መካን ፡ ወበ ጺሖ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብሩ ፡ አስተብም'ዖ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሶ ፡ እምሕን ካሴ ፡ እግሩ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሕያወ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ርቱዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግ ዚእ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወምስለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ የማነ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
252 And again, one (day) among the days, a lame man came from another place and, having arrived at the tomb of our father, begged the latter to heal him of the lameness of his foot: he was healed and walked straight like all the people. May the prayer and blessing of this father of ours, renowned and just, Madhänina Egzi', be with our king Däwit, with our father Yamäna Krestos, and with us all, his children and his beloved ones, in the centuries of centuries, amen.
ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወረድኤተ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ የማነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ሰማዕያን ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
253 Miracle of our father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just. May his prayer, his blessing, and the help of his God be with our father Yamäna Krestos and with us all, the listeners, his children and his beloved ones, in the centuries of centuries, amen.


[p. 564] {#po.51.p564}
ወሀለዉ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘትሰመይ ፡ ዲጋ ፡ ዘምድረ ፡ ሺሬ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ ማኅበሮን ፡ ለመነኮሳይያት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ወእማንቱ ፡ ደናግለ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ይሑሩ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አመ ፡ ፻ ለወርኃ ፡ ኅዳር ፡ መንገለ ፡ ወፍሮሙ ፡ ለተገብሮ ፡ ማዕረር ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ አጺደ ፡ እክል ፡ ማዕረረ ፡ ገራህቶሙ ፡ ዘተገበሩ ፡ ይቤልዎሙ ፡ አብያጽ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ህየ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ኦአኃው ፡ ኢሑሩ ፡ ለተግባረ ፡ ማዕረር ፡ ዮም ፡ ወኢትትዓደዉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዕለተ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ዕምርት ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ አበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እመንገለ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወሀብት ፡ ወዓበይዎሙ ፡
254 There were monks in a town called Digä, in the land of Shire; it (was) the convent of a community of holy nuns and these (were) the virgins of the grace of our father Sämu'ël. When these monks wanted to go, on the day of the feast of our father Madhänina Egzi', the third of the month of Hedär, to their field to work at the harvest—the harvest of their labor at which they were working was the wheat harvest—, (their) companion monks who were there with them said to them: "O brothers, do not go to work at the harvest today and do not pass over, for this day (is) the day of the distinguished feast of our father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just, and he (is) also the father of our father Sämu'ël from the point of view of grace and gift." These monks refused them.
እሙንቱ ፡ መነካሶት ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤልዎሙ ፡ ኢትሑሩ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ አስር ሕት ፡ ዘተሠርዓ ፡ ለተገብሮ ፡ ተግባር ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ እመኒ ፡ ለአጺድ ፡ ወእ መኒ ፡ ለምክያደ ፡ እክል ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ለተግባረ ፡ እድ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ለባዕድ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ዓበይዎሙ ። ወሥልሰ ፡ ይቤልዎሙ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ንብለክሙ ፡ ኢት ግበሩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዘዮም ፡ ክብርት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በዘአዕረፈ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ እምፃማ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘፃመወ ፡ በዓለም ፡ የሐዩ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ። ወባቲ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ ሕያው ፡ በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ ጻድቃንሰ ፡ ኢሞቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ ፈለሱ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ለአግብ ርተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሞት ፡ አላ ፡ ፍልሰት ። ወዓበይዎሙ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ወኢ ተመይጡ ፡ ለፌ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ህየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕሊናሆሙ ፡ ሰይጣናዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢያእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምክር ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይገብራ ፡ ወኢለበዉ ፡ ዘኒ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እሉ ፡ አብያጺሆሙ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ መካርያነ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ጻድቃንሰ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ይመክሩ ፡ ወትነብር ፡
255 They said to them: "Do not go (there): do you not have days of labor that are instituted to do the work that is appropriate either for the harvest, or for the threshing of the wheat, or for manual labor, or for something else?" They refused them again. A third time, they said to them: "We tell you: Do not do this thing thus, in consideration of God, according to what the Scripture says: Glorious (is) the death of the just before God (cf. Ps 115[114]:6). In fact, this day of today is glorious, this father of ours, renowned and just, having rested on it from the fatigues of this world and having gone into eternal life, according to what the Scripture says: He who has labored in the world will live forever. In it (= this day) also he passed to his living creator, according to what is said: The just do not die, they pass to their God (cf. Wis 5:15). It is said again: There is no death for the servants of God, but a passage." They refused them a third time and did not turn away from where they were; on the contrary, they were there in their satanic thought, for they did not know that a counsel (is) good for whoever puts it into practice and they did not understand what was said about these monks, their companions, counselors of wisdom: The just counsel wisdom and this counsel remains forever.
ይእቲ ፡ ምክር ፡ ለዓለም ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አብያጽ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እመኒ ፡ አብዝሩ ፡ ገሥጾቶሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ምዕረ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ወሥልሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢተመይጡ ፣ እምሕሊናሆሙ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ኀደግዎሙ ፡ ይሑሩ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ እምከመ ፡ ምዕረ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ወሥልሰ ፡ ኅድጎ ፡ ለዘዓ በየ ፡ ወኢተመይጠ ፡ ዓማ ፡ እምእኩዩ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ በተሀብሎ ፡ ወበተ ዓድዎ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ወፍሮሙ ፡ ዘማዕረር ፡ጸዊሮሙ ፡ ወነሚኦሙ ፡ ምዝረ ፡ ዘይሰትዩ ፡ ህየ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጽምዖሙ ፡ ወአኅዙ ፡ ይትቀነዩ ፡ በተገ ብሮ ፡ አጺድ ፡ ማዕረረ ፡ ገራህቶሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ አኀዞሙ ፡ ጽምዕ ፡ ኃያል ፡
256 These companion monks, having seen that, even if they had warned those ones much—once, twice, a third time—, they had not turned away from their evil thought, let them go where they wanted, according to what is written that says: After you have warned him once, twice, three times, leave the unjust who has refused and has not turned away from his wickedness (Ti 3:10). After that, (the other monks) went with arrogance and much excess toward their harvest field, having carried and taken beer that they would drink there at the moment of their thirst; they began to apply themselves to the work of harvesting the harvest of their labor. When a violent thirst took them at

the cause of the hardness of the labor and the weight (of the middle) of the day, they went where they had approached the beer before, in the shade; having arrived (there), they found the jug empty, without beer, (as) if this had happened by a desire, the jug not being broken and its beer not having flowed onto the ground. They did not find any clue (of the place) where the beer had gone; simply their heart did not have a suspicion: if there had been some man who had drunk it in secret, he would have been near them, face to face, no one having hidden it from their eyes. 257 Because of this, they were astonished, marveled greatly, and were stunned by this affair: they did not know what to say and what to do regarding this affair, fear and trembling seized them extremely. After that, they placed the affliction in their souls and were saddened because of their sins. In fact, know that this sign that happened to them came from the anger of the power of our father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just, because of what they had violated and treated with arrogance the glorious feast of his rest. These monks were also very ashamed and confused by their manual labor. They became a subject of reproach for their neighbors, of laughter and mockery for their environment, and especially before their companion monks whose word they had not accepted the good warning. Shame covered their face under the word of the one who insults and outrages them (Ps 44[43]:14, 16-17), for they had forgotten and had not remembered the name of the holy fatherhood of this father of ours, renowned and just: they had raised their hands for the work of the harvest during the glorious feast of his rest. Behold then that all this happened to these monks, enemies of a good warning. From that moment was known and announced in the whole universe this miracle of the great power of this father of ours, renowned and just, Madhänina Egzi'. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the centuries of centuries, amen. Miracle of the restoration of the church
ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወረድኤተ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ የማነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ሰማዕያን ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወፍቁራኒሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
258 Miracle of our father Madhänina Egzi', renowned and just. May his prayer, his blessing, and the help of his God be with our father Yamäna Krestos and with us all, the listeners, his children and his beloved ones, in the centuries of centuries, amen.
ወአመ ፡ ተሐድሶ ፡ ሕንጻሃ ፡ ዘቅድም ፡ ለመቅደሰ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ በከመ ፡ ተሐደሰት ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ መቅደስ ፡ እምግብረ ፡ ሕንጻሃ ፡ ዘቅድም ፡ በእደ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አንደ ፊር ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ መቅደሰ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ራግዛተ ፡ አናቅጺሃ ፡ ወዓረፍታተ ፡ ሕንጻሃ ፡ እለ ፡ ተሐንጹ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በእደ ፡ አበው ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሐራነ ፡ አምጣን ፡ ወትሑታነ ፡ ዓቅም ፡ በህቁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ልማዶሙ ፡ ወሥምረተ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በኩሉ ፡
259 (It was) during the renovation of the first building of the sanctuary of the great monastery, Dabra Bankol, just as the sanctuary of Jerusalem had been renovated before from the work of the first building by the hand of King Herod, son of Antipater; in fact, the doorposts of the sanctuary of this great monastery and the walls of its building, which had been built long ago by the hand of the first holy fathers, were of reduced dimensions and of a very low elevation, for such (were) the habit and the will of heart


[p. 568] {#po.51.p568}
ግብረ ፡ ሕንጸት ፡ ዘበመዋዕሊሆሙ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ ደኃርተ ፡ ዘመን ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ሐደሱ ፡ ሕንጻሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ መቅደሶሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያነ ውጐ ፡ ወያሜንዩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምቀደምቶሙ ፡ ራግዛተ ፡ አናቅጺሃ ፡ ወአረ ፍታተ ፡ ሕንጻሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ሞገስ ፡ ለማኅደረ ፡ ስብ ሐተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘበቅሩብ ፡ ወዘበርጐቅ ፡ ይኔጽር ፡ ወያስተሐይጽ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ በአእምሮ ፡ ወበለብዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ሐኒጸ ፡ አረፋቲሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ እምአን ቀጽ ፡ ጽባሓዊ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ዓረባዊ ፡ ወእምአንቀጽ ፡ ሰሜናዊ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንቀጽ ፡ ደቡባዊ ፡ ዘ፪8ጾታ ፡ መዓዝን ፡ ዘበውስጥ ፡ ወዘበአፍአ ፡ አንሥኡ ፡ ሠርዌ ፡ ሕንጻ ፡ ቀመር ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ ወአዕረጉ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ወዓቀምዎ ፡ ዲበ፡ ሠረገላ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሐጺረ ፡ ህየ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፻ እመት ፡ እምአምጣነ ፡ ኑጉ ፡ ለቀመረ ፡ ሕንጻ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አረፍተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ይነውኅ ፡ እምአረፍተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ዘቅድም ፡ መጠነ ፡ ኮ፻ እመት ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አውረድዋ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሠርዌ ፡ ወገደፍዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እንበ ይነ ፡ ሕፀራ ፡ ወኃሠሠ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ሠርዌ ፡ ወረከቡ ፡ ዘኑ ፡ ትሩፋ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ንትጋሃ ፡ ለቀዳሚት ፡ ሠርዌ ፡ ወኪያሁ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረጉ ፡ ወዓቀምዎ ፡ ዲበ፡፣ ሠረገላ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዕቁመ ፡ ወጥንቁቀ ፡ በአምጣነ ፡ ኑጐ ፡ ለቀመር ፡ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ለጊዜሁ ።
of the first holy fathers in all the constructions that (were raised) during their days. Because of this fact, the community of monks of a later time renovated the building of this sanctuary of theirs, by enlarging and embellishing—much more than their predecessors—its doorposts and the walls of its structure so that it would be of great grace for the dwelling of the glory of the house of God: he who (was) near and he who (was) far would look at it and observe with the knowledge and intelligence of God—glory to him! 260 After they had finished building the walls of this sanctuary from the eastern door to the western door and from the southern door to the northern door—which (are) the four kinds of cardinal points—, inside and outside, they lifted the old beam of the structure of the vault; they raised it and placed it on the framework (?) of the sanctuary. There, it was too short by three cubits in relation to the size of the vault of the new building; in fact, the wall of the new sanctuary was longer by three cubits than the wall of the old sanctuary. Because of this, they brought down that beam from there and abandoned it on the ground because of its small size. They looked for another beam and found (one) whose length (was) perfect in relation to the lack of the first beam. They also raised it and placed it on the framework (?) of the sanctuary. It was adapted and adjusted for them according to the size of the vault, they rejoiced over it at the moment.
ወእምዝ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ረከቦሙ ፡ ኀዘነ ፡ ልብ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተረከበ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ተምያን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመስል ፡ ዳኅነ ፡ እንተ ፡ አፍአሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መስወ ፡ እንተ ፡ ውሥጡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ርኅወተ ፡ ከበሮ ። ወተዓውቀ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ ድምጹ ፡ እስመ ፡ አስምዓ ፡ ድምጸ ፡ ከመ ፡ ድምጸ ፡ ንፍሐት ፡ ዘቀርነ ፡ ገንታ ፡ እምንፍሐተ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ ረቂቅ ፡ ዘቦአ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ እመንገለ ፡ ንቅዓት ፡ ቀጢን ፡ ወእንበይነዝ ፡ አውረዱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኪያሁኒ ፡ ወገ ደፍዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ኀዘኑ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ኀዘነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወተሀውኩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢሀለወ ፡ ህየ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ለጊዜሁ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ሠርዌ ፡ ቀመር ፡ ድልው ። ወዓዲ ፡ ያስተሐምሙ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብ ሐት ፡ ወያብዕሉ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ትንሣኤሁ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ ጊዜሁ ፡
261 After that, an affliction of heart touched them again, for there was a defect in it (= the beam): while it seemed sound from the outside, it was in fact hollow (?) on the inside like the cavity of a drum. This was recognized by the sign (given by) its sound; in fact, it made a sound like the sound of the breath of a small trumpet (due) to a slight breath of wind that had entered it through a small opening; because of this, they also brought it down and abandoned it on the ground like the first one. After that, they were afflicted with a great affliction because of this affair and were greatly disturbed, for there was no beam wood for a vault suitable there nearby, for the moment. Moreover, they were then putting all their care into doing (the week of) the passion of Christ—glory to him!—there (= in the church) and into celebrating the feast of his holy resurrection, for the time of that (was) near.
ለዝንቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ መስየ ፡ አተዉ ፡ ኅዙናነ ፡ ወትኩዛነ ፡ ልብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲ ሆሙ ፡ ወቤቱ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእሉ ፡ ወያስተበቀዑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀዘን ፡ ዘረከቦሙ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ትገይስ ፡ መንፈስየ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ትእዛዝከ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፳፳ አም ላኪየ ፡ ጸራሕኩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ዕለትየ ፡ ወኢሰማዕከኒ ፡ ወሌሊትየኒ ፡ ቅድሜከ ፡ ወኢሐለይከኒ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቅዱሰኒከ ፡ ተኃድረ ፡ ስቡሐ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወዓዲ ፡ ሰአሉ ፡ ወአስተብሩዑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኀኅቢሮሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡
262 When evening came, they returned afflicted and saddened in heart to their houses and spent the whole night praying and begging the Lord their God regarding the affliction that had reached them; each of them said at night: "In the morning, my spirit is with you, O Lord my God, for your commandment (is) light on earth." Each also said: "My God, I have cried to you during the day and you have not turned me away; at night, (I have been) before you and you have not thought of me. You dwell in your saints, glorified of Israel (Ps 22[21]:3-4)." They also prayed and begged all together their

ክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ኦአ ቡነ ፡ ወመምህርነ ፡ ዘበጽድቅ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ተሐየይከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕንጻ ፡ መቅደ ስከ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ምዕራፈ ፡ ሥጋከ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ጸዋርያነ ፡ ፃማ ፡ ወኅዙናን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕንጻ ፡ መቅደስከ ፡ ኢትስእልኑ ፡ ወኢ ታስተበቀነዕ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰርሕ ፡ ለነ ፡ ተግባረ ፡ እደዊነ ።
renowned and righteous father, our father Madhänina Egzi', saying: "O our true father and master, why have you neglected the construction of your sanctuary, which is the resting place of your glorious body, while we, your children, are burdened and afflicted because of the construction of your sanctuary? Will you not pray and beseech the Lord your God for us, so that He may assist the work of our hands for us?"
ወቤቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወዘይመስሎ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ወበጊዜ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ረከ ብዋ ፡ ለእንታክቲ ፡ ሠርዌ ፡ ቀመር ፡ ዘገደፍዋ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕሀራ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ነዋኃ ፡ ወወሰከት ፡ በዲበ ፡ ቆማ ፡ ፻ እመተ ፡ በአምጣነ ፡ ንት ጋሃ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ወተሐሥዩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ አንከሩሂ ፡ ወተደሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተአምር ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ ከሃሌ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወበአስተብቀዖቱ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ለክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አቡሆሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኃቢሮሙ ፡ በ፳ ቃል ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወዓቢይ ፡ ኃይሉ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ጉልቀ፦ ፡ ለጥበቢሁ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወብዙኅ ፡ አኩቴቱ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ በደብረ ፡ መቅደሱ ። ዘይኤዝዝ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ምድር ። ወቦዘይቤ ፡ እምኔ ሆሙ ፡ ለከ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ በጽዮን ። ወለከ ፡ ይትፌኖ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ በኢየሩሰሌም ። ወቦዘይቤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጽርሕከ ፡ ወመንክር ፡ በጽድቅ ። ወቦእለ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ለቤትከ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ለነዋኅ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኃቢሮሙ ፡ በ፳ቃል ፡ መንገለ ፡ ደብሮሙ ፡ ነኪር ፡ ነገሩ ፡ በእንቲአኪ ፡ ኦሀገረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አዕረግዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሠርዌ ፡ ወዓቀምዋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሠረ ገላ ፡ መቅድስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አምጣኒሃ ፡ ዕቁመ ፡ ወጥንቁቀ ፡ በአምጣነ ፡ ኑ ፡ ለቀመረ ፡ ሕንጻ ፡ ሐዲስ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ መልአ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ አፉሆሙ ፡ ወተሐስየ ፡ ልሳኖሙ ፡ ወአእኩቱ ፡ ፈጣሪሆሙ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መነኮሳት ። ናሁኬ ፡ ተመሰለት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሠርዌ ፡ ቀመር ፡ በእንታክቲ ፡ እብን ፡ እንተ ፡ መነንዋ ፡ ነደቅት ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ማዕዘንት ፡ ከማሃ ፡ ለእንታክቲ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ መነንዋ ፡ ሐናጽያን ፡ ወበአማን ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዛቲ ፡ በከመ ፡ እንታክቲ ፡ ወበጽድቅ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ነካር ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ለዓይነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡
263 They spent the whole night until morning saying things like this and (words) similar to them. At the time of morning, when they went out (and entered) the church, they found the vault beam that they had abandoned earlier because of its small size where it was; (it was) longer and had added to its dimension three cubits, in proportion to its previous lack. Seeing (this), they rejoiced and exulted greatly; they also marveled and were very astonished because of the miracle that had occurred for them by the power of their omnipotent God and by the supplication, to Him, of our renowned and righteous father Madhänina Egzi', their father. 264 After that, they all said together with one voice: "Great (is) God and great His power, there is no measure to His wisdom (Ps 147[146],5)!" They said again, "Great (is) God and abundant His praise in the city of our God, on the mountain of His sanctuary, He who prescribes joy to all the earth (Ps 48[47],2-3)!" There was one among them who said: "To You, O Lord, glory is fitting in Zion; to You is prayer addressed in Jerusalem (Ps 65[64],2)." There was one who said: "Holy (is) Your palace, and admirable in truth!" There were those who said: "To Your house glory is fitting, O Lord, for long days (Ps 93[92],5)." They all said again together with one voice toward their monastery: "Admirable (is) the word about you, O city of God (Ps 87[86],3)!" 265 After that, they raised this beam and placed it on the framework (9) of the sanctuary as before, and its measure was adapted and adjusted to the measure of the length of the vault of the new construction. Immediately joy filled their mouths, their tongues exulted, and they gave thanks to their creator for it (= the church), as the community of monks did formerly. Behold, this vault beam has been like that stone which the builders despised: this one has (gone) to the head of the corner (Ps 118[117],22), like that one after the builders had despised it. In truth, this one comes from God like that one; truly this one is admirable to the eye of the whole universe, in the likeness of that stone.
ዓለም ፡ አምሳለ ፡ እንታክቲ ፡ እብን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ሕንጾ ፡ መቅደሶሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ወእምድኅረኒ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ሥነ ፡ ሕንጻሃ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኃቢሮሙ ፡ በ፳ቃል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ መቅደሶሙ ፡ ቀደሰ ፡ ማኅደሮ ፡ ልዑል ፡ እግዚእብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማእ ከላ ፡ ኢትትሀወክ ፡ ወይረድኣ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍጹመ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡
266 After that, they finished with it the entire construction of their sanctuary; and after having finished its beautiful construction, they all said together with one voice concerning this holy sanctuary of theirs: "The Most High has sanctified His dwelling, God (is) in the midst of it: it is not shaken and God

assists it totally (Ps 46[45],5-6)." They also said: "Its foundations (are) on the holy mountains! God prefers the gates of Zion to all the tents of Jacob. Behold Philistia, Tyre, and the people of Ethiopia who are born there. 'Our mother Zion', they call her, for a great miracle has been done there and He, the Most High, has founded her (Ps 87[86],1-2.4-5)." They said again concerning this house: "The Father founded it first, the Son built it, and the Holy Spirit finished it." After that, the report of the matter concerning the greatness of the miracle and prodigious sign of this renowned and righteous father of ours, our father Madhänina Egzi', which he did by the power of his omnipotent God, spread throughout the universe until today. May his prayer and his blessing be with our father Yamäna Krestos and with us all, the listeners, his children and his beloved ones, for ever and ever, amen.

### Colophon
ዝንቱ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ ኃይል ፡ ዘክቡረ ፡ ስም ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መድኃ ኒነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ተጽሕፈ ፡ በሥምረተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክነ ፡ በ፳፻ወ ፪፻ወ፲ ዓመት ፡ እምፍጥረተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወእም ፲ ወ፻ አቅማረ ፡ ዓመታት ፡ በ፪፻3ወ፪ተረፈ ፡ ቀመር ፡ ወእመዓመተ ፡ ሥጋዌ ፡ በ፲፻ወ ፪፻ወ፲ ዓመት ፡ ወእምዓመተ ፡ ወንጌላውያን ፡ መጋቤ ፡ ዘመን ፡ መርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አበቅቴ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ፳ ወ ቿ ወአበቅቴ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ፳ በምግበ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወበሚመተ ፡ አቡነ ፡ የማነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ በንኮል ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወመ ስፍነ ፡ ትግሬ ፡ ደጅ ፡ አዝማች ፡ ሚካኤል ። ደራሲሁኒ ፡ ወጸሓፊሁ ፡ አባ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ መድኅን ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ጐንድጐንዴ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ደብተራ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ንግሥት ፡ መቅደሰ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ንጉሥ ።
267 These miracles of the power of our renowned and righteous father, Madhänina Egzi', were written with the approval of the Lord our God in the 7210th year since the creation of the world, during the 13th lunar cycle, the 294th of the remainder of the lunar cycle, the 1710th year of the era of the Incarnation, (the year of) Mark the Evangelist of the era of the Evangelists governing time, while the epact of the moon (was) 28 and the epact of the sun 20, under the reign of our king Däwit and during the mandate of our father Yamäna Krestos of Dabra Bankol the holy, the governor of Tigré (being) the dadjazmätch Mikä'êl. The one who composed and wrote them (is) abbä Tawalda Madhen of Dabra Guendeguendë the holy, while he was dabtara at Dabra Berhän the royal, sanctuary of King Iyäsu.

268 To the one who wrote them we have conceded and given, we the members of the community of Dabra Bankol, five fields that are in the land of Tälë d'Acharwo - our superior (being) abbä Re'esa Häymänot, under the reign of our king Iyäsu -, to our son Tawalda Madhen so that they might be for his sons and the sons of his sons, this because forty-four measures of grain (coming) from his property remain with us (?). Now, we have instituted that no official shall enter his place except for the administrator (of the monastery). If there is one who transgresses this word of ours, let him be cursed by the mouth of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit and by the mouth of Peter and Paul; whoever would erase this curse, let him be like Arius the cursed and like Judas the impious, amen.
